# Choregos — critical review and implementation plan (2026-10-27)

> Written for an orchestrating Claude Code session that will fan this plan out to parallel
> worker agents (Claude Sonnet), one story per agent, one PR per story. English on purpose
> (ADR 0046): the plan is committed as `docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md` by its first story.

## 0. Context

The owner asked for three things on 2026-10-27:

1. **A critical analysis** of Choregos: design gaps, functional gaps, technical implementation
   weaknesses — and a plan that fixes them.
2. **The project fully in English**: code, identifiers, file and directory names, tests, docs,
   plan, READMEs, charts and policy identifiers — with compatibility shims where something running
   or external depends on an old French name; the French archives (`docs/fr/`, the 43 French
   `# Moved` ADR stubs, the 7 French `docs/plan` pointer files) are **deleted**.
3. **The ability to use an MCP Gateway** — which is **another product**. Choregos must never
   become an MCP gateway (ADR 0022, 0028, 0029, 0065 already say so) and must meet it only through
   public interfaces, exactly as ADR 0079 treats graal.

State of the repository at `b3a7893` (version 0.20.5): ~250 k lines of Python (apps/api 101 k,
apps/orchestrator 33 k, packages/adapters 29 k, core 18 k, runner 15 k), 41 k lines of TypeScript
(apps/web), 87 ADRs, 302 backlog stories; STATUS counts 395 delivered, **229 partial**, 5 not
started. French debt measured today: **~10,900 French prose findings in 638 files**, ~1,850
Python functions/classes with French names, ~400 French-named paths.

## E. Execution in this session (owner's decisions of 2026-10-28)

The owner asked to open a PR with this plan, then implement it. Decisions: **I merge each story
PR myself (squash) once its whole CI is green**; commits carry
`Signed-off-by: Vincent Devillers <vincent.devillers@layer4.fr>` (plus the Co-Authored-By and
Claude-Session trailers); **all waves**, stopping only at the release points, which stay the owner's.

**Phase A — done: the plan is recalibrated on `main` 3156d12.** `main` moved after this plan was
first written (2026-10-27, `b3a7893`): the S43 stream "agents ship as plugins"
(`PLAN-2026-10-27-servicenow.md`, ADRs 0093 and 0096) **merged** at `3156d12`; S44 holds kernel
confinement (ADR 0094), SkillSpector (ADR 0095) and a code-graph trial; ADRs 0090–0092 landed and
0088/0089 are held by open PRs (#987, #988). Hence:

1. **Renumbering.** This plan's streams are **S45** (English everywhere, finished — ADR **0098**),
   **S46** (consuming an external MCP gateway — ADR **0097**) and **S47** (hardening — ADRs
   **0099–0104**). Before each ADR-creating story, check `docs/adr/` on `main` and take the next free
   number if another session took it. Main's own S43-0x/S44-0x stories keep their ids; when a story
   text means them, it says "main's" or "plugin-plan".
2. **Every story was re-verified against 3156d12** (paths, symbols, line anchors, what main already
   did, new importers and call sites): 94 got amendments, 14 were unchanged, none is obsolete. Each
   amended story carries a **"Recalibrated on main 3156d12"** list that overrides its text. Line
   anchors into hot files that grew (openapi.yaml +464, models.py +118, deployment.md +108,
   usage.md +86, webhooks.py +156, adapters `__init__.py` +44) are given as new numbers or as
   symbols: anchor by symbol when in doubt.
3. **Reconciled with ADR 0093/0096** (owner's rules): (a) the gateway ADR's boundary allows the
   platform-run **plugin servers** of ADR 0096 — Choregos still never routes, aggregates or re-serves
   third-party MCP servers; (b) `required` gateway mode exempts organisation connectors that are
   plugin servers (`org_connectors.plugin_server` set); (c) a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is
   an **extension** — `choregos_api.greffons` becomes `choregos_api.extensions`, never `plugins`.
   `services/plugin_servers.connector_client` is now the single builder of organisation connector
   clients (broker, `connector.call`, discovery): S46-09 composes with it.
4. No plugin-stack gating is needed any more (the stack merged). Other open PRs (#982–#993, S39/S38,
   #1035) may still land; workers rebase on the latest `main` before pushing.

**Phase B — the plan PR.** Story S45-00 (plan file, streams and story entries in
`08-backlog.yaml`, PR template sections STATUS row / Release note / Rename table,
`tests/docs/test_plan_stories_in_backlog.py`) on `claude/cool-bell-szzj37`; run `make ci`
locally; open the PR; merge when green.

**Phase C — waves.** For each wave of §4:

1. One worker per story (`Agent`, `isolation: "worktree"`, `model: "sonnet"`, at most 2 at a time
   on this 4-CPU container), prompt of §1.3 with the story block, branch
   `s<stream>/<story-id>-<slug>` (e.g. `s46/S46-03-mcp-client-2026-07-28`) from the latest `main`.
   The worker runs the story's commands, the lint/typecheck of its packages and the tests it touches;
   GitHub CI is the authority for the full suite.
2. The worker pushes and opens its PR (template filled). I subscribe to the PR's activity, fix red
   CI and review findings on the branch (never skip a test), and **squash-merge when `ci-ok` and
   every required check are green**, contract-change PRs first and renames last within a wave.
3. After the wave: the integrator PR `docs(plan): STATUS for wave N` (STATUS rows with the new ids,
   backlog statuses, regenerated English baselines, `alembic heads` single head), merged when green;
   then re-anchor the next wave's stories with the merged rename tables.
4. Stop and hand over to the owner at **0.21.0** (after wave 14: tag and release are the owner's;
   S45-39 waits until 0.21.0 is deployed) and **0.22.0** (after waves 25–26; then S45-49), or on
   anything outside a story's scope that needs a product decision.

**Verification.** Per PR: GitHub CI green (python, web, charts, contracts, english, dco,
secrets…). Per wave: the integrator checks of §1.2 step 6. Milestones: §6.

## 1. How to execute this plan (read first)

### 1.1 Roles

- **Integrator** = the session that receives this plan. It never implements a story itself. It
  (a) runs story `S45-00` (commits this plan, registers streams), (b) launches each wave, (c)
  reviews and merges PRs, (d) is the **only** writer of `docs/plan/STATUS.md`,
  `docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml` and `docs/plan/BLOCKERS.md`, (e) cuts the releases that let
  one-release aliases be removed.
- **Workers** = one Claude Sonnet sub-agent per story, each in its own git worktree
  (`Agent` tool, `isolation: "worktree"`, `model: "sonnet"`), branch `s<stream>/<story-id>-<slug>`
  (e.g. `s46/S46-03-org-mcp-gateway-api`) cut from the latest `main`.

### 1.2 Wave loop (integrator)

1. Take the next wave from §4. Every story of a wave is file-disjoint from the others and all its
   `depends_on` are merged. Wave 0 is special: merge S45-00, S45-01, S46-01, S46-18, S47-06,
   S47-01 **one at a time in that order**, each rebased and green, before cutting wave-1 branches.
2. **Re-anchor before launching** (waves ≥ 9): a story written against a French name that an
   earlier S45 story renamed must be given the new name. Each merged S45 story leaves its rename
   table in `docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/<story-id>.md`; apply it to the story block you hand out
   (the stories flagged "Re-anchor" in their amendments are the known cases).
3. Launch one worker per story **in a single message** (parallel), with the worker prompt below.
4. Merge order inside a wave: **contract-change PRs first**, then features and hardening,
   **renames last** (so a rename sweeps code that already landed). For each PR: check the
   acceptance boxes, run `make ci` (and `make web-ci` when `apps/web` changed; `make charts-lint`
   / `make policies-test` / the replay suite when the story says so) on the PR head,
   squash-merge, rebase the others, **regenerate generated files instead of hand-merging them**
   (`make contracts`, `make docs-cli`, `apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json`,
   `apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json`), re-run CI.
5. Reject a PR that touches a file outside its story's file list (the obvious mechanical
   follow-ups — importers of a renamed name, generated files — excepted and named in the PR).
6. After the wave, one integrator commit: (a) a `docs/plan/STATUS.md` row per merged story,
   copied from its PR's `STATUS row` (nothing ✅ without a test that fails in its absence);
   (b) backlog statuses; (c) `tools/english_baseline/*.tsv` regenerated with
   `uv run python tools/check_english.py --all --update-baseline` after checking no area count
   rose; (d) `alembic heads` shows one head (after waves 4, 8, 12, 15); (e) the replay suite green
   when `workflows/` or `activities/` changed.
7. Never start a wave while a contract-change PR of the previous wave is unmerged.

### 1.3 Worker prompt (copy verbatim, then paste the story block from §5)

```text
You implement exactly one story of docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md in this worktree.
1. Read AGENTS.md (it is CLAUDE.md), then the story block below. Read every file it names
   before editing. If a path or symbol named in the story does not exist, search for its
   current name; if the story is wrong, stop and report instead of improvising.
2. Do only what the story says. Out-of-scope problem: do not fix it; describe it in the PR body
   under "Findings filed" (the integrator files the issue).
3. Never edit docs/plan/STATUS.md, docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml or docs/plan/BLOCKERS.md. Write the
   STATUS row text (what the PR proves AND what it does not prove) in the PR body under
   "STATUS row".
4. Every new or modified line is English (tools/check_english.py). A line that must stay French
   (a frozen Temporal patch id, a kept alias) carries `# lang: fr-ok`.
5. Add the tests the story lists; each must fail without your change (check it by reverting
   the production change locally once).
6. Run the story's commands, then `make ci` (and `make web-ci` if apps/web changed). Fix until
   green. Do not skip, xfail or delete a test to get green.
7. Never commit tools/english_baseline/*.tsv (the integrator regenerates them; S45-01 and
   S45-47 excepted). An S45 story writes its rename table (old -> new modules, symbols, paths,
   env vars) to docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/<story-id>.md and in the PR body.
8. Commit with the story's PR title as a conventional commit, push the branch, open the PR with
   .github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md filled (story id, change, acceptance boxes ticked, proof =
   commands and their results, contracts box, STATUS row, release note, rename table,
   findings).
```

Hand a worker its block with: `awk '/^### S46-08 /{p=1;print;next} /^##/{p=0} p'
docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md` (replace the id). Give it the block, not the whole plan.

### 1.4 Rules that apply to every story

- `make ci` green = lint (ruff on `packages apps/api apps/orchestrator tools tests`, ruff format,
  `tools/check_english.py`) + mypy strict + pytest + contracts-check + charts-lint.
- Contracts (`packages/contracts`) change only in stories flagged `contract_change: true`
  (ADR 0001); they run `make contracts` and commit the regenerated `packages/contracts/ts/`.
  A consumer story never edits the contract.
- Temporal: `workflow.patched` ids are frozen forever; activity/signal wire names keep the old
  name registered as an alias; no PR on `apps/orchestrator/.../workflows/` without the replay
  suite (`tests/replay/`) green and, for a behaviour change, a new recorded history
  (`make replay-record`).
- Fakes are mandatory (ADR 0005): every new integration ships its scriptable `Fake*`, selected
  by `CHOREGOS_FAKES=1`.
- Hot files (one writer per wave; §4 is built so): `docs/plan/*`, `AGENTS.md`, `Makefile`,
  `pyproject.toml`, `compose.yaml`, `.github/workflows/*.yml`, `packages/contracts/**`,
  `tools/check_english.py`, `tools/english_baseline/*.tsv` (integrator only),
  `docs/{usage,deployment,security,development}.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`,
  `apps/api/src/choregos_api/{main.py,config.py,db/models.py}`,
  `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py`, `apps/web/src/lib/{api.ts,types.ts}`,
  `apps/web/src/mocks/data.ts`, `charts/choregos/values.yaml`,
  `apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py`, `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py`.
- Every new or modified line is translated: `tools/check_english.py` reads a renamed or moved line
  as added, so a rename also translates the prose on the lines it touches.
- Stories written against today's French names keep them; S45 renames come after (§4). Old
  names keep working through aliases (`alias:internal` until S45-40/S45-46, `alias:external` until
  0.22.0, removed by S45-49).
- The implementing environment needs network access for `uv sync` / `pnpm install`; Helm ≥ 3.18
  with helm-unittest 1.1.2 for `make charts-test`, Kyverno CLI 1.19.1 for `make policies-test`.

## 2. Critical analysis

Evidence was gathered by reading the code at `b3a7893`, then re-checked by adversarial
verifiers (verdict column: ✔ confirmed, ◐ partly, ✘ refuted). §2.8 maps every finding to the
stories that fix it.

### 2.1 What is solid (keep it)

- **Governance is a mechanism, not a prompt**: gates, per-run virtual keys with hard caps,
  governed actions with an outbox (ADR 0085), a broker that holds every org MCP call (policy
  `forbidden` by default, schema drift closes a tool, input/output schema checks, injection guard,
  cost ledger, audit digests, spans).
- **Engineering discipline**: strict mypy, 4,400 Python tests + 1,100 web tests, contracts first
  with generated types, fakes for every integration, replay histories (68), RLS fail-closed,
  SBOM + provenance + Trivy on release, an English guard on every added line.
- **Honest tracking**: STATUS says what each PR does *not* prove.

### 2.2 Design and architecture

| # | Finding (evidence) | Verdict | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| ARC-1 | **Declared governance that nothing enforces** — the product's worst kind of false promise: `ReviewSpec.humans` has no reader and no warning, yet the shipped template `dsl/templates/advanced.yaml:94` writes it (contradicting ADR 0044 §4); transition `review.agents` is never scheduled and even suppresses the `actor.unused` warning (`validator.py:721-724`); `approvals.*`, `attempts.default_max`, `review.require_human_for_risk` produce warnings that only reach the server log (`services/definitions.py:379`); a project's `agent.allowed_backends` and the deployment's `CHOREGOS_ALLOWED_AGENT_BACKENDS` are never checked at stage start (`stage.py:209`, only `_require_backend_enabled`), contradicting `docs/usage.md:1233`. | ✔ | **high** |
| ARC-2 | **The YAML tool catalogue (ADR 0014) bypasses the broker's governance**: in `routers/internal.py:922-931` a catalogue tool (HTTP POST or MCP) is called directly — no governed action, no approval — while organisation-connector writes go through `proposer`/governed actions. | ◐ | high |
| ARC-3 | **The orchestrator is welded to the API package**: `apps/orchestrator/pyproject.toml` depends on `choregos-api`; 149 imports in 29 files (db.models 34, services 17, db.session 11); direct row writes (`gates.py:274`, `run_key.py:60/128`, `tracker.py:62/238`, `stage.py:396`, `actions.py:112`…). Row builders diverge: `HumanRequest` built in 3 places, only one sets `due_at` (agent questions and scope requests have no SLA date). | ◐ | med |
| ARC-4 | API layering: routers hold 459 SQL calls (agents.py 88); the organisation lookup is copied 12 times; 7 routers import private `_helpers` of other routers; `services/readiness.py:740` imports a router (`KNOWN_BACKENDS`, a duplicate of core's `KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES`). | ✔ | low–med |
| ARC-5 | `packages/ontology/service` (an API plugin) reaches past the published plugin seam into ORM models, private services and the RLS session helper. | ◐ | low |
| ARC-6 | Cross-backend review: selection (`stage.py:1286`) and measurement (`costs.py:386`) define "implementer" and "cross review" differently, so the published metric counts verify runs as "same" and fixed/remote reviewers as "other". | ◐ | low |
| ARC-7 | `PATCH /projects/{id}` and `PUT /projects/{id}/models` replace the whole `config` JSON with no ETag/If-Match, no row lock, and an audit entry without before/after: lost updates cannot even be reconstructed. | ✔ | med |
| ARC-8 | ADR 0034 §1 says a project holds several named connectors; the code has one project connector per kind (`models.py:99`) and named multiples at org level — the ADR is stale. | ◐ | low |
| ARC-9 | Choregos is not an MCP gateway (ADR 0022/0028/0029/0065) and cannot use one; stdio and OAuth catalogue servers are uninstallable; MCP 2026-07-28 (ADR 0017) is unimplemented and the client adopts any server-announced version. | ✔ | med (owner's ask) |

### 2.3 Functional gaps

| # | Finding (evidence) | Verdict | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| FUN-1 | **Tracker commands are dead letters** (#138): `/choregos approve|reject|answer|scope|pause|resume|stop` become `HUMAN_DECISION` events that reach the interpreter's inbox and are silently dropped by `_absorb` (`interpreter.py:994-1007`), growing the workflow history; no tracker-login → member mapping exists. README principle 1 ("the tracker is the human interface") is not true for decisions. | ✔ | med–high |
| FUN-2 | **Webhook events lost** when the work item's interpreter has ended: `deliver_inbound` (`temporal.py:419-422`) does not translate `NOT_FOUND`, the webhook returns 500, the dedupe row rolls back and later events of the same delivery (incl. train boarding on `PR_MERGED`) are skipped. | ✔ (new) | med |
| FUN-3 | GitHub Projects v2 card moves likely never route real deliveries (keyed on `content_node_id` and a `repository` field org-level webhooks do not carry); when they do, any state with a matching status is accepted without graph/identity/RBAC check. Only a forged-payload e2e covers it. | ◐ (new) | med |
| FUN-4 | Ecosystem asymmetry: trackers = GitHub, GitLab, Jira, internal; but SCM = GitHub only, CI = Tekton only, CD = Argo CD only, notify = Slack only (**no email**). A GitLab or Jira project still needs GitHub for code. | ✔ | med |
| FUN-5 | API features with no client: memory A/B report, cross-backend metric, gateway keys, memory re-import (secrets rewrap has a CLI command). | ◐ | low–med |
| FUN-6 | `activities/scm.py:65-67` reads `payload["reviewers"]` that no caller sets: PR reviewer requests are never sent. | ✔ (new) | low |
| FUN-7 | Declared-partial product areas (from STATUS): A2A on-behalf-of (#756), hosted agents in a cluster (gVisor, buildkitd, keyless verification), the data-platform stream S14 (not started), investment research "not production-ready", remote/code agents. Out of this plan's scope except where a story above touches them. | from STATUS | — |

### 2.4 Technical implementation

| # | Finding (evidence) | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|
| TEC-1 | Oversized modules: `routers/agents.py` 1,844 lines, `workflows/interpreter.py` 1,650 (was 675 on 2026-09-24; the planned split never happened), `db/models.py` 1,560 (57 ORM classes), `routers/knowledge.py` 1,493, `activities/stage.py` 1,344, `adapters/__init__.py` 1,288 (the whole registry); 12 functions ≥ 150 lines (`render_project_manifests` 320, `prepare_stage` 292, `_register_builtins` 271). They are merge hot spots for parallel agents. | med |
| TEC-2 | 766 function-local imports (PLC0415 ignored globally), mostly to dodge import cycles; 114 `except Exception`; 2,874 `Any`. | low |
| TEC-3 | Web: `src/mocks/data.ts` 3,464 lines (was 464), one 966-line API client, JS bundle at 1,586 of 1,600 kB budget (S31-06). | low |
| TEC-4 | French identifiers in ~1,850 Python functions/classes and ~1,000 TS declarations; French module names (`courtier`, `greffons`, `effets`, `limiteur`, `amorcage`, `parcours`…) — see §3.2. | med (owner's ask) |

### 2.5 Tests and CI

| # | Finding (evidence) | Verdict | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| TST-1 | **The orchestrator suite never runs on PostgreSQL in CI.** `apps/orchestrator/tests/conftest.py:30` reads `CHOREGOS_TEST_ORCH_ON_POSTGRES`; nothing sets it. So the PG branch of the budget reservation lock (`services/budgets.py:63-67`, `pg_advisory_xact_lock`) never runs in CI, and even with the flag the mode uses `create_all()` as superuser: no migrations, RLS bypassed — while the orchestrator writes straight through the API's models. | ✔ | med–high |
| TST-2 | **4 of 13 workflows have no replay history**: ProjectProvisioning, FindingsTriage, MemoryIngestion, AgentTrial; nothing checks that every workflow in `ALL_WORKFLOWS` has one. | ✔ | med |
| TST-3 | **Nightly live tests skip green**: `tests/live/conftest.py:22-23` skips when variables are missing, `nightly.yml:130-131` has no skip guard (unlike `ci.yml`), and the job does not even export the secrets most live tests need (ACA, Ecphoria, Rovo, realtime, OTel). The conformance report step is `continue-on-error: true` (`nightly.yml:83`). | ✔ | med |
| TST-4 | Coverage: `make ci` runs pytest without `--cov`, so the 80 % floor is enforced only in GitHub CI; `[tool.coverage.run].source` omits cli, ontology, playbooks, quant, tools-mcp (~8.3 k lines); stale comments (72 %, "core, runner, orchestrator"); the web has no coverage floor at all. | ◐ | low |
| TST-5 | 157 of 229 partial STATUS rows say "does not prove"; 35 are proven against mocks only, 29 never ran on PostgreSQL, 25 never against the real API; the 193 PR Playwright tests run in mock mode (2 tests against the real API). | from STATUS | med |
| TST-6 | Agent evals check instructions, not behaviour (S33-06). | from STATUS | med |

### 2.6 Security and operations

| # | Finding (evidence) | Verdict | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| SEC-1 | **OIDC account takeover by email.** `routers/auth.py:239-249` `_ensure_user` looks users up by `User.email`, ignores `email_verified` and a differing stored `oidc_sub` (the MCP door does it right: `mcp/appelant.py:134-139`). An IdP that lets a user set an unverified email signs them in as the victim. An IdP-side email change also inserts a duplicate `oidc_sub` → 500 on every login. | ✔ (new) | **high** |
| SEC-2 | **IdP group removal never revokes a membership.** `routers/auth.py:290-292` returns early when no group maps, and only upserts roles; `Membership` has no provenance column. Offboarding also misses API tokens (90 days default, up to 3650 or none, `schemas/identite.py:45`) and sessions (8 h, no server-side revocation). | ✔ | **high** |
| SEC-3 | **Session secret default accepted.** `config.py:80` `"dev-session-secret-change-me"`; no validator checks it, nor emptiness (an existing chart Secret without the key renders `""`, `secrets-embarques.yaml:50`). A known/empty key forges any session and the GitHub install state. | ✔ | med |
| SEC-4 | **Rate limiter**: in-memory per replica, keyed on the client-controlled left-most `X-Forwarded-For` (`limiteur.py:49-52`) → bypass, and exhaustion of a third party's budget (spoofed GitHub hook IPs → 429 on real deliveries). Its sweep is O(n) per request beyond 10 000 keys: 30 000 forged keys = 48 s CPU (measured). | ✔ | med–high |
| SEC-5 | **No CSRF defence on cookie-authenticated mutations** (SameSite=Lax only); 49 body-less mutating routes (skill approval, train departure, project suspension, secrets rewrap) are reachable by a same-site form. The chart never sets `CHOREGOS_CORS_ORIGINS`, so every Helm API allows credentialed CORS from `http://localhost:3000`; `*` is not refused. | ◐ | med |
| SEC-6 | **OIDC hygiene**: discovery failure caches a Keycloak-shaped fallback for the process (`auth.py:119-124`); no `issuer` check on the discovery document; userinfo `sub` never compared to the ID token `sub` (OIDC Core §5.3.2 MUST). | ◐ | med |
| SEC-7 | Signed values share one key with no purpose binding (session cookie, OIDC state/handshake, remote-OAuth state/cookie); no rotation list. (128-bit HMAC tag itself is fine.) | ◐ | low |
| SEC-8 | `X-Request-Id` taken verbatim (`main.py:289`), logged and reflected (log volume, correlation spoofing). | ✔ | low |
| SEC-9 | In `env=dev` (the chart's base default, `values.yaml:20`) without a webhook secret, `/webhooks/github` accepts unsigned `installation` events that claim installations (`webhooks.py:209-213`). | ✔ (new) | med (dev tenants exposed publicly) |
| SEC-10 | Secret KEKs only in `CHOREGOS_SECRETS_KEKS` env (API **and** orchestrator pods); no KMS/Vault-transit wrapper (a plugin seam exists). | ✔ | low (defence in depth) |
| SEC-11 | Backup not watched continuously; S3 WAL key shared in clear text in pod specs (BLOCKERS, infra#754); 1 of 16 runbooks played; HA (3-node Temporal, 3-instance CNPG) never run. | from STATUS/BLOCKERS | high (operator side, out of this repo) |

### 2.7 Documentation and language

| # | Finding (evidence) | Verdict | Severity |
|:--|:--|:--|:--|
| DOC-1 | **STATUS.md is unreliable as a source of truth**: stale rows S1-08 (68 replay histories exist), S0-08 (SBOM + release Trivy + blocking nightly scan exist), S7-07 (OTel traces exist), S6-05 (Jira/GitLab webhooks are live), S7-10 (16 runbooks, not 9), "465 tests, 84 %" (4,400 tests); BLOCKERS lists S27 PRs merged on 2026-10-14/15. `tests/docs/test_comptes_de_status.py:17` checks only the total and **locks it in French**. | ✔ | med |
| DOC-2 | French where ADR 0046 says English: README.md (46/98 prose lines), `docs/plan/00-index.md` (48/74), STATUS.md (1,132/1,709), BLOCKERS.md, `PRODUCT-REVIEW-2026-10-07.md` (English name, French content), `05-release-train-findings.md`, `apps/web/README.md`, `docker/README.md` (fully French), `infra/README.md`; `essai/` (34 files, 100 % French). | ✔ | med |

## 3. Design decisions

### 3.1 Consuming an external MCP gateway (stream S46, ADR 0097)

**Today.** No agent ever talks to a third-party MCP server: `StageInput.tools.mcp` only holds the
pod-local `choregos` sidecar and `memory`. Organisation MCP servers are reached by the **API pod**
through the hand-written `ClientMcp` (`packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/client.py`)
inside the broker (`apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/courtier.py`) and exposed to agents as
sidecar tools `<connector>__<operation>` via `/internal/runs/{id}/tools`. That broker path carries
all governance (born-forbidden operations, approvals, governed writes, schemas, result bounding,
injection guard, cost ledger `mcp:<connector>`, `TOOL_CALLED` digests, spans, network journal).
Stdio servers (`needs_hosting`) and OAuth servers (`needs_oauth`) of the catalogue cannot be
installed. The client speaks 2025-03-26 → 2025-11-25 only; MCP 2026-07-28 (ADR 0017) is not
implemented.

**Decision — "Choregos consumes an MCP gateway; it never is one" (ADR 0097).**

1. The gateway is another product, met only through public interfaces: MCP Streamable HTTP
   (2024-11-05 … 2026-07-28 negotiated) and standard HTTP auth — bearer, API-key header, OAuth 2.0
   client credentials with an RFC 8707 `resource`, or a short-lived **JWT bearer assertion**
   signed by a per-organisation key whose public half Choregos publishes as a JWKS. No vendor
   SDK, admin API, header or code path; vendor names only as examples in `docs/`. A boundary test
   (`tests/licences/test_mcp_gateway_boundary.py`) enforces it, as ADR 0079 does for graal.
2. Agents never reach the gateway: the broker stays the agent→tool policy point; the gateway is
   the policy point for hosting, upstream credentials/OAuth and upstream network. No upstream
   credential of a gateway-served server is ever stored in Choregos.
3. One gateway per organisation, an `Organization.settings` key `mcp_gateway` (copy of the
   outbound-proxy pattern, ADR 0087): endpoint, routing `path` (`{server}` in the URL) or
   `header`, auth mode, sealed write-only credential, audience, timeout, mode
   `off | available | required` (`required` refuses new direct `mcp` connectors and calls to
   existing ones). `org_admin` only, audited, with `POST …/test` (initialize + tools/list).
4. A new connector type `mcp-gateway` (kind `mcp`, config `server`, no secret field): discovery,
   drift, per-operation policy and governed writes are unchanged because the kind stays `mcp`.
5. Catalogue entries that are `needs_hosting` / `needs_oauth` gain an organisation-specific
   `install_routes` (`gateway`) when the org has a gateway: the gateway hosts them.
6. Network: the gateway host is opened for the `api` and `workers` families (governed writes run
   in the workers), never for runners; `CHOREGOS_MCP_GATEWAY_INTERNAL_HOSTS` is the only way to
   aim at http / private / in-cluster hosts (SSRF guard); chart `global.mcpGateway` renders the
   NetworkPolicy.
7. `FakeMcpGateway` (scriptable, all auth modes, JWKS-verified assertions) for CI, the demo
   fakes server and `docker compose --profile mcp-gateway`; a nightly live test against a real
   gateway that **fails** (not skips) when configured and unreachable.

Precisions that came out of the critique (all re-verified against the code):

- The MCP client keeps its own `CLIENT_VERSIONS` (core's `VERSIONS` stays what Choregos's own
  servers announce), never sends `MCP-Protocol-Version` on `initialize`, refuses an unknown
  negotiated version, re-initialises once after a 404 (lost session on a load-balanced gateway),
  and takes per-request headers (OAuth token cache, per-call assertion).
- Every refusal before a call is a typed `GatewayRefusal` with a code (`gateway_required`,
  `gateway_not_configured`, `gateway_assertion_key_missing`): a 403 tool error of type
  `network_policy` on reads and writes, a 403 before an approval-gated Action exists, a 409 at
  declaration and discovery, a final `EffetRefuse` in the workers — never an Action row, a
  ledger line, a 500 or a Temporal retry.
- "Never for runners" is enforced in the API, not only by the chart: the gateway host is denied in
  every run's effective network, `runners` openings of it are refused, and `mcp-gateway`-origin
  egress rules are revoked when the gateway moves or is deleted (S46-19).
- The JWKS answers the same 404 for an unknown organisation and for one without a key.
- No DB migration and no Temporal workflow change (effects are activity code): no replay history
  needed for S46.

Defaults chosen for S46's open points (the integrator may change them before dispatch):
`DELETE` of the gateway is refused (409 `gateway_in_use`) while `mcp-gateway` connectors exist;
`off` keeps them but refuses calls; key rotation publishes the new key and keeps the previous
one in the JWKS; the YAML tool catalogue (ADR 0014) is out of `required` mode's scope; moving a
direct connector behind the gateway means declaring a new `mcp-gateway` connector.

### 3.2 English everywhere, finished (stream S45, ADR 0098)

**Today.** ADR 0046 (English everywhere) is enforced only on *added* lines
(`tools/check_english.py`); S34-04 renamed ontology, cli, tools-mcp, tools, charts prose,
templates and parts of `tests/`. What remains (inventory at `b3a7893`): ~11.6 k French prose
lines; French identifier declarations — apps/api ≈ 2,580, apps/web ≈ 1,000, packages/adapters
≈ 820, apps/orchestrator ≈ 800, packages/core ≈ 670, packages/runner ≈ 230; ~400 French-named
paths (25 api modules, 21 migration files, 80 web files, `essai/`, `tests/langue`,
`tests/paquets`, runbooks…); STATUS.md is 1,132/1,709 lines French; the root README says the
reference documentation "below is in French".

**Decision — "English everywhere, finished: a per-area ratchet, aliases with a window, and the
names that stay frozen" (ADR 0098).**

1. **Measure first (S45-01).** `tools/check_english.py --all` counts per tracked file: French
   prose on every line, French identifier declarations (Python AST; TS declarations) and French
   path components. Counts live in `tools/english_baseline/<area>.tsv` (one file per area → no
   merge hot spot). A count can only go down (`--update-baseline` refuses a rise); a new file
   with findings fails. It replaces the `ENGLISH` / `STILL_FRENCH` tuples of
   `tests/langue/test_english_packages.py`. Escapes: `lang: fr-ok` (line),
   `lang: fr-ok-file` (a shim or redirect stub), `FROZEN_PATHS` (history files named after
   recorded workflow ids).
2. **Serial enablers before any mechanical rename**: the contract change (access-report keys
   `evenements/deduites/acces/motifs/autre`, `role.playbook_introuvable`, S34-03 aliases removed,
   `?jeton=` kept as alias); **dual-read** of every French key/error type recorded in Temporal
   histories or persisted in rows (readers first, writers switch one release later, S45-39);
   the plugin API used by `choregos-ee` in English with deprecated French aliases (helper
   `choregos_core.compat`); env vars via `AliasChoices`; `python -m` / console-script shims.
3. **Alias policy.** Internal renames: `Old = New  # lang: fr-ok alias:internal` or an old-path
   re-export shim (`# lang: fr-ok-file alias:internal`); names that tests monkeypatch use
   `compat.forward_assignments`; all internal aliases are deleted by S45-40. External names
   (plugin API, env vars, entry points, deprecated contract fields, alert names via a
   `legacy_alertname` label, runbook URLs via redirect stubs, the YAML key `outils:`) keep an
   `alias:external` window of one release (removed in 0.22.0) with a removal issue each.
4. **Frozen forever**: `workflow.patched` values, activity/signal wire aliases, error-type
   strings and payload keys recorded in histories, history file contents (and the four history
   files whose stem is a recorded workflow id), Grafana dashboard uids (no alias exists).
   Persisted French JSON values are read under both names; no row is rewritten.
5. **Package stories are file-disjoint**, split by sub-area, each ≤ L. Files owned by S46-01
   (the MCP path) are excluded everywhere. apps/api renames run **after** the S47 security
   stories.
6. **Archives deleted** (S45-38): `docs/fr/`, the 43 French ADR stubs, the 7 French plan
   pointers — after every link and the tests that count or follow them are fixed. Runbook
   renames keep 3-line redirect stubs (alert `runbook_url`s of deployed versions point at them).

**Rejected**: one mass-rename PR; PEP 562 `__getattr__` aliases (they give `Any` to strict
mypy); one shared baseline file; rewriting histories or persisted rows; renaming Grafana uids;
dual Kyverno rules (renamed with upgrade notes instead).

### 3.3 Hardening and gaps (stream S47, ADRs 0099–0104)

ADR numbers: 0097 = S46 (MCP gateway), 0098 = S45 (English finished), 0099–0104 = S47. On `main`,
0090–0096 exist and 0088/0089 are held by open PRs; take the next free number at merge time.

- **P0 security chain in apps/api, serialized** (they share `config.py`, `main.py`,
  `routers/auth.py`, the API subchart): S47-01 session key refused at API start when empty,
  published or < 32 bytes on a deployed instance, full-length purpose-bound MAC (legacy tags read
  for one release, verify-only previous key for rotation) → S47-02 limiter keyed on the address
  seen by the nearest trusted proxy, IPv6 grouped by /64, ≤ 10,000 keys LRU → S47-03 Origin guard
  on cookie-authenticated mutations, explicit CORS rendered by the chart, bounded `X-Request-Id`
  → S47-04 mandatory OIDC discovery with issuer check, sign-in bound to `sub`, ID-token claims
  checked (ADR 0100) → S47-05 IdP-granted roles revoked at the next IdP sign-in (provenance
  column; manual rows untouched; the last org_admin kept). S47-06: project config revision,
  `base_revision`, 409 `stale_base`, row lock (contract change).
- **Governance that is not enforced is refused when written** (ADR 0101): `review.humans`,
  review agents that never run, `approvals.*`, `attempts.default_max`,
  `review.require_human_for_risk` → 422 / validator errors at policy PUT, workflow save and
  project creation; stored documents keep loading. Catalogue tools that may write are dropped
  from the run's tool list unless marked `read_only` (a direct call answers 409). The project's
  allowed backends are enforced at stage start.
- **The tracker carries no decision** (ADR 0102): approve/reject/answer/scope answered with a
  console link and never queued in the interpreter; pause/resume/stop honoured only from
  HMAC-verified GitHub deliveries whose numeric commenter id an org admin linked to a member;
  card moves no longer move work items (S47-38); a webhook for an ended interpreter no longer
  500s and loses the rest of the delivery.
- **Architecture ratchets** (ADR 0099 "the API is a dependency that only shrinks"): tests count
  crossing imports (149 orchestrator→api, 22 ontology→api, 1 services→routers, 7 router-private,
  12 org lookups) and fail only above the ceiling; reducing stories lower it. One shared
  organisation lookup; routers share helpers through services; cross-backend definitions as pure
  functions in `choregos_core`; one `HumanRequest` constructor (agent questions get a due date).
- **Tests/CI**: orchestrator suite on PostgreSQL (then migrated schema + application role, under
  RLS); replay histories for the 4 missing workflows plus a guard "one history per workflow";
  nightly live tests fail when a required service's secrets vanish; one measured coverage floor
  enforced by `make ci`, and a web floor.
- **Functional**: SMTP notifier with its fake; console screens for the cross-backend metric,
  memory re-import, memory A/B report, gateway keys; GitLab CI and GitLab SCM adapters (P2,
  one-day project access token per run, ADR 0104); KEK key-wrapper seam (P2, ADR 0103, no KMS
  shipped).
- **Splits of the largest modules** (P2) only after the English rename of their package, with
  re-exports so import paths and Temporal names do not move; the interpreter split is replayed.
- S47-37 (integrator) collects an "Upgrading to 0.21" section in `docs/deployment.md`.

## 4. Execution waves

Every story of a wave is file-disjoint from the others and all its `depends_on` are in earlier waves (checked mechanically when the plan was recalibrated on `main` 3156d12). Inside a wave: contract-change PRs merge first, then features and hardening, renames last. `C` = contract change.

| Wave | Stories | Why this order |
|:--|:--|:--|
| 0 | `S47-06` M C — contract(projects): a project configuration carries a revision; a PATCH from a stale base answers 409<br>`S45-00` S — docs(plan): the 2026-10-27 plan, streams S45-S47 and the PR template's STATUS row<br>`S45-01` L — feat(tools): full-repo English ratchet with per-area baselines, identifier and path passes<br>`S46-01` M — refactor(adapters): English names for the MCP client and the fake MCP hook, French aliases kept<br>`S46-18` M — refactor(api): the broker lives in services/broker.py in English, services/courtier.py re-exports it<br>`S47-01` M — fix(api): refuse an empty, published or short session secret at API start and sign with a full-length purpose-bound MAC | S45-00 (integrator: this plan's PR) first. Serial enablers. Merge them one at a time in this order, each rebased on the one before, before any wave-1 branch is cut. They have no dependencies, touch no file in common, and each heads one of the longest chains. S45-01 is the English ratchet; its ADR is renumbered 0098 and its baseline check becomes 'count <= baseline'. S46-01 puts English MCP client names in place, with French aliases, before any adapters or apps/api rename (the cross-track rule). S46-18 creates services/broker.py. S47-06 is the first of five serialized contract changes. S47-01 is the session secret and heads the security/auth/config chain that must land before the apps/api rename. |
| 1 | `S45-03` M — fix(orchestrator): read recorded and persisted French keys under both names before any rename<br>`S45-41` S — feat(core): choregos_core.compat, two-way aliases for renamed module names<br>`S46-02` S — docs(adr): 0097 Choregos consumes an MCP gateway and never is one, with its boundary test<br>`S46-03` M — feat(adapters): the MCP client negotiates 2026-07-28, survives a lost session and takes per-request headers<br>`S46-04` M — feat(adapters): a fake MCP gateway, scriptable and served alone<br>`S47-02` M — fix(api): rate-limit sign-in and webhooks by the trusted-proxy client address, in bounded memory<br>`S47-17` M — feat(web): project screens for the cross-backend review metric and the memory re-import<br>`S47-19` M — refactor(core): one definition of the cross-backend review, used by the metric and by the reviewer swap<br>`S47-22` S — ci: run the orchestrator suite on PostgreSQL<br>`S47-23` M — test(replay): record histories for ProjectProvisioning, FindingsTriage, MemoryIngestion and AgentTrial, and require one per workflow<br>`S47-24` S — ci(nightly): live tests fail when a required service's secrets vanish, and a crashing conformance report surfaces | Heads of every remaining chain. **Releases.** Cut **0.21.0 after wave 14** (security hardening, MCP gateway consumption, dual readers, all aliases with their windows, S47-37's 'Upgrading to 0.21', plus main's own post-0.20.6 changes: kernel confinement and the plugin stack). Merge S45-39 (English writers, wave 26) only after 0.21.0 is released **and deployed**. Cut **0.22.0 after waves 25–26**, then run S45-49 (wave 27). Aliases first shipped after the 0.21.0 cut keep a window until 0.23.0.

**Alembic.** On `main` the head is `c9e1a3b5d7f8` (`20261028_0900_plugin_servers.py`) and file-name order no longer matches chain order. S47-05 (w4) and S47-14 (w8) each add one revision on the head of the day; S47-14's `down_revision` is S47-05's. S45-13 renames migration *file names* only, never revision ids. Check `alembic heads` shows one head after waves 4, 8, 12, 15.

**Replay safety net.** S47-23 (w1) records the four missing histories before S45-22 (w4) renames the workflows; S45-22, S47-32 and S45-39 replay every history.

**Optional speed-up (≈3 waves).** Before wave 1, an integrator docs story may split `docs/usage.md`, `docs/deployment.md` and `docs/security.md` into per-topic pages (they serialize ~35 stories); then re-plan from wave 1.

## 5. Stories

Each block is self-contained: give a worker the block of its story (see §1.3). Extract one with `awk '/^### S46-08 /{p=1;print;next} /^##/{p=0} p' docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md` (replace the id). The **Recalibrated on main** list of a story overrides the text above it where they differ.

### S45-00 · docs(plan): the 2026-10-27 plan, streams S45-S47 and the PR template's STATUS row

**Wave** 0 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** S · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `docs(plan): the 2026-10-27 plan, streams S45-S47 and the PR template's STATUS row (S45-00)`

**Goal.** Commit this plan where every worker can read it, register its streams (S45, S46, S47) and stories in the backlog, and give the PR template the sections the plan requires (STATUS row, release note, rename table). Integrator story, merged before anything else in wave 0.

**Context.**

The plan lives at /root/.claude/plans/fais-une-analyse-critique-greedy-journal.md in the planning session; the integrator copies it into the repository. `docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml` lists streams (header block `streams:`) and stories (`stories:` entries with id, title, stream, sprint, priority, size, depends_on, acceptance, notes). `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md` has the sections Story, Change, Acceptance criteria, Proof, Contracts, Findings filed. `tools/check_english.py` reads added Markdown prose: French identifiers must be inside backticks.

**Files.**

- Create: `docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md`, `tests/docs/test_plan_stories_in_backlog.py`
- Modify: `docs/plan/00-index.md`, `docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml`, `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`

**Steps.**

1. Copy the plan file to `docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md` unchanged.
2. Run `uv run python tools/check_english.py`; for each flagged line of the new file, put the French identifier in backticks (do not translate identifiers).
3. In `docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml`, add under `streams:` exactly `S45: "English everywhere, finished (plan 2026-10-27, ADR 0098)"`, `S46: "Consuming an external MCP gateway (plan 2026-10-27, ADR 0097)"`, `S47: "Hardening and gaps from the 2026-10-27 audit"` (never an S43 or S44 key: main uses those streams for the plugin stack and kernel confinement); then one `stories:` entry per story of §5 with `id`, `title`, `stream`, `sprint` = its wave number from §4, `priority` (P0→1, P1→2, P2→3), `size` and `depends_on` (the acceptance lives in the plan).
4. In `docs/plan/00-index.md`, on line 3 (the 'Current plans (English):' line), after the `PLAN-2026-10-27-servicenow` link, add `[PLAN-2026-10-27](PLAN-2026-10-27.md) (streams S45-S47: English, MCP gateway, hardening)`.
5. In `.github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md`, add three sections after `## Findings filed`: `## STATUS row` ("What this PR proves, and what it does not prove — the integrator copies it into docs/plan/STATUS.md"), `## Release note` ("Operator-visible change, or 'none'"), `## Rename table` ("S43 stories only: old → new modules, symbols and paths; also appended to docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/<story-id>.md").
6. Create `tests/docs/test_plan_stories_in_backlog.py` with `test_every_plan_story_is_in_the_backlog`: parse only the story headings of `docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md` matching `^### (S4[5-7]-\d+[a-z]?) ·` (the plan's prose also cites main's own S43/S44 stories, which are not backlog entries), load `docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml` with `yaml.safe_load`, assert every id is a backlog story id.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Stream keys: on main, S43 is 'agents ship as plugins' (docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27-servicenow.md) and S44 is kernel confinement / SkillSpector / code-graph trial; neither is registered under `streams:` in docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml (the block still stops at S38, L6-43, and the file is unchanged since b3a7893). Add exactly the renumbered keys S45 (English), S46 (MCP gateway, ADR 0097) and S47 (hardening); never add S43 or S44 keys, and never give a story entry stream S43/S44.
- docs/plan/00-index.md: put the link on line 3, the 'Current plans (English):' line, after the `PLAN-2026-10-27-servicenow` link, e.g. `[PLAN-2026-10-27](PLAN-2026-10-27.md) (streams S45-S47: English, MCP gateway, hardening)`.
- tests/docs/test_plan_stories_in_backlog.py: read only docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md and match only story headings with `^### (S4[5-7]-\d+[a-z]?) ·` (after renumbering). The plan's prose cites main's own S45-0x/S46-0x stories, which are not backlog entries and must not be required. tests/docs/test_comptes_de_status.py::test_each_story_has_one_row requires unique ids in STATUS.md, which already holds S43-01..S43-07 and S44-01..S44-03 rows from main, so every id this PR writes must be the renumbered one.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/docs/test_plan_stories_in_backlog.py :: test_every_plan_story_is_in_the_backlog :: every story id of the plan is a story of 08-backlog.yaml (fails before step 3)

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] The plan is at docs/plan/PLAN-2026-10-27.md and linked from 00-index.md
- [ ] Streams S45, S46, S47 and every story of §5 are in 08-backlog.yaml
- [ ] The PR template has STATUS row, Release note and Rename table sections
- [ ] make ci green

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run python tools/check_english.py
uv run pytest tests/docs -q
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md here (no row changes yet)
- Do not translate French identifiers quoted in the plan; backtick them

**Docs.** `docs/plan/00-index.md`

**Goal.** Count every French prose line, distinct French identifier declaration and French path component per file. Store the counts in one baseline file per area, and fail CI whenever a count rises. Also port the shipped-text detector into tools/check_english.py, and write ADR 0098, which every later S45 story follows.

**Context.**

tools/check_english.py today reads only the lines a diff adds. Anchors:

Lexicon. A prototype at b3a7893 found no English word among the strong-word hits:
- words(name): split on [^A-Za-z0-9]+; DROP tokens holding a digit; split camelCase with `[A-Z]+(?=[A-Z][a-z])|[A-Z]?[a-z]+|[A-Z]+`; lowercase.
- A name is French when one word is in CODE_IDENTIFIER_WORDS, or at least two DISTINCT words are in CODE_IDENTIFIER_WEAK.
- CODE_IDENTIFIER_WORDS = (IDENTIFIER_WORDS - {depart, departs, coupe}) | STRONG. 'depart' is an English verb (depart_now, /trains/{env}/depart).
- STRONG = acces accessibilite activite activites adaptateur adaptateurs affiche ajout ajoute ancien ancienne anciens anglais annonce annoncee appartient appel appelant appeler appels applicatif cout couts coutures couvre creer dans debordement debut declarer decouverte deduites defaut defauts delai demandes demarre demarrer demarrage densite depense depenses deploiement derniere dernier erreur erreurs escalade essai essais etape etapes etats etiquette etiquettes evenement evenements executer executeur executeurs exemple exemples exigence exigences externe externes fait faits familles fantomes faux fiche fichier fichiers fournisseurs fraiche fraicheur frontiere gabarit gabarits garanties garde gardes gele geles geste gestes gouvernee gouvernees gouvernes graphe greffon greffons grilles historique humain humaine humains identite illisible indisponible installateur installateurs installe integre interne introuvable irreversibles jeton jetons laisse laterale lecteur ligne lignes limiteur lire lisible liste lister livraison livre livres locataire locaux logiciel logicielle longueur maitrisee manifeste marquer matrice membre memoire mesures metier metriques migrer modeles montee montes mort moteur mots naissance noeud nombre noms nouveau nouvelle nouveaux observabilite onglets ontologie operabilite optionnelle oubli outil outils panier panneau panneaux paquet fr-ok -->
- WEAK = le la les de du des un et en au aux l d n qu ce se sa ses son par pour sur sous sans qui que ne pas plus est ('a' is NOT in it). <!-- lang: fr-ok -->

Counting:
- prose: 1 per French prose line;
- identifiers: 1 per DISTINCT declared name per file; a name counts if at least one of its declarations is on a line without `lang: fr-ok`;
- path: 0 or 1 per file.

Areas (longest prefix wins), 23:
- api-mcp: apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/
- api-routers: .../routers/
- api-services: .../services/
- api-schemas: .../schemas/
- api-migrations: .../migrations/
- api-subsystems: .../db/, .../a2a/, .../egress/, .../knowledge/
- api-tests: apps/api/tests/
- api-root: apps/api/
- orchestrator-tests: apps/orchestrator/tests/
- orchestrator-src: apps/orchestrator/
- web: apps/web/
- core: packages/core/
- adapters: packages/adapters/
- runner: packages/runner/
- contracts: packages/contracts/
- packages-other: packages/
- tests-language: tests/langue/, tests/language/, tests/docs/, tests/ci/, tests/dev/, tests/licences/, tests/integrations/, tests/charts/
- tests: tests/
- docs-plan: docs/plan/
- docs: docs/
- trials: essai/, trials/
- ops: charts/, infra/, docker/
- repo: '' (everything else)

This story is the single writer this wave of tools/check_english.py, Makefile, ci.yml, AGENTS.md, docs/development.md and docs/adr/README.md. ADR number 0098 belongs to this track.

**Files.**

- Create: `tools/english_baseline/<area>.tsv for each of the 23 areas in context`, `tests/langue/test_english_baseline.py`, `docs/adr/0098-english-everywhere-finished.md`, `docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/README.md`
- Modify: `tools/check_english.py`, `tests/langue/test_check_english.py`, `tests/langue/test_english_packages.py`, `tests/langue/test_anglais.py`, `tests/langue/test_orchestrateur_en_anglais.py`, `packages/playbooks/tests/test_playbooks_in_english.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_erreurs_en_anglais.py`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`, `docs/development.md`

**Steps.**

1. Widen what is read.
   - SCANNED_DIRS += essai/, trials/, infra/, docker/, examples/, integrations/.
   - is_scanned also accepts the root files compose.yaml, justfile, pyproject.toml, cliff.toml and CODEOWNERS.
   - Readers by file type:
     - '#' comments: .sh, .toml, .ini, .Dockerfile, and files named Dockerfile, justfile or CODEOWNERS;
     - '--' comments: .sql;
     - _ts_prose(jsx=False): .js, .mjs, .mts, .css, .rs;
     - whole lines with <...>, {%...%} and {{...}} removed: .html, .j2.
   - Do not scan .json.
2. Add FILE_ESCAPE = 'lang: fr-ok-file'. A file whose first 5 lines contain it is skipped by every full-scan pass. ESCAPE lines are skipped by the prose pass and by the identifier pass.
3. Add FROZEN_PATHS: dict path -> reason. List the 4 history files with the reason 'stem is a recorded workflow id (ADR 0098)'. The path pass skips them.
4. Add CODE_IDENTIFIER_WORDS, CODE_IDENTIFIER_WEAK, _code_words(name) and code_identifier_reason(name) -> str | None, exactly as in context (digit tokens dropped, distinct weak words). Leave IDENTIFIER_WORDS and french_identifier_words unchanged.
5. Add the two declaration readers.
   - python_declarations(content) -> Iterator[tuple[int, str]] (ast): FunctionDef, AsyncFunctionDef and ClassDef names; every ast.arg; Name with Store ctx; Attribute with Store ctx (.attr); alias.asname. On SyntaxError, yield nothing.
   - ts_declarations(content): first replace every comment and string/template token matched by _TS_TOKEN with spaces (keep newlines, so line numbers hold), then match `\b(?:function|const|let|var|class|interface|type|enum)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)`.
6. Add path_reason(path): split on '/' only and apply code_identifier_reason to each whole component.
7. Add file_findings(path, content) -> list[Finding] with the counting rules in context; it honours ESCAPE, FILE_ESCAPE and FROZEN_PATHS.
   Add scan_tree(prefixes=('',)) over `git ls-files`:
   - the path pass covers tracked files under SCANNED_DIRS or at the root, minus EXCLUDED and EXCLUDED_NAMES;
   - the prose and identifier passes cover only is_scanned files;
   - it raises RuntimeError('not a git checkout') when git fails.
8. Add AREAS (the 23 in context) and area_of(path).

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- The per-area check passes when a count is **lower than or equal to** its baseline; only a rise (or a new file with findings) fails. Lowering a baseline is the integrator's job after each wave.
- After this story the TSVs under `tools/english_baseline/` are **integrator-owned**: no other story commits one (S47-47 excepted). The integrator regenerates them after each wave and checks that no area count rose.
- The identifier and path passes never flag an existing French identifier that a modified line merely reuses (S46 and S47 stories are written against today's French names).
- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

### S45-03 · fix(orchestrator): read recorded and persisted French keys under both names before any rename

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S45-01` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(orchestrator): read recorded and persisted French keys under both names before any rename (S45-03)`

**Goal.** Every reader of a French key or error type that is recorded in Temporal histories or persisted in the database also accepts the English spelling. Writers do not change (S45-39). Bare activity definitions get an explicit name=.

**Context.**

Under apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator unless prefixed. Readers to switch:
- workflows/action.py:80 `plan['effets']` (written by activities/actions.py:80 {effets, statut}).
- workflows/action.py L89-90 and L129 read {'attendre_une_preuve': {'jusqu_a'}} (written by packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/actions.py:624).
- activities/actions.py L297-312 mark_awaiting_evidence reads {en_attente, jusqu_a, preuve}. These are sent by workflows/action.py L131-153, and the activity persists actions.result 'attente'/'preuves'. L310 `.pop('attente')` is the writer's own; leave it.
- activities/train.py:424 `payload.get('conclure')` (sent by workflows/train.py:371).
- activities/interpretation.py L185 `payload['cle']` and L190 `(e.payload or {}).get('cle')`, which matches persisted events. Also 'refus'.
- workflows/interpreter.py L1204-1206: `proposee.get('refus')` and `proposee['refus']` read the recorded result of propose_transition_action, which returns {'refus': ...} at activities/actions.py L262, L274, L276.
- TrainInput (workflows/train.py L130-158): field seuil_de_relais is a continue-as-new input.

Non-retryable sets read by workflows:
- interpreter.py CONFIGURATION_REFUSALS L176-186 (AgentIndisponible, InstructionsInvalides, SkillAbsente);
- action.py EFFET L38-44 (EffetRefuse, ActionIntrouvable).

API and ontology:
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/outils.py:610 `annonce.get('ecriture')` (plugin key; the ontology service/tools.py:46 WRITES_KEY writes it);
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/garde_fous.py:44 counts audit payloads with 'ecriture';
- the ontology persists effect kinds 'ontology.effet'/'ontology.preuve' (service/actions.py L50-51) and the params key 'ontologie' (PARAMS_KEY L53).

Bare `@activity.defn` decorators: activities/actions.py L61, 83, 152, 194, 248, 297, 316.

tests/replay replays 68 histories, which are never edited. tests/replay/test_english_names.py L33-50 pins the patch ids.

temporalio's default converter ignores unknown dataclass keys and fills missing ones with defaults, so a new optional TrainInput field is safe; prove it with a test.

A one-line edit each to mcp/garde_fous.py and mcp/outils.py: run in a wave where no S47 story edits those files.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/wire_names.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_mcp_write_key.py`, `packages/ontology/tests/test_effect_kind_aliases.py`
- Modify: `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/action.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/interpreter.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/train.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/actions.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/train.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/interpretation.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/outils.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/garde_fous.py`, `packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/actions.py`, `tests/replay/test_english_names.py`

**Steps.**

1. wire_names.py (pure stdlib, importable in the workflow sandbox like workflows/activity_names.py):
   - FORMER_PAYLOAD_KEYS (English -> French): effects:effets, status:statut, states:etats, await_evidence:attendre_une_preuve, until:jusqu_a, waiting:en_attente, evidence:preuve, conclude:conclure, key:cle, refusal:refus. Mark it `# lang: fr-ok`, with the comment 'recorded in histories and persisted rows (ADR 0098 §4): read forever'.
   - read_key(payload, english, default=None).
   - FORMER_ERROR_TYPES: AgentUnavailable:AgentIndisponible, InvalidInstructions:InstructionsInvalides, SkillMissing:SkillAbsente, EffectRefused:EffetRefuse, ActionNotFound:ActionIntrouvable, ActionNotYetVisible:ActionPasEncoreVisible.
   - both_names(*english) -> list[str].
2. Error-type sets:
   - CONFIGURATION_REFUSALS = frozenset({'ModelResolutionError', 'SkillPendingReview', 'KnowledgeRefused', 'BackendDisabled', *both_names('AgentUnavailable', 'InvalidInstructions', 'SkillMissing')});
   - EFFET's non_retryable_error_types = both_names('EffectRefused', 'ActionNotFound').
3. Workflow reads:
   - action.py L80: read_key(plan, 'effects'); L89: read_key(resultat, 'await_evidence'); L129: read_key(attente, 'until');
   - interpreter.py L1204-1206: `refus = read_key(proposee, 'refusal')`, and use it in both places.
   Workflow-sent dicts stay unchanged.
4. Activity reads:
   - actions.py mark_awaiting_evidence: read waiting, until and evidence through read_key;
   - add `name='<function name>'` to the seven bare decorators;
   - add `# lang: fr-ok` to the `async def <french alias>` lines L343-373.
   - train.py:424: read_key(payload, 'conclude').
   - interpretation.py: L185 read_key(payload, 'key'); L190 `read_key(e.payload or {}, 'key') == cle`; refusal through read_key.
5. TrainInput:
   - add `relay_threshold: int | None = None` after seuil_de_relais;
   - suffix the seuil_de_relais line with `# lang: fr-ok — continue-as-new inputs recorded before S45-03 carry this name (ADR 0098)`;
   - add threshold(), which returns relay_threshold, else seuil_de_relais, else SEUIL_DE_RELAIS;
   - seuil() delegates to threshold() (alias:internal);
   - seuil_au_repos_d_avant reads relay_threshold, then seuil_de_relais;
   - do not change continue_as_new.
6. API:
   - outils.py:610: `bool(annonce.get('write', annonce.get('ecriture')))  # lang: fr-ok alias:external`;
   - garde_fous.py:44 counts payloads where 'write' or 'ecriture' is true.
7. Ontology service/actions.py:
   - register the same executors under 'ontology.effect' and 'ontology.evidence' (constants EFFECT_EN, EVIDENCE_EN) with the same policy;
   - read params.get('ontology'), then params.get(PARAMS_KEY);
   - writers still write the French values.
8. tests/replay/test_english_names.py: add the AST test that every @activity.defn under apps/orchestrator/src is a Call with a name keyword.
9. Run tests/replay: all 68 histories replay. Then follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Vocabulary (owner's rule (c), ADR 0093 Decision 1): the tool announcement read at apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/outils.py:610 comes from an *extension*, an entry-point package declared through `_outils_des_greffons` (L593). It is not an ADR 0093 plugin. Name the test `test_extension_tool_marked_write_or_ecriture_is_a_write_tool`, write 'the ontology extension active' in test_both_effect_kinds_resolve, and say 'extension', never 'plugin', in every new comment and docstring.
- Every anchor in Context holds at 3156d12 (no listed file changed), including the seven bare `@activity.defn` in activities/actions.py (L61, 83, 152, 194, 248, 297, 316), which are the only bare ones under apps/orchestrator/src. The plugin stream changed activities/base.py (pin_plugin_tracker) and workflows/knowledge_distill.py (a cancellation branch); neither reads a FORMER_PAYLOAD_KEYS value, so the AST guard needs no exemption for them.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_read_key_prefers_english_then_former :: read_key({'effets': 2}, 'effects') == 2; read_key({'effects': 3, 'effets': 2}, 'effects') == 3
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_no_workflow_or_activity_reads_a_former_key_directly :: AST over workflows/*.py and activities/*.py: no `.get('<v>')` call and no Load-context subscript `['<v>']` for any FORMER_PAYLOAD_KEYS value; fails today on action.py:80, interpreter.py:1204 and interpretation.py:185
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_configuration_refusals_accept_both_names ::
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_effect_retry_policy_names_both ::

- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_migration_record_is_idempotent_across_key_names :: an event whose payload has 'cle' = k blocks a second record for {'key': k}
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_wire_names.py :: test_train_input_decodes_with_and_without_relay_threshold :: the default payload converter decodes both JSON shapes and threshold() is equal
- tests/replay/test_english_names.py :: test_every_activity_names_itself ::

### S45-04 · feat(api): the plugin API in English (plugins, effects, quotas, admission, secrets), French names warned until 0.22.0

**Wave** 9 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-02`, `S45-03`, `S45-41` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(api): the plugin API in English (plugins, effects, quotas, admission, secrets), French names warned until 0.22.0 (S45-04)`

**Goal.** Give choregos-ee an English plugin API for the router, tool, gesture, installer, effect, quota, admission and secret-resolver seams. Every French name keeps working until 0.22.0 with a DeprecationWarning. edition.py and Principal follow in S45-12, and load_plugins in S45-10.

**Context.**

apps/api/src/choregos_api/greffons.py (352 lines):
- routers and sections: declarer_un_routeur L32, routeurs_declares L40, signatures L44, reinitialiser L66, declarer_une_section_d_administration L94, sections_declarees L112, chemins_d_une_section L130;
- tool providers: FournisseurDOutils(lister, appeler) L165 (frozen dataclass), declarer_un_fournisseur_d_outils(nom, lister, appeler) L180, fournisseurs_d_outils L187, FournisseurHumain(lister, appeler, en_attente) L195, declarer_des_outils_pour_les_humains L219, fournisseurs_humains L227;
- template installers: _INSTALLATEURS L241 (patched by apps/api/tests/test_gabarit_extensions.py:42), declarer_un_installateur_de_gabarit L244, installateurs_de_gabarit L253;
- gestures: GESTES L265 (English ids), DemandeDeGeste(geste, org, principal, cible) L275, GesteRefuse(raison, nature='interdit'|'conflit'|'reauth') L282, declarer_un_controle_de_geste L296, controler L304 (maps conflit->409, reauth->401, else 403), reinitialiser_les_controles L324;
- theme: declare_theme_provider L340.

apps/api/src/choregos_api/effets.py: EffetRefuse L31, ContexteEffet L37, Executer L43, _EFFETS, declarer_un_effet(nom, executer, *, politique='approval') L51, politique_de_l_effet L66, effet L71, effets_declares L78, rendre L82, reinitialiser L260. The DSL value aliases verifier/motif/oui (L255-268) keep their lang: fr-ok lines.

Core:
- packages/core/src/choregos_core/quotas.py: Quota(cpu, memoire, pods) frozen with __post_init__ checks, DEFAUT, Fournisseur, declarer_un_quota, quota_pour, reinitialiser;
- admission.py: DemandeDeRun (English fields), AdmissionRefusee, Controle, _CONTROLES, declarer_une_admission, admissions_declarees, verifier, reinitialiser;
- secrets.py: SecretIntrouvable L24, declarer_un_resolveur L95.

Internal importers (main.py, deps.py, routers, services, orchestrator activities and gitops, ontology) are NOT switched here (rule h). They keep resolving through the typed shims and aliases. Their own stories switch them, so this story stays clear of S47 files.

The documented plugin API: docs/development.md L225-530, docs/deployment.md:382, docs/usage.md L157 and L2143.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9. EXTERNAL_ALIAS_REMOVAL comes from choregos_core.compat.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/effects.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/greffons.py (becomes a shim)`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/effets.py (becomes a shim)`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/quotas.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/admission.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/secrets.py (two names only)`, `docs/development.md`, `docs/deployment.md`, `docs/usage.md`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/extensions.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_extension_api_aliases.py`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv greffons.py plugins.py`, then translate it fully. Renames:
   - routers and sections: declare_router, declared_routers, signatures, reset, declare_admin_section, declared_sections, section_paths;
   - tool providers: ToolProvider(list_tools, call_tool), declare_tool_provider, tool_providers, HumanToolProvider(list_tools, call_tool, pending), declare_human_tools, human_tool_providers;
   - template installers: _INSTALLERS, declare_template_installer, template_installers;
   - gestures: GESTURES, GestureRequest(gesture, org, principal, target), GestureRefused(reason, nature='forbidden'), declare_gesture_check, check_gesture, reset_gesture_checks.
   The two provider dataclasses become `@dataclass(frozen=True, init=False)`. Their explicit __init__ takes the English fields positionally, plus keyword-only `lister=None`, `appeler=None` and `en_attente=None`, each on its own line marked `# lang: fr-ok alias:external`; a former keyword warns. They also get properties lister, appeler and en_attente. declare_tool_provider and declare_human_tools accept the same former keywords.
   GestureRequest gets the properties geste and cible.
   GestureRefused maps 'interdit' to 'forbidden' and 'conflit' to 'conflict' with a warning; .nature is always English; .raison is a property. check_gesture compares against 'conflict'.
   At the end of the module:
   - `_FORMER = {...}` with one line per public pair, each `# lang: fr-ok alias:external`, then `alias_names(__name__, _FORMER, removed_in=EXTERNAL_ALIAS_REMOVAL)`;
   - `alias_names(__name__, {'_INSTALLATEURS': '_INSTALLERS', '_ROUTEURS': '_ROUTERS', ...})` for the private names, each line fr-ok.
2. Recreate greffons.py:
   - SPDX line, then `# lang: fr-ok-file alias:external — former module of the plugin API, removed in 0.22.0 (ADR 0098)`;
   - `import sys`, then explicit `from .plugins import <English names>`;
   - one typed `Former = English` per former name, including _INSTALLATEURS;
   - `replace_module(__name__, sys.modules['choregos_api.plugins'])`.
3. Same procedure for effets.py -> effects.py: EffectRefused, EffectContext, Execute, _EFFECTS, declare_effect(name, execute, *, policy='approval', politique=None), effect_policy, effect, declared_effects, render, reset. politique= warns. Keep the DSL value aliases and their lines.
4. In place, in core:
   - quotas.py: QuotaProvider, DEFAULT, declare_quota, quota_for, reset. Quota(cpu, memory, pods) becomes `@dataclass(frozen=True, init=False)` with __init__(cpu='32', memory='96Gi', pods=40, *, memoire=None) (the former keyword on its own fr-ok line). __init__ uses object.__setattr__ and keeps the __post_init__ checks. Add a `memoire` property.
   - admission.py: RunRequest, AdmissionRefused, AdmissionCheck, _CHECKS, declare_admission, declared_admissions, check, reset.
   - secrets.py: SecretNotFound and declare_resolver only.
   Each module keeps typed `Former = English  # lang: fr-ok alias:external` lines and ends with alias_names(..., removed_in=EXTERNAL_ALIAS_REMOVAL). Translate quotas.py and admission.py fully; in secrets.py translate only the two renamed definitions.
5. Do not switch any importer outside these files (rule h). Run `uv run mypy ...`: every former import must still type-check through the shims.
6. Docs: rewrite the plugin examples in English in docs/development.md L225-530, docs/deployment.md:382 and docs/usage.md L157/L2143. Add the table 'Former names (removed in 0.22.0)' to docs/development.md.
7. Open the issue 'Remove the French plugin API aliases in 0.22.0' and a choregos-ee coordination issue listing every pair. Link both in the PR.
8. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Owner's rule (c): the English module is apps/api/src/choregos_api/extensions.py, NOT plugins.py. On main, 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package: routers/plugins.py, schemas/plugins.py, services/plugin_*.py and choregos_core/plugins/ exist.
  - Run `git mv greffons.py extensions.py`.
  - The greffons.py shim ends with `replace_module(__name__, sys.modules['choregos_api.extensions'])`, and its header says 'former module of the extension API'.
  - The test file is apps/api/tests/test_extension_api_aliases.py. test_the_former_modules_are_the_new_ones asserts `importlib.import_module('choregos_api.greffons') is choregos_api.extensions`. Parametrize over greffons/extensions.
  - PR title: `feat(api): the extension API in English (extensions, effects, quotas, admission, secrets), French names warned until 0.22.0 (S45-04)`. The removal issue is 'Remove the French extension API aliases in 0.22.0'.
  - Wherever this story writes 'plugin API' or 'load_plugins (S45-10)', write 'extension API' and 'load_extensions (S45-10)'.
- greffons.py is now 353 lines. S42-15 added `"section_icon"` to SECTION_FEATURES (L92), so every anchor from L95 on moves by +1:
  - declarer_une_section_d_administration L95, sections_declarees L113, chemins_d_une_section L131;
  - FournisseurDOutils L166, declarer_un_fournisseur_d_outils L181, fournisseurs_d_outils L188, FournisseurHumain L196, declarer_des_outils_pour_les_humains L220, fournisseurs_humains L228;
  - _INSTALLATEURS L242, declarer_un_installateur_de_gabarit L245, installateurs_de_gabarit L254;
  - GESTES L266, DemandeDeGeste L276, GesteRefuse L283, declarer_un_controle_de_geste L297, reinitialiser_les_controles L325, declare_theme_provider L341.

### S45-05 · feat(api): English entry points, ontology activation variable and agent catalogue behind compatibility shims

**Wave** 11 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-04` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(api): English entry points, ontology activation variable and agent catalogue behind compatibility shims (S45-05)`

**Goal.** Rename the French surfaces that deployments and scripts reach by name, and keep each former name working until 0.22.0:
- `python -m choregos_api.migrer`;
- the choregos-admin module;
- CHOREGOS_ESSAI_ONTOLOGIE;
- the test-suite PostgreSQL variable;
- the agent catalogue package and its `python -m ...evals` entry point.

**Context.**

Entry points:
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/migrer.py: GROUPE_DE_MIGRATIONS L38, emplacements_des_greffons L43, configuration(*, avec_les_greffons=True) L62 (called by apps/api/tests/test_migrer.py:26 and packages/ontology/tests/test_plugin.py:426), main L74.

- choregos-admin = 'choregos_api.outils:main' (apps/api/pyproject.toml:33); apps/api/tests/test_routeurs_nus.py:767 imports outils.app.

Environment variables:
- packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/plugin.py:25 ACTIVATION = 'CHOREGOS_ESSAI_ONTOLOGIE' (docs/getting-started.md:146, ADR 0036:17).
- CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES is set at .github/workflows/ci.yml:236 and read at apps/api/tests/conftest.py:30 (S45-42 switches conftest).
- The broker variable CHOREGOS_COURTIER_ATTENTE_ECRITURE_S (config.py:139) is S46-01's, with services/courtier.py.

Agent catalogue, packages/core/src/choregos_core/catalogue_d_agents/:
- __init__.py: DOSSIER L27; frozen slots dataclass EntreeDuCatalogue(slug, genre, version, role, resume, document, client, portee, skills, outputs, tools, human_gate_after, tags) L30-51; property empreinte L53-57; _contexte L60; _lire L64; @cache entrees L91; entree L98; skills_du_catalogue L102; fichiers_de_la_skill L106. <!-- lang: fr-ok -->
- evals.py.
- _contexte.md is prepended to every internal agent's instructions, so it is part of their document and digest: rename the file, never its content.
- clients/*.yaml (6) have French YAML comments. Comments never enter the parsed document, so translating them is safe.
- `python -m choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents.evals --all`: Makefile:336, .github/workflows/nightly.yml:98.
