- By-path readers: tests/langue/test_anglais.py YAML_LIVRES L80, tests/langue/test_la_porte_mcp.py LIEUX L22, tests/procedures/test_coverage.py:99.
- Importers in apps/api, packages/playbooks and core tests keep the old path through the shim (rule h). dsl/flow.py:18 is S45-07's.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/migrate.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/admin_cli.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/agent_catalogue/ (git mv of the package, with _context.md)`, `packages/core/tests/test_agent_catalogue.py`, `packages/core/tests/test_agent_catalogue_evals.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_configuration_aliases.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/migrer.py (shim)`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/outils.py (shim)`, `apps/api/pyproject.toml`, `packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/plugin.py`, `packages/ontology/tests/test_plugin.py`, `compose.yaml`, `charts/choregos/charts/api/templates/migrations-job.yaml`, `tests/cluster/test_platform_on_cluster.py`, `tests/paquets/test_distribuables.py`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/nightly.yml`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/catalogue_d_agents/__init__.py (shim)`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/catalogue_d_agents/evals.py (shim)`, `tests/langue/test_anglais.py`, `tests/langue/test_la_porte_mcp.py`, `tests/procedures/test_coverage.py`, `docs/development.md`, `docs/deployment.md`, `docs/usage.md`, `docs/getting-started.md`, `docs/adr/0026-what-the-core-rls-covers.md`, `docs/adr/0036-a-template-ships-what-a-plugin-installs.md`, `docs/adr/0040-a-built-in-agent-catalogue.md`, `packages/core/tests/test_plugins.py`, `docs/plugins.md`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/flow.py`
- Delete: `packages/core/tests/test_catalogue_d_agents.py (git mv)`, `packages/core/tests/test_catalogue_evals.py (git mv)`

**Steps.**

1. ontology plugin.py:25: ACTIVATION = 'CHOREGOS_ONTOLOGY_TRIAL' and FORMER_ACTIVATION = 'CHOREGOS_ESSAI_ONTOLOGIE'  # lang: fr-ok alias:external. The plugin is active when either is '1'; log a deprecation warning when only the former is set.
2. ci.yml:236: set both CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_ON_POSTGRES and CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES to '1'; the former line carries `# lang: fr-ok alias:internal — conftest switches in S45-42`.
3. `git mv migrer.py migrate.py`, then translate it:
   - MIGRATIONS_GROUP and plugin_locations;
   - configuration(*, with_plugins: bool = True, avec_les_greffons: bool | None = None), where the former keyword is on its own fr-ok line and wins when given;
   - main is unchanged.
   Recreate migrer.py with:
   - SPDX line, then `# lang: fr-ok-file alias:external — python -m choregos_api.migrer, removed in 0.22.0`;
   - explicit imports, and `emplacements_des_greffons = plugin_locations`, `GROUPE_DE_MIGRATIONS = MIGRATIONS_GROUP`;
   - `if __name__ == '__main__': main()`.
   Point compose.yaml:158, migrations-job.yaml:92 and test_platform_on_cluster.py:120 at choregos_api.migrate. test_distribuables.py:155 expects both files in the wheel.
4. `git mv outils.py admin_cli.py` and translate it. Recreate outils.py as an alias:external shim (app and main, with a __main__ guard). Set the pyproject entry to `choregos-admin = 'choregos_api.admin_cli:main'`.
5. `git mv catalogue_d_agents agent_catalogue` and `git mv agent_catalogue/_contexte.md agent_catalogue/_context.md` (content unchanged). Translate __init__.py and evals.py:
   - FOLDER;
   - CatalogueEntry with fields kind, summary and reach, and read-only properties genre, resume and portee (alias:internal);
   - `digest` stays a @property, plus `empreinte = digest` on the class;
   - _read, _context, entries(), entry(), catalogue_skills(), skill_files(); internal calls go through module globals.
   At the end: typed former names (EntreeDuCatalogue, DOSSIER, entrees, entree, skills_du_catalogue, fichiers_de_la_skill), then alias_names(__name__, {those pairs}).
   If `git grep -n 'EntreeDuCatalogue('` shows keyword construction outside, apply rule (e).
   Translate the YAML comments of clients/*.yaml.
6. Recreate the catalogue_d_agents/ package:
   - __init__.py: header `# lang: fr-ok-file alias:external`; explicit English imports from choregos_core.agent_catalogue; typed former assignments; `replace_module(__name__, sys.modules['choregos_core.agent_catalogue'])`;
   - evals.py: `from choregos_core.agent_catalogue.evals import case_paths, evaluate, load_case, main` and `if __name__ == '__main__': raise SystemExit(main())`.
   At runtime the replaced package's __path__ is agent_catalogue/, so `python -m choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents.evals` runs the new evals. The test proves it.
   Point Makefile:336 and nightly.yml:98 at choregos_core.agent_catalogue.evals.
7. Update the by-path readers: test_anglais.py L80, test_la_porte_mcp.py L22 and test_coverage.py:99, which read data files that moved.
8. `git mv` the two core tests to test_agent_catalogue.py and test_agent_catalogue_evals.py and translate them. Before changing any code, run the old code once and paste the {slug: digest} dict into test_digests_did_not_move.
9. Docs: rename migrer, outils:main, catalogue_d_agents and CHOREGOS_ESSAI_ONTOLOGIE in docs/development.md (L407-408, L424, L501-510, plus the new test-suite variable), docs/deployment.md:642, docs/usage.md, docs/getting-started.md:146 and ADRs 0026:42, 0036:17 and 0040:7/21/60. Say that the former names work until 0.22.0.
10. Open the issue 'Remove the French entry points and variables in 0.22.0'.
11. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Owner's rule (c): the migrations that migrer.py collects belong to extensions (entry-point packages). In migrate.py:
  - `emplacements_des_greffons` becomes `extension_locations`, not plugin_locations;
  - the keyword is `configuration(*, with_extensions: bool = True, avec_les_greffons: bool | None = None)`, not with_plugins;
  - MIGRATIONS_GROUP stays as written;
  - the shim lines are `emplacements_des_greffons = extension_locations` and `GROUPE_DE_MIGRATIONS = MIGRATIONS_GROUP`;
  - test_migrate_configuration_accepts_the_former_keyword compares configuration(avec_les_greffons=False) with configuration(with_extensions=False);
  - translated messages say 'an extension's migrations'.
  In the ontology test, write 'activate the ontology extension'.
- Agent catalogue on main: S43 added `entry_of(brut)` at catalogue_d_agents/__init__.py L69. It builds the entry with keyword arguments (`genre=`, `resume=`, `portee=` at L79-93): rename those keywords to kind/summary/reach (rule e). apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/plugin_installer.py:41-42 imports `EntreeDuCatalogue, entry_of` from choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents and `evaluate` from catalogue_d_agents.evals, then calls `evaluate(suite, agent=...)` at L191. So:
  - keep the name `entry_of` in agent_catalogue;
  - list `entry_of` among the shim's explicit English imports, so mypy accepts the importer;
  - the evals shim must export `evaluate`.
  Current anchors: DOSSIER L27, EntreeDuCatalogue L30-57 (empreinte L53-57), _contexte L61, _lire L65, entry_of L69, @cache entrees L96-97, entree L104, skills_du_catalogue L108, fichiers_de_la_skill L112. Tests patch `entrees` at test_catalogue_d_agents.py:84,269, test_agent_tags.py:158 and test_agent_history.py:215. <!-- lang: fr-ok -->
- New by-path readers of the catalogue folder that the git mv breaks:
  - packages/core/tests/test_plugins.py:194-196: `CORE_CASES = .../choregos_core/catalogue_d_agents/eval_cases`. Point it at agent_catalogue/eval_cases; otherwise the parametrized test_the_eval_format_is_the_core_catalogues silently collects nothing.
  - docs/plugins.md:97 names `choregos_core/catalogue_d_agents/eval_cases/`.
  Update both. Do NOT edit packages/contracts/schemas/plugin-evals.schema.json:5 or ts/schemas.ts:158, which name `choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents.evals`: this is not a contract-change PR, and the name keeps working through the shim. List it under 'Findings filed' for S45-49.
- Anchors that moved:
  - docs/deployment.md: `python -m choregos_api.migrer` is at L733 (was 642);
  - tests/langue/test_anglais.py: the catalogue paths are at L79 and L83;
  - tests/langue/test_la_porte_mcp.py: L20;
  - docs/adr/0040: L10/L24/L63 (was 7/21/60);
  - apps/api/tests/conftest.py: reads CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES at L29-32.
  These are unchanged: compose.yaml:158, migrations-job.yaml:92 (plus the L88 comment naming `migrer`), test_platform_on_cluster.py:120, test_distribuables.py:155, apps/api/pyproject.toml:33, test_routeurs_nus.py:767, ontology service/plugin.py:25, getting-started.md:146, ADR 0026:42, ADR 0036:17, ci.yml:236, Makefile:336 and nightly.yml:98. docs/usage.md has no mention to update.
- S45-07 (wave 7) cannot use agent_catalogue, which this story creates in wave 11. So this story also updates the comment at packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/flow.py:18 (`catalogue_d_agents/_contexte.md` becomes `agent_catalogue/_context.md`), the only line of flow.py it touches. The two template tests that S45-07 leaves on `from choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents import entree` keep that import (rule h).

If it did not, proceed, and write under 'Findings filed' that the plugin-stream S45-09 must target choregos_core.agent_catalogue.

### S45-06 · contract(contracts): English names for the contracts package's Python helpers

**Wave** 5 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S45-02` · **Contract change** yes

**PR title:** `contract(contracts): English names for the contracts package's Python helpers (S45-06)`

**Goal.** Rename the contracts package's French Python helpers, keep aliases for them, and bring contracts.tsv to zero.

**Context.**

packages/contracts has about 10 French declarations in src and 18 in tests. The helpers to rename:
- empreinte_de_skill: 2 importers each in apps/api, packages/core, apps/orchestrator and packages/runner;
- packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/workflow.py: est_un_etat_de_production; PREFIXE_PRODUCTION (imported by packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/edition.py:27); effet_implicite L424 (used at L435 and by core dsl/edition.py:193); effet_de_la_transition L433 (imported by core dsl/exigences.py:28 and dsl/trains.py:16).

S45-02 put `# lang: fr-ok alias:external` on openapi.yaml's deprecated 'autre' enum line, so openapi.yaml has no finding.

ADR 0001: this is a contract-change PR. There is no OpenAPI, JSON Schema or TypeScript change.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (b) and (f), and §9.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/contracts/tests/test_contracts_in_english.py`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/__init__.py`, `packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/workflow.py`, `the module defining empreinte_de_skill (`git grep -n 'def empreinte_de_skill' packages/contracts`)`, `packages/contracts/tests/*.py with French`

**Steps.**

1. Rename the helpers:
   - empreinte_de_skill -> skill_digest;
   - est_un_etat_de_production -> is_production_state;
   - PREFIXE_PRODUCTION -> PRODUCTION_PREFIX;
   - effet_implicite -> implicit_effect;
   - effet_de_la_transition -> transition_effect.
   Keep every former name as a typed `Old = New  # lang: fr-ok alias:internal`, and list both names in __all__.
2. Translate every comment, docstring and local identifier in packages/contracts/src and packages/contracts/tests.
3. Create the scan test.
4. Run `--update-baseline --area contracts`. The file must hold only its header.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/contracts/tests/test_contracts_in_english.py :: test_contracts_have_no_french :: scan_tree(('packages/contracts/',)) returns no finding, including in openapi.yaml
- packages/contracts/tests/test_contracts_in_english.py :: test_former_helper_names_are_the_english_ones :: each of the five former names is its English object

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] contracts.tsv is empty below its header.
- [ ] The core, api, orchestrator and runner importers pass unchanged.

### S45-07 · refactor(core): the workflow DSL, gates, policy, instructions and presets in English

**Wave** 7 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-02`, `S45-06`, `S45-41`, `S47-11` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(core): the workflow DSL, gates, policy, instructions and presets in English (S45-07)`

**Goal.** Translate and rename every French identifier, comment, docstring and file name in core's DSL, gates, policy, instructions and presets, and in their 29 tests. Keep aliases for the names other packages import.

**Context.**

Owned source (packages/core/src/choregos_core/):
- dsl/: __init__, dates, edition (158 declarations), engine, exigences, flow, graph, legacy, parser, process, trains, validator, yamlsource;
- gates/__init__.py;
- instructions.py, policy.py, policy_ceiling.py, domain.py;
- presets/*.yaml (French comments).
core/__init__.py is NOT yours (S45-08). It keeps importing former names through your aliases.

Owned tests (packages/core/tests/), 29: test_action_de_transition, test_already_fixed, test_artifact_flow, test_attempts_ceiling, test_carte_de_demonstration, test_declared_outputs, test_dsl_validation, test_edition, test_engine, test_exigences, test_gate_hors_de_l_arbre, test_gates, test_github_software_delivery_template, test_governed_delivery_template, test_graph, test_graph_labels, test_job_actor, test_legacy_declarations, test_output_schema, test_policy, test_policy_ceiling, test_policy_ceiling_network, test_process, test_process_view_names_the_agent, test_spawn, test_tache_humaine, test_templates_du_deploiement, test_trains, test_workflow_budget.

Names imported outside core (they need aliases):
- dsl.exigences ORDRE and exigences;
- dsl.trains approbation_du_train, env_du_train (apps/orchestrator workflows/interpreter.py:36, apps/api routers/webhooks.py:15) and train_apres_fusion;
- dsl.dates DateIllisible;
- dsl.edition EditionRefusee;
- gates ACTION_REGLEE;
- instructions rendre_les_instructions and erreurs_des_instructions.

Rename table:
- exigences.py -> requirements.py: Requirement, requirements, ORDER, ROLES_IN_A_REPOSITORY, ROLES_IN_PRODUCTION;
- trains: TrainApproval, takes_the_train, train_env, train_approval, train_after_merge;
- dates: UnreadableDate, field_of, due_date;
- edition: EditRefused, plus every other French name;
- gates: ACTION_SETTLED;
- instructions: render_instructions, instruction_errors, SAMPLE_VARIABLES.

Test file renames: test_action_de_transition -> test_transition_action, test_carte_de_demonstration -> test_demo_map, test_exigences -> test_requirements, test_gate_hors_de_l_arbre -> test_gate_outside_the_tree, test_tache_humaine -> test_human_task, test_templates_du_deploiement -> test_deployment_templates.

Traps:
- Issue codes, DSL keywords, JSON keys and messages a client matches are contract: do not change them.
- test_carte_de_demonstration reads apps/web/src/mocks/dev-complex.json; keep that path.
- test_edition.py:392 and test_graph_labels.py:4 name web paths that S45-29 and S45-27 update later.
- dsl/flow.py:18 names catalogue_d_agents: write agent_catalogue.
- test_github_software_delivery_template.py and test_governed_delivery_template.py import catalogue_d_agents: use choregos_core.agent_catalogue (S45-05 has merged).

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9. Shims: SPDX line, the header, explicit imports and typed `Old = New` (never `from x import New as Old`).

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/requirements.py`, `packages/core/tests/test_dsl_in_english.py`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/*.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/gates/__init__.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/instructions.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/policy.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/policy_ceiling.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/domain.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/presets/*.yaml`, `the 29 owned tests`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv dsl/exigences.py dsl/requirements.py`, translate it, and recreate exigences.py as a shim (rule a).
2. Translate every other owned source file with the rename table. Add alias:internal lines for every name in the 'imported outside core' list (including env_du_train). Rename every other French name without an alias.
3. Translate prose: comments, docstrings, preset YAML comments and prose strings that are not matched as codes.
4. `git mv` the six French-named tests and translate all 29: test names, locals, docstrings and prose samples.
5. Switch the imports inside your files to the English names.
6. Create test_dsl_in_english.py.
7. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9. No owned file may remain in core.tsv.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Drop the two traps that assume S45-05 merged: S45-05 is in wave 11 and this story in wave 7, so choregos_core.agent_catalogue does not exist yet. Keep the comment at packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/flow.py:18 (`catalogue_d_agents/_contexte.md`; S45-05 updates it). Keep `from choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents import entree` at packages/core/tests/test_github_software_delivery_template.py:10 and test_governed_delivery_template.py:12. These are module paths, not French declarations: the ratchet's identifier pass does not count them.
- Owner's rule (c): packages/core/tests/test_gate_hors_de_l_arbre.py (becomes test_gate_outside_the_tree.py) talks about a 'greffon' (an entry-point package, `choregos.plugins`) at L3, L55, L72 and L80. Translate it as 'extension', never 'plugin', for example test_without_the_extension_the_workflow_is_invalid and test_with_the_extension_the_validator_accepts_it.
- No owned file changed on main between b3a7893 and 3156d12. All 29 owned tests and apps/web/src/mocks/dev-complex.json exist, and the outside importers listed in Context are unchanged (interpreter.py:36, webhooks.py:15, apps/api/tests/test_gabarit_livraison_logicielle.py:93).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/core/tests/test_dsl_in_english.py :: test_dsl_gates_policy_have_no_french :: scan_tree over the owned source paths and the 29 tests returns no finding
- packages/core/tests/test_dsl_in_english.py :: test_former_names_still_import :: exigences.exigences and ORDRE; trains.approbation_du_train, env_du_train and train_apres_fusion; dates.DateIllisible; gates.ACTION_REGLEE; instructions.rendre_les_instructions: each is the English object

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] The apps/api, apps/orchestrator and packages/playbooks suites pass unchanged, and mypy strict is green.
- [ ] The STATUS row says it proves the DSL, gates and policy are English, and does not prove their importers switched.
- [ ] make ci is green.

```bash
uv run python tools/check_english.py --all --area core

```

### S45-08 · refactor(core): access report, exit codes, MCP helpers, tool catalogue and the rest of core in English

**Wave** 12 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-04`, `S45-05`, `S45-06`, `S45-07`, `S47-08`, `S47-12`, `S47-19` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(core): access report, exit codes, MCP helpers, tool catalogue and the rest of core in English (S45-08)`

**Goal.** Translate and rename everything in core outside the DSL. Keep aliases, including member aliases, for names exported to six packages, and accept the catalogue YAML key `tools:` while keeping `outils:`.

**Context.**

Owned source (packages/core/src/choregos_core/): __init__.py (the only writer), acces.py, admission.py and secrets.py (the parts S45-04 left), catalogue.py, catalogues.py, cron.py, egress.py, errors.py, hosted.py, images.py, llm_guardrail.py, llm_spans.py, mcp.py, mcp_catalogue/, memory_bindings.py, memory_scope.py, models.py, network.py, outbound_proxy.py, prompt_loop.py, quotas.py (the remainder), redaction.py, sorties.py, telemetry.py, web_search.py.
skill_catalogue/bundled and injection.py are excluded from the scan; leave them.

Owned tests (24): test_acces, test_backend_enabled, test_catalogue, test_cron, test_docs_updated, test_egress, test_egress_network_bounds, test_injection, test_llm_guardrail, test_llm_spans, test_mcp_catalogue, test_memory_bindings, test_memory_scope, test_models_resolution, test_native_sidecars, test_network, test_numbers_measured, test_procedures_format, test_prompt_loop, test_redaction, test_secrets, test_skill_catalogue, test_telemetry, test_web_search_bounds.

Renames and their importers:
- acces.py -> access.py: Acces -> AccessLine (cible -> target, demandes -> requests, refus -> refusals, motifs -> reasons; refuse -> refused); RapportAcces -> AccessReport (acces -> lines, evenements -> events, deduites -> inferred, refuses -> refused_lines, par_nature -> by_nature); rapport_d_acces -> access_report (apps/api routers/runs.py). to_dict and former_dict outputs are unchanged (S45-02 contract).
- sorties.py -> exit_codes.py: explication -> explanation (adapters, runner).
- mcp.py: ERREUR_DE_SYNTAXE -> PARSE_ERROR, REQUETE_INVALIDE -> INVALID_REQUEST, METHODE_INCONNUE -> METHOD_NOT_FOUND, PARAMETRES_INVALIDES -> INVALID_PARAMS, ERREUR_INTERNE -> INTERNAL_ERROR, negocier -> negotiate, version_acceptable -> acceptable_version, erreur -> error, resultat_texte -> text_result (kwargs en_erreur -> is_error and structure -> structured; rule e), est_notification -> is_notification, defaut_du_message -> message_defect. Importers: apps/api, packages/runner, packages/tools-mcp.
- catalogue.py: AppelHttp -> HttpCall, AppelMcp -> McpCall, OutilCatalogue -> CatalogueTool (method ouvert_a -> open_to, keeping `ouvert_a = open_to`, rule d; apps/api/src/choregos_api/catalogue.py:53 calls it). Catalogue.outils -> tools: `Field(validation_alias=AliasChoices('tools', 'outils'))` with populate_by_name, plus `@property def outils` (rule d; catalogue.py:53 reads it). Also par_nom -> by_name, OutilInconnuError -> UnknownToolError, ArgumentRefuseError -> ArgumentRefusedError, valider_les_arguments -> validate_arguments, construire_requete -> build_request, charger_catalogue -> load_catalogue, GABARIT -> PLACEHOLDER.
- secrets.py: est_une_reference_du_magasin -> is_store_reference, ReferenceInvalide -> InvalidReference, VariableRefusee -> RefusedVariable, PATTERN_PAR_DEFAUT -> DEFAULT_PATTERN, verifier -> check, resoudre -> resolve.

The operators' YAML key `outils:` (demo/outils/catalogue.yaml:21, docs/deployment.md:434) is external.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9. Write an upgrade-notes fragment and do not edit the hot-spot docs: S45-34 switches the demo catalogue and its doc.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/access.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/exit_codes.py`, `packages/core/tests/test_core_in_english.py`, `docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/S45-08.md`
- Modify: `the owned core source files`, `the 24 owned tests`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv acces.py access.py` and `git mv sorties.py exit_codes.py`. Translate both and recreate the shims (rule a).
2. Apply the renames in mcp.py, catalogue.py and secrets.py. Add alias:internal lines for the exported names, and add the member aliases (rule d) and keyword aliases (rule e).
3. catalogue.py: implement the `tools`/`outils` key. Log a one-time warning that `outils:` is deprecated until 0.22.0. A list document still maps to tools.
4. __init__.py: export the English names of S45-07 and S45-08, and keep the former names as alias:internal.
5. Translate every other owned file. `git mv test_acces.py test_access.py` and translate all 24 tests.
6. Write the fragment: the catalogue key outils: -> tools:.
7. Create test_core_in_english.py. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/core/tests/test_core_in_english.py :: test_core_outside_the_dsl_has_no_french :: scan_tree over the owned paths returns no finding
- packages/core/tests/test_core_in_english.py :: test_former_names_still_import :: sorties.explication, mcp.METHODE_INCONNUE, catalogue.OutilCatalogue and choregos_core.rapport_d_acces are the English objects
- packages/core/tests/test_core_in_english.py :: test_former_members_still_read :: catalogue().outils is catalogue().tools, and a tool's ouvert_a(g) == open_to(g)
- packages/core/tests/test_catalogue.py :: test_catalogue_reads_tools_and_former_outils_key :: both YAML keys load the same tools
- packages/core/tests/test_access.py :: test_access_report_keys_unchanged :: AccessReport.to_dict keys equal the S45-02 contract

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] apps/api, packages/runner, packages/tools-mcp and packages/adapters pass unchanged, and demo/outils/catalogue.yaml still loads.
- [ ] The STATUS row says it proves core is English outside the DSL, and does not prove operators switched to `tools:`.
- [ ] make ci is green.

**Commands.**

```bash

uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools

```

- Do not change JSON-RPC codes, access-report output keys or the catalogue's English fields.
- Do not edit S45-07's files, skill_catalogue/bundled or injection.py.

### S45-10 · refactor(adapters): registry, plugin loader, trackers, executors, gateways and SCM in English

**Wave** 10 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-04`, `S45-41`, `S46-01`, `S46-05`, `S47-14` · **Contract change** no

**Goal.** Rename and translate the connector registry, the plugin loader, base types, trackers, executors, gateways and the CD, CI, SCM, HTTP and A2A adapters. Former fake globals and charger_les_greffons must keep following monkeypatching.

**Context.**

Owned source (packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/):
- __init__.py (1,288 lines), EXCEPT S46-01's MCP parts: the ('mcp','mcp') spec, FAUX_MCP and _client_du_faux_mcp (whatever names S46-01 gave them);
- registry.py (TYPES_PAR_DEFAUT), base.py (CAPACITES_EXECUTEUR), errors.py;
- tracker/ (adf, champs, github, github_events, gitlab, gitlab_events, interne, jira, jira_events);
- executor/, gateway/, cd/, ci/, scm/, github/, http/, a2a/.

Owned tests: _transport.py (Fil and sans_attente are imported by S45-09's test_ecphoria.py; keep the aliases), conftest.py, test_greffons.py, and test_a2a_card, test_a2a_client, test_aca, test_agent_sandbox, test_argocd, test_direct_et_interne, test_egress_transport, test_equipe_du_projet, test_gateway_capabilities, test_git_source_egress, test_github_auth_et_client, test_github_scm, test_github_tracker, test_gitlab, test_hosted_executors, test_image_builders, test_jira, test_k8s_job, test_local_docker, test_log_archive, test_openai_compatible_gateway, test_pod_security, test_rbac_du_job, test_registry_defaults, test_rest_client, test_routing_executor, test_run_gateway_key, test_simulated_executor, test_tekton, test_types_de_connecteurs.

Renames:
- GROUPE_DE_GREFFONS ('choregos.plugins', an English value) -> PLUGIN_GROUP;
- charger_les_greffons L1259 -> load_plugins. It is patched by apps/orchestrator/tests/test_worker_greffons.py; main.py:16/256 and orchestrator worker.py keep the former name (rule h).
- TYPES_PAR_DEFAUT -> DEFAULT_TYPES; CAPACITES_EXECUTEUR -> EXECUTOR_CAPABILITIES;
- tracker/interne.py -> tracker/internal.py; tracker/champs.py -> tracker/fields.py (ANCIENS_NOMS -> FORMER_NAMES, whose French board field names are compatibility data marked `lang: fr-ok`);
- FAUX_ENTRA, FAUX_MARKETDATA, FAUX_WEB_SEARCH -> FAKE_*. They are module globals patched by apps/api and tests/e2e/test_m6_rh.py:58-59.

alias_names composes with any call S46-01 made on this module.

By path: tests/langue/test_orchestrateur_en_anglais.py L118 names tracker/champs.py.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/tracker/internal.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/tracker/fields.py`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_registry_in_english.py`
- Modify: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py (non-MCP parts)`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/registry.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/base.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/errors.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/tracker/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/executor/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/gateway/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/cd/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/ci/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/scm/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/github/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/http/`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/a2a/`, `the owned tests`, `tests/langue/test_orchestrateur_en_anglais.py (L118 only)`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_extensions.py (git mv of test_greffons.py)`
- Delete: `packages/adapters/tests/test_greffons.py (git mv)`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv tracker/interne.py tracker/internal.py` and `git mv tracker/champs.py tracker/fields.py`, then shim both. Update the path at L118 of test_orchestrateur_en_anglais.py.
2. Apply the renames with typed aliases. At the end of __init__.py: `alias_names(__name__, {'FAUX_ENTRA': 'FAKE_ENTRA', 'FAUX_MARKETDATA': 'FAKE_MARKETDATA', 'FAUX_WEB_SEARCH': 'FAKE_WEB_SEARCH', 'charger_les_greffons': 'load_plugins', 'GROUPE_DE_GREFFONS': 'PLUGIN_GROUP'})`.
3. _transport.py: Fil -> Wire and sans_attente -> no_wait, with typed aliases (test_ecphoria imports them).
4. Translate every other owned file. Leave every line of S46-01's MCP parts untouched.
5. `git mv test_greffons.py test_plugins.py`. Rename test_direct_et_interne -> test_direct_and_internal, test_equipe_du_projet -> test_project_team, test_github_auth_et_client -> test_github_auth_and_client, test_rbac_du_job -> test_job_rbac, test_types_de_connecteurs -> test_connector_types. Translate all of them.
6. Create test_registry_in_english.py. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Owner's rule (c), ADR 0093 Decision 1: the loader loads extensions.
  - GROUPE_DE_GREFFONS becomes `EXTENSION_GROUP`. Its value 'choregos.plugins' stays: ADR 0093 keeps the entry-point group.
  - charger_les_greffons becomes `load_extensions`.
  - Use `alias_names(__name__, {..., 'charger_les_greffons': 'load_extensions', 'GROUPE_DE_GREFFONS': 'EXTENSION_GROUP'})`.
  - `git mv test_greffons.py test_extensions.py`, not test_plugins.py: 'plugin' now means the ADR 0093 package (packages/core/tests/test_plugins.py, adapters test_plugin_servers.py and test_plugin_tracker.py).
  - The test is test_patching_charger_les_greffons_patches_load_extensions.
  - PR title: `refactor(adapters): registry, extension loader, trackers, executors, gateways and SCM in English (S45-10)`.
- Anchors at 3156d12:
  - __init__.py is 1,332 lines: `'charger_les_greffons'` sits in `__all__` at L71, GROUPE_DE_GREFFONS at L1300, charger_les_greffons at L1303 (was 1259);
  - FAUX_MCP L741 (S46-01's: do not touch), FAUX_ENTRA L763, FAUX_METIER L783 (not in the rename list: leave it), FAUX_MARKETDATA L900, FAUX_WEB_SEARCH L913;

  - tests/langue/test_orchestrateur_en_anglais.py names tracker/champs.py at L123 (`TABLE_DE_COMPATIBILITE`, was L118) and in the comment at L33. Update both.
- The plugin stream added English code in owned files. Keep it as it is, and never change its stored identifiers:

### S45-12 · refactor(api): root modules, edition plugin API, Principal and configuration in English

**Wave** 12 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-05`, `S45-41`, `S47-01`, `S47-02`, `S47-03`, `S47-04`, `S47-12`, `S47-13`, `S47-14`, `S47-26`, `S46-07`, `S46-08` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(api): root modules, edition plugin API, Principal and configuration in English (S45-12)`

**Goal.** Translate and rename the API root modules: main, config, deps, security, limiter, metrics, edition and the rest. edition.py's plugin names and Principal.authentifie_le get external aliases until 0.22.0.

**Context.**

S47-API-GATE stands for every S47 story that edits apps/api security.py, limiteur.py, config.py, main.py, deps.py, rbac.py, edition.py, routers/auth.py, mcp/garde_fous.py, mcp/transport.py, a2a/door.py or db/models.py. They are written against today's French names and merge first.

Owned (apps/api/src/choregos_api/): __init__, adaptateurs, amorcage, audit, catalogue, config, deps, edition, errors, events, fraicheur, limiteur, local_demo, logging, main, metriques, object_store, rbac, secret_store, security, temporal, theme, trace_drift.
Not yours: db/, a2a/, egress/, knowledge/ (S45-48); greffons/plugins and effets/effects (S45-04); migrer/migrate and outils/admin_cli (S45-05).

Renames:
- adaptateurs.py -> adapter_wiring.py (brancher_memoire_lexicale -> attach_lexical_memory; orchestrator import);
- amorcage.py -> bootstrap.py;
- fraicheur.py -> freshness.py (age_de_l_authentification -> authentication_age; ontology import);
- limiteur.py -> rate_limit.py (Limiteur -> RateLimiter, with method admet -> admits keeping `admet = admits` per rule d, because mcp/transport.py and a2a/door.py call it; installer -> install; FENETRE_S -> WINDOW_S; tests import Limiteur);
- metriques.py -> metrics.py;
- catalogue.py: outils_du_projet -> project_tools (orchestrator), appeler -> call;
- deps.py: get_db_plateforme -> get_platform_db, jeton_rest -> is_rest_token, noter_l_usage -> record_usage, exiger_admin_de_plateforme -> require_platform_admin, _valider_la_session -> _validate_session (routers import these; rule b).

edition.py (plugin API, alias:external with removed_in): COMMUNITY, ENTERPRISE, declare(name, *, features=None, publisher, version, licence, fonctions=None), declare_group_mapper, group_mapper, current, features, is_enterprise, SessionRefused, declare_session_validation, session_validations, declare_platform_administrator, platform_administrators, reset. The docstring L4 links docs/adr/0024-two-editions.md.

rbac.py Principal (slots dataclass, L108-125): field authentifie_le -> authenticated_at, plus a property authentifie_le with getter AND setter (alias:external). deps.py:188 WRITES it; switch that line to authenticated_at. Readers in services/actions.py and agent_releases.py stay (rule h).

Logger names choregos.amorcage and choregos.metriques become choregos.bootstrap and choregos.metrics; they go in the fragment. Tests patch `fraicheur.time` (the global time module): unaffected. Use replace_module in the root-module shims anyway.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9. docs/development.md is a serialized hot spot this story may edit (the edition and Principal rows of 'Former names'). Do not edit docs/deployment.md or docs/usage.md.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/adapter_wiring.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/bootstrap.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/freshness.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/rate_limit.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/metrics.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_api_root_in_english.py`, `docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/S45-12.md`
- Modify: `the 23 owned root modules`, `docs/development.md`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv` the five modules, translate them, and recreate the shims with replace_module. main.py imports the English modules.
2. edition.py: translate it in place. Keep the typed former names `# lang: fr-ok alias:external` and add alias_names(__name__, {...}, removed_in=EXTERNAL_ALIAS_REMOVAL); `fonctions=` sits on its own line and warns.
3. rbac.py: rename the Principal field to authenticated_at and add the getter/setter property authentifie_le (alias:external). deps.py:188 writes authenticated_at.
4. Rename the deps.py and catalogue.py names with typed aliases. Translate every owned file: comments, docstrings, prose strings, and local and private names. Settings fields and environment variable names do not change.
5. docs/development.md: add the edition and Principal rows to 'Former names (removed in 0.22.0)'. Fragment rows: logger names, log events and keys renamed, `python -m` paths if any.
6. Create the scan test. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- deps.py now writes `principal.authentifie_le` at L189 (was 188); the Principal field is at rbac.py L125.
- Owner's rule (c): edition.py is the edition *extension* API (entry-point packages are 'extensions'; 'plugin' is the ADR 0093 package).
  - PR title: `refactor(api): root modules, edition extension API, Principal and configuration in English (S45-12)`.
  - The Context note 'greffons/plugins and effets/effects (S45-04)' becomes 'greffons/extensions and effets/effects (S45-04)'.
  - 'plugin-edition tests' means the extension-edition tests.
- Owned modules changed on main; keep their new behaviour when translating:
  - amorcage.py now finds the bootstrap organisation through `db.org_aliases.organisation_by_slug_or_alias` (ADR 0090);
  - deps.py `resolve_project` maps a former org slug through `db.org_aliases.current_slug`;
  - metriques.py skips cost lines whose project_id is null (S42-10e);
  - config.py (+39) and main.py (+20) carry the plugin, plugin-server and relay settings and wiring. Settings field and environment variable names stay unchanged.
  The new root modules org_alias_middleware.py, relay/ and scanners/ are English and not owned: leave them.
- New importers of former names keep working unchanged (rule h):
  - routers/projects.py calls `exiger_admin_de_plateforme`;
  - apps/api/tests/test_org_slug_aliases.py imports `choregos_api.amorcage.amorcer` and `choregos_api.edition.declarer_un_administrateur_de_plateforme, reinitialiser`.
  The amorcage shim must therefore still export `amorcer`, and edition must keep both names through its alias:external map.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_api_root_in_english.py :: test_api_root_has_no_french :: scan_tree over the owned files returns no finding
- apps/api/tests/test_api_root_in_english.py :: test_former_root_modules_still_import :: limiteur.Limiteur is rate_limit.RateLimiter; importlib.import_module('choregos_api.fraicheur') is choregos_api.freshness; the same holds for metriques, amorcage and adaptateurs

- apps/api/tests/test_api_root_in_english.py :: test_principal_former_attribute_reads_and_writes :: p.authentifie_le = 5 sets p.authenticated_at to 5, and reading it gives 5
- apps/api/tests/test_authentification_fraiche.py :: (existing) :: passes unchanged

### S45-14 · refactor(api): the inbound MCP door in English

**Wave** 13 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S45-03`, `S45-04`, `S45-12` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(api): the inbound MCP door in English (S45-14)`

**Goal.** Rename and translate apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/ (171 French declarations) and its three dedicated tests. Tool names, error codes and persisted audit keys do not change.

**Context.**

apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/ is the inbound door (ADR 0030), not S46's outbound path.

Renames:
- appelant.py -> caller.py: Appelant -> Caller; jeton -> token; portees -> scopes; ecrit -> writes; cle -> key, with a `cle` property (rule d) because a2a/door.py and a2a/push.py read it; motifs -> reasons, with a property; identifier -> identify; incarner_l_agent -> act_as_agent; PORTEES_MCP -> MCP_SCOPES.
- garde_fous.py -> limits.py: APPELS_PAR_MINUTE -> CALLS_PER_MINUTE, ECRITURES_PAR_JOUR -> WRITES_PER_DAY, TAILLE_MAX -> MAX_SIZE, ecritures_du_jour -> writes_today, LIMITEUR -> RATE_LIMITER, tronquer -> truncate. L44 already dual-reads 'ecriture' since S45-03.
- outils.py -> tools.py: Outil.ecriture -> write; _outils_des_greffons -> _plugin_tools (not monkeypatched here; the patched one is in routers/internal.py, S45-16).
- transport.py: _servir -> _serve, _repondre -> _answer, _auditer -> _audit. L126 `LIMITEUR.admet(appelant.cle)` becomes `RATE_LIMITER.admits(caller.key)`. It writes the audit keys jeton= and ecriture=, and issue 'refus' at L210; those stay until S45-39.
- oauth.py.

Owned tests: apps/api/tests/test_mcp_porte.py (L338 patches transport.LIMITEUR), test_mcp_oauth.py and test_jetons_a_portee.py.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/caller.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/limits.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/tools.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_mcp_door_in_english.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/*.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_mcp_porte.py (git mv)`, `apps/api/tests/test_mcp_oauth.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_jetons_a_portee.py (git mv)`

**Steps.**

1. `git mv` appelant.py, garde_fous.py and outils.py, translate them, and add the shims. They keep LIMITEUR, ECRITURES_PAR_JOUR and ecritures_du_jour for a2a/door.py until S45-48.
2. Translate transport.py and oauth.py. Keep jeton=, ecriture= and 'refus' with `# lang: fr-ok — persisted audit key, S45-39`.
3. `git mv` test_mcp_porte.py -> test_mcp_door.py and test_jetons_a_portee.py -> test_scoped_tokens.py, and translate them and test_mcp_oauth.py. test_mcp_door.py patches transport.RATE_LIMITER.
4. Create the scan test. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Rule (c), extensions vocabulary. In mcp/outils.py (mcp/tools.py after the move): L593 `_outils_des_greffons` -> `_extension_tools`, not `_plugin_tools`. L578 `_executeur_de_greffon` -> `_extension_executor`. L29 `from ..greffons import fournisseurs_humains` -> `from ..extensions import human_tool_providers`: under rule (c), S45-04's module is choregos_api.extensions, not choregos_api.plugins; take the exact names from docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/S45-04.md. Translate every 'greffon' in the door's prose (8 lines in outils.py) as 'extension', never 'plugin': 'plugin' now means an ADR 0093 package (choregos_core.plugins, services/plugin_*.py, routers/plugins.py).
- Your renamed modules must keep a typed alias (rule b) for every name imported from outside your files. a2a/door.py L64-67 imports SCOPES_A2A, Appelant, Refus and identifier from mcp.appelant; ECRITURES_PAR_JOUR, LIMITEUR and ecritures_du_jour from mcp.garde_fous; emetteur from mcp.oauth; and TAILLE_MAX_DU_CORPS, _origines_admises and _refus_http from mcp.transport. a2a/push.py L40 imports SCOPES_A2A, Refus and caller_of_token. apps/api/tests/test_a2a_inbound.py:401 imports transport.TAILLE_MAX_DU_CORPS. test_agents_externes.py:148 imports Appelant and incarner_l_agent. mcp/oauth.py `emetteur` (L33) is also imported by routers/integrations.py:11 and routers/platform_identity.py:16, which are S45-15's in the same wave 13. If you rename it, keep `emetteur = <new>  # lang: fr-ok alias:internal`.

**Steps.**

1. `git mv` the seven modules, translate them, and add the shims.
2. Translate actions, agents, agent_lifecycle, definitions and installation in place, with aliases. Add alias_names(__name__, {'derniere_version': 'latest_version'}) in definitions.py.
3. Point the routers/agents.py import lines at services.agent_catalogue.
4. Fragment: the log event gabarit.extension_sans_greffon -> its English name.
5. Create the scan test. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- services/actions.py reads `principal.authentifie_le` at L321, not L320. services/installation.py has `"sans_greffon"` at L51 and L172 (not L170), and the log event at L168.
- Rule (c): rename the log event `gabarit.extension_sans_greffon` to `template.extension_without_installer`, never a 'plugin' wording. The audit `reason="aucun greffon actif ne l'installe"` at L171 is display text, not a key: translate it (e.g. 'no active extension installs it'). The 'sans_greffon' key stays French. Switch `from ..greffons import installateurs_de_gabarit` (installation.py L162) to S45-04's `..extensions` module and its English name. Translate 'greffon' prose (definitions.py L55/L268, gabarits.py L51, actions.py L363) as 'extension'.
- gabarits.py (-> templates.py) changed on main. It resolves `plugin:<plugin>/<template>` refs through `.plugin_installer.plugin_template` (ADR 0093). `manifeste_du_gabarit(session, template_ref, org_id=None)` gained `org_id`, and it now uses choregos_core.paths (inside, files_under, symlinks_under). installation.py marks agents from `("catalogue:", "plugin:")` as `scope="platform"` and writes the label for `source == "plugin"`. These 'plugin' values are ADR 0093 values: keep them verbatim. connecteurs.py (-> connectors.py) gained `declared_operation` and `undeclared` (English), and `human_floor` now handles plugin servers. `politique_pour_le_projet` is French: rename it with an alias.
- Main added importers that rely on your shims and aliases. Do not edit them; mypy strict must stay green: services/plugin_installer.py L69-71 (`from .agents import empreinte, erreurs_d_une_version`, `from .catalogue_d_agents import DIVERGE`, `from .installation import installer_skills_et_agents`); services/plugin_tracker.py L116 (`from .gabarits import manifeste_du_gabarit`); knowledge/distill.py L822 and apps/api/tests/test_knowledge_distillation_ops.py:128 (`services.couts.record_cost`); apps/api/tests/test_plugin_servers.py L165/L190/L225 (`services.connecteurs.human_floor / declared_operation / politique_pour_le_projet`). The services/catalogue_d_agents.py shim must re-export DIVERGE; the couts shim must re-export record_cost. packages/core/src/choregos_core/paths.py:4 names services.gabarits in a docstring: not yours.

- apps/api/tests/test_services_a_in_english.py :: test_former_service_names_still_import :: services.agents.AgentIndisponible is AgentUnavailable; services.gabarits.livraison is services.templates.delivery; services.evenements.persist_event is services.events.persist_event

- apps/api/tests/test_workflows_multiples.py :: (existing patch of derniere_version) :: still green

### S45-22 · refactor(orchestrator): workflows, queue routing, worker and configuration in English, replay-proven

**Wave** 4 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-03`, `S45-41`, `S45-43` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(orchestrator): workflows, queue routing, worker and configuration in English, replay-proven (S45-22)`

**Goal.** Rename and translate the orchestrator's workflow code and root modules without changing any recorded wire name, and prove it by replaying every history.

**Context.**

Owned (apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/):
- __init__.py;
- workflows/*.py (interpreter, train, action, planification, memory, reconciliation, trial, provisioning, findings, evals, knowledge, knowledge_distill, agent_build, agent_session, activity_names, __init__);
- repartition.py, worker.py, config.py, gitops.py, local.py, markdown.py, demo.py, testing.py, train_client.py, context_reduction.py, org_egress.py.

Frozen:
- the six patch id VALUES (interpreter.py L76/81/86/90, action.py:29, train.py:33), in the line shape `NAME = "<id>"  # lang: fr-ok...` that tests/replay/test_english_names.py L33-50 needs;
- workflow type names;
- signal and query method names without name= (for example `def preuve` in action.py, marked fr-ok: signal wire name);
- ENGLISH_TO_FRENCH and named() in activity_names.py;
- wire_names.py;
- TrainInput.seuil_de_relais.

Renames:
- planification.py -> scheduling.py: executer_activite -> execute_on_its_queue and demarrer_activite -> start_on_its_queue. All of workflows/* import them; apps/orchestrator/tests/test_repartition_des_files.py:119-130 uses planification.executer_activite through the shim.
- repartition.py -> queues.py: ACTIVITES_PAR_FILE -> ACTIVITIES_BY_QUEUE, FILE_PAR_ACTIVITE -> QUEUE_BY_ACTIVITY, nom_de_l_activite -> activity_name (tests/replay/test_english_names.py:58), file_de -> queue_of.
- config.py: url_du_sidecar_d_outils -> tools_sidecar_url (property), keeping the property alias (rule d) for activities/stage.py:110.
- workflows/action.py:32 PreuveManquante -> MissingEvidence.
- workflows/train.py: SEUIL_DE_RELAIS -> RELAY_THRESHOLD and SEUIL_DE_RELAIS_D_AVANT -> FORMER_RELAY_THRESHOLD. apps/orchestrator/tests/test_train_se_relaie_tot.py (S45-25's) patches them with setattr in 4 lines: edit those lines. Never use alias_names in workflows/.
- Workflow instance attributes (self.etat, self.faits, self.preuves, self.attente) are not serialized; query outputs keep their keys.
- Comments in config.py L45/L106 name outils_locaux: write local_tools.

Rules: ADR 0098 §3 (a)-(h) and §9. Use English names of other stories where they exist (rule h).

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/scheduling.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/queues.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_workflows_in_english.py`
- Modify: `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/__init__.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/*.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/repartition.py (shim)`, `the owned root modules`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_train_se_relaie_tot.py (4 setattr lines only)`

**Steps.**

1. Record the set of (workflow type, signal or query name) pairs first.
2. `git mv` planification.py and repartition.py, translate them, and add the shims. Every workflow imports .scheduling.
3. Translate every owned file. Keep frozen values and wire names with fr-ok and a reason. Edit the 4 setattr lines.
4. Run `uv run pytest -q tests/replay` after each workflow file. Any non-determinism means a wire name or the command order changed: revert that change.
5. Create the test. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

There is no new patch id and nothing new to freeze. tests/replay/histories/knowledge-distill-S42-10.json must still replay. Owned:

- apps/web/src/mocks/** except parcours.ts (data.ts, 3,464 lines, is a hot spot);
- apps/web/src/types/** and src/proxy.ts;
- tests whose first `@/` import is `@/lib/` or `@/mocks/`, plus tests/api-erreurs.test.ts, jetons.ts, mocks-en-anglais.test.ts, couleurs-utilitaires.test.ts, grilles-tailwind.test.ts and theme-contraste.test.ts.

French-named lib files: activite-du-run.ts -> run-activity.ts, sortes-de-connecteurs.ts -> connector-kinds.ts, plan-de-l-agent.ts -> agent-plan.ts.

User-visible strings are already English: change only identifiers, comments and test titles.

Keep these paths: src/lib/policy-ceiling-form.ts, web-search-declared.json and integrations.ts (read by tests/docs and tests/procedures); src/mocks/mcp-catalogue.json, procedures.json and dev-complex.json (generated or read by path). Keep data-testid values (S45-29). data.ts:2412's essai comment is S45-32's.

TS shim format: `// lang: fr-ok-file alias:internal — former path of ./run-activity, removed by S45-29`, then `export * from './run-activity';`, plus `export { default } from './run-activity';` when the module has a default export. Renamed exported symbols imported elsewhere keep `export const old = neu; // lang: fr-ok alias:internal` (or `export type Old = New;`).

The stale comment at apps/api/src/choregos_api/theme.py:13 is S45-45's.

Run this only while no S46 or S47 PR touching api.ts, types.ts or data.ts is open. Baselines: ADR 0098 §9.

- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/**`, `apps/web/src/mocks/** (except parcours.ts)`, `apps/web/src/types/**`, `apps/web/src/proxy.ts`, `the owned apps/web/tests files`

4. Run make web-ci. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

Main's web changes in lib/ and mocks/ are English with no new files (lib still has 48 files; data.ts 3,464 lines; essai comment at data.ts:2412). Nothing else changes.

- apps/web/tests/lib-shims.test.ts :: former lib paths still export the new modules :: import * from '@/lib/activite-du-run' has the same exports as '@/lib/run-activity'

Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- charts/choregos/templates/demo-fakes.yaml: `CONNECTOR_DEMO_JETON` is on L53 (not L52). L6 (garde.yaml comment) and L43 (fakes.serveur) are unchanged.
- docs/deployment.md: the `mountPath: /etc/choregos/outils` example is now on L427 (not L422); the file grew by about 100 lines with the plugins sections. Put 'Renamed in 0.21' after the existing sections. Leave the plugin sections (about L640-720) as they are.

Do not rename them or list them in 'Renamed in 0.21'. Their namespace `choregos-plugins` matches pod-security's `choregos-*`, so the upgrade note on renamed pod-security rules (`pas-de-hostpath` -> `no-hostpath`) must also warn about PolicyExceptions written for `choregos-plugins`.
- values.yaml L57-63 comments: 'a plugin shipped inactive' means an entry-point package (the ontology). Per rule (c), write 'an extension shipped inactive' (ADR 0093 Decision 1), and keep `CHOREGOS_ONTOLOGY_TRIAL`.
- Anchors re-checked and unchanged: _helpers.tpl L333-367, monitoring.yaml L17/47/49, migrations-job.yaml:66, values.yaml:110, api_test.yaml:85, operabilite_test.yaml L11/105/115/124, the Kyverno rule lines, tests/charts/test_kyverno_engine_runs.py:34, test_policies_cover_init_containers.py:32 and tests/cluster/test_node_loss.py:251.

**Context.**

Activities are not replayed, so these need no patch.

### S45-41 · feat(core): choregos_core.compat, two-way aliases for renamed module names

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S45-01` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(core): choregos_core.compat, two-way aliases for renamed module names (S45-41)`

**Goal.** Give every later rename one composable way to keep a former name. The former name must work under strict mypy, follow monkeypatching in both directions, and warn when it is external.

**Context.**

Tests monkeypatch module globals by their French names and expect the module's own code to see the patch:
- choregos_core.catalogue_d_agents.entrees (apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_d_agents.py:84,269; test_agent_tags.py:158; test_agent_history.py:216);
- choregos_adapters.FAUX_ENTRA, FAUX_MARKETDATA, FAUX_WEB_SEARCH, FAUX_MCP and charger_les_greffons;
- services.tickets.tracker_du_projet and services.definitions.derniere_version;
- greffons._INSTALLATEURS;
- routers.internal._outils_des_greffons;
- mcp.transport.LIMITEUR and a2a.door.LIMITEUR.

A plain `Old = New` does not forward a patch. A module `__getattr__` returns Any, which strict mypy rejects when it is returned (warn_return_any, pyproject.toml [tool.mypy] strict).

The mechanism:
- The module source keeps a typed `Old = New`, for mypy and readers.
- At runtime the module's class becomes a ModuleType subclass with one property per former name; the property reads and writes the English name in the module __dict__.
- Data descriptors on the type win over the instance dict; `from m import Old` goes through getattr; monkeypatch's setattr and undo go through the property.
- Several stories call alias_names on the same module (S45-10, and possibly S46-01, on choregos_adapters), so a second call must extend the existing class.

Warnings: pyproject.toml has filterwarnings=['ignore::DeprecationWarning'], and Python ignores DeprecationWarning outside __main__, so a warning never breaks a run. The version is 0.20.5 and aliases ship in 0.21.x, so external aliases end in 0.22.0.

Never use alias_names in a module under apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/: the Temporal sandbox re-imports those.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/compat.py`, `packages/core/tests/test_compat.py`
- Modify: `docs/development.md`

**Steps.**

1. compat.py: `EXTERNAL_ALIAS_REMOVAL = '0.22.0'`.
   `def alias_names(module: str, aliases: Mapping[str, str], *, removed_in: str | None = None) -> None`:
   - mod = sys.modules[module]. If type(mod) does not carry the marker `__choregos_aliases__`, create `type(f'_Aliased_{module}', (type(mod),), {'__choregos_aliases__': {}})` and assign it to mod.__class__. Otherwise reuse the existing class.
   - For each old -> new: raise ValueError if old == new, if new is not in mod.__dict__, or if old is already aliased to another name.
   - Set on the class `property(fget, fset, fdel)`:
     - fget warns `DeprecationWarning(f'{module}.{old} is deprecated: use {new} (removed in {removed_in}, ADR 0098)')` with stacklevel=2 when removed_in is set, then returns self.__dict__[new];
     - fset sets self.__dict__[new];
     - fdel deletes self.__dict__[new].
   - Record the pair in the marker dict.
2. `def replace_module(former: str, current: ModuleType) -> None: sys.modules[former] = current`. The docstring explains that a shim calls it at its end, so `import former` and `from pkg import former` both give the current module.
3. Module docstring: the pattern. A typed `Old = New  # lang: fr-ok alias:internal` stays in the source, then one alias_names call at the end of the module. Never use a module `__getattr__`. Never use either helper in a workflows/ module.
4. docs/development.md: add the subsection 'Compatibility aliases' under 'The English ratchet', with a six-line example.
5. Run `--all` and lower any stale area.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_former_name_reads_the_english_object :: on a synthetic module, mod.old is mod.new, with no warning when removed_in is None (warnings set to error)
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_external_alias_warns_with_its_window :: removed_in='0.22.0' makes reading old raise a DeprecationWarning matching 'use new.*0.22.0'
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_patching_the_former_name_patches_the_english_one :: monkeypatch.setattr(mod, 'old', x) makes mod.new is x; undo restores both
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_patching_the_english_name_is_seen_through_the_former_one ::
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_from_import_of_a_former_name_gets_the_current_object :: a real module file in tmp_path, after patching new, `from m import old` returns the patch
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_two_calls_compose :: two calls with different pairs both work, and type(mod) is the same class after the second
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_alias_to_a_missing_name_is_refused :: ValueError
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_replace_module_makes_the_former_import_the_new_module :: importlib.import_module(former) is current
- packages/core/tests/test_compat.py :: test_unknown_name_still_raises_attribute_error ::

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] compat.py is typed and passes strict mypy, with no module __getattr__.
- [ ] The STATUS row says it proves both directions and composition, and does not prove any rename (later stories).
- [ ] make ci is green.

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest -q packages/core/tests/test_compat.py
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py --all
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not rename anything else.
- Do not install aliases on any real module in this PR.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/development.md`

**Wave** 23 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-08`, `S45-09`, `S45-11`, `S45-12`, `S45-13`, `S45-14`, `S45-15`, `S45-16`, `S45-17`, `S45-18`, `S45-22`, `S45-23`, `S45-25`, `S45-31`, `S45-32`, `S45-33`, `S45-34`, `S45-38`, `S45-48`, `S47-37`, `S46-17` · **Contract change** no

- docs/deployment.md L477 (outils_locaux), L971 (test_image_par_composant), L976, L1089, L1118 (garde.yaml);

- docs/development.md:243.

docs residue: the English ADRs and pages with French lines (docs/adr/0048, 0055, 0078, 0061, 0084, 0083, 0079, ... see `--report --area docs`). Proper names and quoted French samples keep `lang: fr-ok`.

2. Apply the mapping to docs mentions as stated in context.
3. Translate the remaining docs findings.
4. Create the guard test with an explicit HISTORICAL allowlist (for example 'renamed from ...' quotes in ADRs), each entry with its reason.

### S45-45 · chore(repo): residue in unowned areas and the lexicon's blind spots swept; TS members join the ratchet

**Wave** 23 · **Priority** P2 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-21`, `S45-25`, `S45-29`, `S45-31`, `S45-34`, `S45-38` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `chore(repo): residue in unowned areas and the lexicon's blind spots swept; TS members join the ratchet (S45-45)`

**Goal.** Translate the French that no package story owns, and find the French the lexicon cannot see:
- single-weak-word paths;
- TypeScript interface members and parameters;
- stale comments naming renamed files.
Then extend the identifier pass so these blind spots stay closed.

**Context.**

Unowned residue at b3a7893:
- packages/ontology src and tests: about 15 French prose lines (cel.py, compiler.py, errors.py, loader.py, mcpgen.py, model.py, observations.py, service/objects.py, store.py, tools.py, validator.py);
- packages/playbooks (1);
- tools/hosted_dockerfile.py and tools/rename_tenant_names.yaml comments (tenant data stays);
- templates/github-tekton-argo-k8s/tekton/agent-stage.yaml and templates/software-delivery-governed/manifest.yaml (comments only; template identifiers are checked by tests/language/test_template_identifiers.py);
- .github/dependabot.yml and the French line in .github/workflows/ci.yml;
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/theme.py:13 (names theme-contraste.test.ts).

Comments naming renamed files across code: garde.yaml in packages/adapters __init__.py:147,1269 and tests, outils_locaux in packages/adapters/tests/test_k8s_job.py L361-374, and others. Find them with `git grep -n` of each old name from the S45-44 mapping.

Blind spots:
- `git ls-files | grep -E '(^|[/_.-])(de|du|des|d|l|la|le|les|au|aux|sans|par|pour|sur|et|un|une)([/_.-])'` (the rule needs two weak words, so test_x_de_y escapes);
- word-order French with English words (docs/runbooks/rotation-secrets.md);
- TypeScript interface and type members and function parameters.
Every match is renamed (with a shim or redirect where something external links to it) or added to FROZEN_PATHS with a reason.

This story is the single writer this wave of tools/check_english.py and ci.yml. Baselines: ADR 0098 §9.

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/language/test_blind_spots.py`
- Modify: `packages/ontology/**`, `packages/playbooks/**`, `tools/*.py`, `tools/rename_tenant_names.yaml (comments only)`, `templates/** (comments only)`, `.github/dependabot.yml`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml (the French line only)`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/theme.py (L13)`, `files whose comments name a renamed file (found by grep)`, `apps/web/src/** (TS member and parameter renames found by the new pass)`, `tools/check_english.py`, `tests/language/test_english_baseline.py`

**Steps.**

1. Translate the unowned residue listed in context.
2. Update every comment naming a renamed file.
3. Run the path grep and the word-order review; rename each match or add it to FROZEN_PATHS with a reason (list every decision in the PR).
4. tools/check_english.py: extend ts_declarations to read interface and type members (`name?:` inside interface or type bodies) and function or arrow parameters, with comments and strings still blanked. Rename what it finds in apps/web (keeping data-testid and API field names, which are the contract), then lower web.tsv.
5. Create the tests. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

- packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py: the `garde.yaml` comments are now on L148 and L1313 (not L147 and L1269). The test_k8s_job.py `outils_locaux` comments are still at L361-374, and theme.py:13 is unchanged.
- The blind-spot path grep matches no new path on main (compared with b3a7893). Main added no tools/*.py and no templates changes.
- 'S44-owned files' and 'the S44 track' mean this plan's MCP-gateway stream (renumbered S46), not main's S44 (kernel confinement, SkillSpector, code-graph trial).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/language/test_blind_spots.py :: test_no_single_weak_word_french_path :: every tracked path matching the grep in context is in FROZEN_PATHS

### S45-46 · refactor(apps): internal aliases and shims defined under apps/ removed

**Wave** 25 · **Priority** P2 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S45-40`, `S47-29`, `S47-30`, `S47-31`, `S47-32`, `S47-33` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(apps): internal aliases and shims defined under apps/ removed (S45-46)`

**Goal.** Delete every `alias:internal` alias line and every internal shim defined under apps/api and apps/orchestrator, after switching every remaining user. This includes main.py's and worker.py's charger_les_greffons.

**Context.**

Find them with:
- `git grep -n 'alias:internal' -- apps`;
- `git grep -ln 'lang: fr-ok-file alias:internal' -- apps`.

Internal users still on former names (rule h), for example: main.py:16/256 and orchestrator worker.py (charger_les_greffons -> load_plugins), a2a and push readers, routers on deps.py former names, orchestrator imports of services.Naissance.

Keep:
- alias:external names: plugins, effects, edition, Principal.authentifie_le, migrer, outils, catalogue_d_agents and the other external entry points;
- the frozen lines in wire_names.py, activity_names.py and the persisted keys.

conftest.py's SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES fallback is removed by S45-47 together with ci.yml.

Split by app if more than 40 files change. Baselines: ADR 0098 §9.

**Files.**

- Modify: `every file under apps/ with an alias:internal line`, `every user of a removed former name`
- Delete: `every apps/ module whose header holds 'lang: fr-ok-file alias:internal'`

**Steps.**

1. Same procedure as S45-40, for aliases defined under apps/.
2. Run tests/replay: a workflow module's import change must not change determinism.
3. Baselines: follow ADR 0098 §9.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Rule (c): `charger_les_greffons` has the English name `load_extensions`, not load_plugins. It is defined under packages/ (adapters __init__.py), so S45-40 already switched its users and removed its alias: main.py:16,265 (not 256), orchestrator worker.py:110,117, and also orchestrator activities/base.py:15,35, which the story text did not name. Here, only verify that none remain.
- Rule (c), alias:external names to keep: the module `choregos_api.greffons` now forwards to `choregos_api.extensions`, not `choregos_api.plugins`. Read 'plugins' in the Keep list as the greffons -> extensions window. Leave main's ADR 0093 modules alone (choregos_api.routers.plugins, schemas.plugins, services.plugin_*): they are not aliases.
- Main's plugin stream added internal users of French apps/ names; switch them before deleting the aliases. services/plugin_installer.py:69-71 uses `agents.empreinte`, `erreurs_d_une_version`, `catalogue_d_agents.DIVERGE` and `installation.installer_skills_et_agents`, and L75-76 `skills.SkillRefusee`, `valider` and `empreinte`. apps/api/src/choregos_api/db/org_aliases.py:24 uses `db.session.TOUT` and `limiter_aux_organisations`. apps/api/src/choregos_api/effets.py now calls services/plugin_servers.connector_client and services/connecteurs.declared_operation.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/language/test_english_packages.py :: test_no_internal_alias_remains :: `git grep -n 'alias:internal'` matches only conftest.py's SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES line (removed by S45-47)
- tests/replay/test_replay.py :: (existing) ::

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Only alias:external and frozen French names remain.
- [ ] The STATUS row says what it proves and what it does not.
- [ ] make ci is green.

**Commands.**

```bash
git grep -n 'alias:internal'
uv run pytest -q tests/replay apps

```

### S45-49 · chore(repo): external French aliases removed once their 0.22.0 window closed

**Wave** 27 · **Priority** P2 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S45-47`, `S45-39` · **Contract change** yes

**PR title:** `chore(repo): external French aliases removed once their 0.22.0 window closed (S45-49)`

**Goal.** Remove every `alias:external` name whose removal release is 0.22.0 or earlier, after 0.22.0 is released: French plugin-API names, env-var and settings aliases, old entry points, deprecated contract fields, legacy alert labels, the `?jeton=` webhook alias, runbook redirect stubs. Temporal patch ids and French activity/signal wire aliases stay forever.

**Context.**

S45-47 created the tracking table of external aliases (name, kind, story, removal release, removal issue). Mechanisms: `choregos_core.compat` aliases with a deprecation warning (S45-41/S45-04), pydantic `AliasChoices` in apps/api config (S45-05, S45-12; S46-01 for the broker wait setting), shims for `python -m choregos_api.migrer` / `choregos_api.outils:main` (S45-05), deprecated OpenAPI fields (S45-02), `legacy_alertname` labels and runbook stubs (S45-33). Contract fields change only in a contract-change PR (this story is one).

**Files.**

- Modify: `the files listed in S45-47's tracking table for rows with removal release <= 0.22.0`, `packages/contracts/openapi.yaml`, `packages/contracts/ts/`, `docs/deployment.md`

**Steps.**

1. Check that 0.22.0 is tagged (`git fetch --tags && git tag -l v0.22.0`); otherwise stop.
2. For each tracking-table row with removal release <= 0.22.0: delete the alias; turn its 'old name warns' test into an 'old name is gone' test (ImportError, unknown env var ignored with no effect, 404/422 for the old field or parameter); close the removal issue in the PR body.
3. Remove the deprecated contract fields and the `?jeton=` alias, run `make contracts`, commit `packages/contracts/ts/`.
4. Remove `legacy_alertname` labels and the runbook redirect stubs; keep Grafana uids (frozen).
5. Add an 'Upgrading to 0.23' section in docs/deployment.md listing every removed name and its replacement.
6. Update the tracking table: removed rows deleted; rows of aliases first shipped after 0.21.0 keep their 0.23.0 window.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1, owner's rule): a `choregos.plugins` entry-point package is an **extension**; 'plugin' means the ADR 0093 package. Every rename target of this story that would say plugin(s) for an entry-point package says extension(s) (`choregos_api.extensions`, `load_extensions`, `EXTENSION_GROUP`, `_extension_tools`, `extensionSections`); the entry-point group value `choregos.plugins` and persisted prefixes (`greffon:`) never change.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- 'S46-01 for the broker wait setting' means this plan's MCP-gateway S46-01 (renumbered S46-01), not main's S44-01 (kernel confinement, ADR 0094).
- Rule (c): the 'French plugin-API names' to remove are the extension API's former names (the `choregos_api.greffons` shim over `choregos_api.extensions`, `effets`, ...). Do not touch the entry-point group `choregos.plugins` (kept by ADR 0093 Decision 1) or anything of main's ADR 0093/0096 plugin feature: routers/plugins.py, routers/plugin_sources.py, services/plugin_*.py, choregos_core.plugins, choregos_adapters.plugin_servers, the /webhooks/plugins/{org}/{plugin}/{server} route.
- The `?jeton=` alias is still read only at apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/webhooks.py:253-259 (the Tekton sink). Main's new plugin webhook route authenticates with a bearer token and carries no jeton alias. Main's openapi.yaml grew by about 460 lines for plugins; `make contracts` regenerates packages/contracts/ts/ with them.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- for each removed alias, the converted test asserting the old name is gone (fails while the alias exists)

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] No alias:external row with removal release <= 0.22.0 remains
- [ ] Contract regenerated
- [ ] Upgrade notes list every removal
- [ ] make ci and make web-ci green

**Commands.**

```bash
make contracts
make ci
make web-ci
```

- Do not remove workflow.patched ids or French activity/signal wire aliases
- Do not touch persisted-data dual readers (they stay)
