### S46-01 · refactor(adapters): English names for the MCP client and the fake MCP hook, French aliases kept

**Wave** 0 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(adapters): English names for the MCP client and the fake MCP hook, French aliases kept (S46-01)`

**Goal.** The MCP client that S46 is about to extend is in English before it is extended. Every former client name keeps working through an alias, so importers outside this story stay untouched. The test hook FAUX_MCP becomes FAKE_MCP with no alias, so a missed site fails loudly. Schema digests stay byte-identical.

**Context.**

Cross-track:
- S45 (english track) excludes this story's files and S46-18's from its package stories, and later removes the aliases.
- The broker rename that used to be bundled here is now S46-18 (split for size). It shares no file with this story, so both run in W1.

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/client.py (218 lines, French docstring and comments):
- `from choregos_core.mcp import VERSIONS` L26 (leave it; S46-03 replaces it). PAGES_MAX L32.
- class ErreurMcp(RuntimeError) L35 (noqa N818), with `.code`.
- @dataclass(frozen=True) OutilMcp L43-54.
- def empreinte_du_schema L56: 'sha256:' + sha256 of json.dumps(schema or {}, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False). The result is persisted in connector_operations.schema_digest.
- _lire_sse L62.
- class ClientMcp L79. __init__ L80-112 has exactly 8 parameters after self, the ruff max-args limit. Attributes _suivant L111 and serveur L112.
- _entetes L114-131.
- _envoyer L133-161, with French messages L143 'refusé par le serveur' and L157 'réponse illisible'. <!-- lang: fr-ok -->
- _ouvrir L163-181, _http L183, list_tools L186-211 (French pages message L211), call_tool L213, test L216.

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/__init__.py L4-6 exports the four French names.

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py:
- FAUX_MCP L713-714.
- _client_du_faux_mcp L717-732, registered at L1004: `register("mcp", "fake")(_client_du_faux_mcp)`.
- ClientMcp is also used at L775-777 and L910-912 (demo guichets).
- The real factory is a lambda at L1230-1243.

FAUX_MCP sites (`git grep -l FAUX_MCP`, besides the registry):
- apps/api/tests: test_agent_realtime.py, test_agent_sessions.py, test_boite_des_decisions.py, test_broker_result_bounds.py, test_connector_enablement.py, test_connector_secrets.py, test_courtier.py, test_decouverte_mcp.py, test_egress_enforced.py (L35, L72), test_gabarit_joiners_leavers.py, test_mcp_catalogue.py (2 sites), test_operation_result_cap.py, test_rename_tenant_names.py;
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_actions.py L251;
- tests/e2e/test_ecriture_gouvernee.py and tests/e2e/test_m6_rh.py L59;
- tools/e2e_live_api.py L55.
The tests use monkeypatch.setattr(choregos_adapters, "FAUX_MCP", ...), which raises AttributeError on a missed site. tools/e2e_live_api.py ASSIGNS the attribute instead, so a missed rename there would fail silently; the acceptance greps for it.

packages/adapters/tests/test_client_mcp.py:
- L59 `match="refusé"` matches the client's message.
- L119 `client._entetes()` calls a private method this story renames.
- L62 `match="outil inconnu"` is the fake SERVER's JSON-RPC message, relayed unchanged. It stays.

Importers that keep the French names through the aliases (do not edit them):
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/effets.py L131;
- routers/org_connectors.py L532;
- services/courtier.py L393;
- packages/runner/src/choregos_runner/prompt.py L22 (`from choregos_adapters.mcp.client import ClientMcp`);
- packages/adapters/tests/test_cd_de_demo.py, test_egress_transport.py, test_faux_rh.py, test_faux_servis.py;
- tests/conformance/connecteurs/test_connecteurs_metier.py.

### S46-02 · docs(adr): 0097 Choregos consumes an MCP gateway and never is one, with its boundary test

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** S · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `docs(adr): 0097 Choregos consumes an MCP gateway and never is one, with its boundary test (S46-02)`

**Goal.** The decision is written and guarded before any code:
- ADR 0097 is accepted.
- The ADRs that refused gateways say what is still refused (building or being one) and what is now allowed (consuming one).
- A test turns CI red when vendor-specific code appears, or when the MCP or A2A door imports what reaches an organisation's connectors.

**Context.**

Wording to amend:
- docs/adr/0022-a-federable-catalogue.md L52-54: '**A runtime MCP gateway** (Obot, Docker MCP Gateway) in front of the sidecar' is discarded. Its Status line (L3) starts with 'proposed'.
- docs/adr/0028-the-governed-execution-of-change.md L56-58, point 2: 'Choregos does not build an identity provider, an MCP gateway, ...'. Status: superseded.
- docs/adr/0029-a-platform-for-governed-agents.md L62-64: 'There is **no general-purpose MCP gateway**'. L109-110: '**Building an identity provider or a general MCP gateway** - still discarded'.
- docs/adr/0065-catalogues-of-mcp-servers-and-skills.md: Status L3; L87 '**A runtime MCP gateway** (aggregators): re-opens the discovery ADR 0022 refuses'.
- docs/positioning.md L11-13: '... not a general-purpose MCP gateway.' L70-80 is a comparison table naming Obot / Docker MCP Gateway. docs/ may name vendors as examples.

ADR format: copy docs/adr/0087-an-organisation-has-one-outbound-proxy.md:
- title '# 0087 — ...' with an em dash;
- '- **Status**:' and '- **Concerns**:' bullets.
docs/adr/README.md has one table row per ADR (the last is 0087), checked by tests/docs/test_index_des_adr.py (test_l_index_n_oublie_aucune_decision, test_le_lien_de_l_index_pointe_le_bon_fichier, test_le_statut_de_l_index_est_celui_de_la_decision). The status word in the row equals the first word after '**Status**:'. tests/docs/test_links.py checks relative links. Link only to English ADR file names: French '# Moved' stubs exist beside them (0022-catalogue-federable.md, 0028-l-execution-gouvernee-du-changement.md, 0029-une-plateforme-d-agents-gouvernes.md) and S45 deletes them.

Catalogue exclusions: tools/catalogues/mcp/exclusions.yaml `names` L32-33:
- { pattern: "gateway", reason: "a gateway that re-opens discovery (ADR 0022)" }
- { pattern: "aggregator", ... }
Reasons are only printed on stderr by tools/catalogues/build_mcp_catalogue.py and are not persisted, so --check shows no diff.

Template for the boundary test: tests/licences/test_independent_products.py (ROOT, the TREES tuple, `git ls-files -z -- *TREES`, ALLOWED_FILES, test_there_is_code_to_check). Its TREES currently lists 2406 tracked files.

Regex trap (verified with python3): `\b` treats `_` as a word character, so `\btoolhive\b` does NOT match 'TOOLHIVE_URL'. Use look-arounds on [A-Za-z0-9]. The pattern given in step 9 matches the positive samples, spares the negative ones, and finds 0 hits in the 2406 files today.

The MCP door is apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/*.py (appelant, garde_fous, oauth, outils, transport). The A2A door is apps/api/src/choregos_api/a2a/*.py (door, push).
- They import `from ..services import (...)` with names such as creer_un_ticket, and choregos_adapters.storage.
- None imports courtier, broker, choregos_adapters.mcp or `choregos_adapters.build` today.
- An `ImportFrom` names a module AND names: `from ..services import broker` resolves to module choregos_api.services and must also be read as choregos_api.services.broker.

**Files.**

- Create: `docs/adr/0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md`, `tests/licences/test_mcp_gateway_boundary.py`
- Modify: `docs/adr/README.md`, `docs/adr/0022-a-federable-catalogue.md`, `docs/adr/0028-the-governed-execution-of-change.md`, `docs/adr/0029-a-platform-for-governed-agents.md`, `docs/adr/0065-catalogues-of-mcp-servers-and-skills.md`, `docs/positioning.md`, `tools/catalogues/mcp/exclusions.yaml`

**Steps.**

1. Write docs/adr/0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md in 0087's format:
   - Title '# 0097 — Choregos consumes an MCP gateway; it never is one'.
   - '- **Status**: accepted, 2026-10-27, by the project owner's request (stream S46); implemented by S46-03 onwards'.
   - '- **Concerns**:' with links to 0022, 0028, 0029, 0034, 0035, 0058, 0065, 0066, 0070, 0074, 0077, 0079 and 0087, by their English file names.
   - Sections '## Context', '## Decision' (points 1-9 of this plan's ADR text), '## Consequences', '## Alternatives rejected'. Vendor names may appear in Context as examples.
2. docs/adr/README.md: add after the 0087 row `| [0097] (a relative link to `0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md`) | <one sentence: Choregos consumes an organisation's MCP gateway through MCP and standard auth only, behind the broker, and never hosts, aggregates or re-serves MCP servers> | accepted |`.
3. 0022:
   - After the L52-54 bullet, add the sentence 'Still rejected: consuming an organisation's own gateway behind the broker is another thing ([ADR 0097] (a relative link to `0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md`)).'
   - Append ', amended by [ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`)' to the Concerns line.
   - Leave the Status word unchanged.
4. 0028, point 2 (L56-58): append ' (consuming a gateway an organisation already runs: [ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`))'.
5. 0029:
   - At L62-64, after 'no general-purpose MCP gateway', add '; Choregos consumes an organisation's gateway through public interfaces only ([ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`))'.
   - At L109-110, add '(being one stays discarded; consuming one: [ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`))'.
6. 0065:
   - On the Status line append '; amended by [ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`) (gateways are consumed, never catalogued)'.
   - At L87 add 'An organisation's own gateway is consumed as its MCP gateway ([ADR 0097] (a relative link to `...`)), not listed.'
7. docs/positioning.md:
   - L13: 'not a general-purpose MCP gateway: it consumes the one an organisation runs, through MCP and standard authentication only ([ADR 0097] (a relative link to `adr/0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md`))'.
   - After the table at L70-80, add one paragraph: a gateway is a hosting and credential layer Choregos can sit behind, never replace.
8. exclusions.yaml reasons:
   - gateway -> 'a gateway product is consumed as the organisation's MCP gateway (ADR 0097), never a catalogue entry'
   - aggregator -> 'an aggregator is a gateway product (ADR 0097), never a catalogue entry'
   Run `uv run python -m tools.catalogues.build_mcp_catalogue --check`: no diff expected.
9. Write tests/licences/test_mcp_gateway_boundary.py:
   - A module docstring citing ADR 0097.
   - ROOT and TREES copied from test_independent_products.py.
   - ALLOWED_FILES = frozenset({'packages/core/src/choregos_core/mcp_catalogue/catalogue.yaml', 'tools/catalogues/mcp/exclusions.yaml'}).
   - VENDOR = re.compile(r'(?<![A-Za-z0-9])(agentgateway|toolhive|context-?forge|mcp-context-forge|obot|mcpjungle|docker[ _-]?mcp[ _-]?gateway)(?![A-Za-z0-9])', re.IGNORECASE).
   - DOOR_DIRS = ('apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp', 'apps/api/src/choregos_api/a2a').
   - FORBIDDEN_MODULES = frozenset({'choregos_api.services.courtier', 'choregos_api.services.broker', 'choregos_api.services.mcp_gateway', 'choregos_api.services.mcp_gateway_identity', 'choregos_adapters.mcp', 'choregos_adapters.mcp.client'}).
   - A helper `forbidden_imports(source: str, module: str, is_package: bool = False) -> list[str]` walks ast.Import and ast.ImportFrom:
     - resolve relative imports: parts = module.split('.'); base = parts[:-level] for a module, parts[:len(parts) - level + 1] for an __init__.py;
     - for an ImportFrom, test BOTH the resolved module and f'{resolved}.{alias.name}' of every alias against FORBIDDEN_MODULES, any prefix match included (`choregos_adapters.mcp.x` counts);
     - also flag `from choregos_adapters import build`.
   - Module name of a file: its path under apps/api/src with '/' -> '.', without '.py'.
10. Run the commands. PR with the template; STATUS row: proves the boundary is guarded; does not prove any gateway feature.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- The boundary test also asserts that the inbound MCP door (`apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/`), the A2A door and the broker expose no passthrough or aggregation endpoint, no virtual server and no multi-tenant routing of third-party MCP servers, and that nothing in `apps/orchestrator` or `packages/runner` reads the gateway URL (the chart-side assertion that no runner pod receives it is S46-12's).

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Renumbered ADR: the gateway ADR is 0097, not 0088. Create `docs/adr/0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md` with the title '# 0097 — Choregos consumes an MCP gateway; it never is one'. Every 'ADR 0088' and every link to `0088-choregos-consumes-...md` in steps 2-8, in the exclusions.yaml reasons and in `test_the_catalogue_still_excludes_gateways_and_aggregators` becomes ADR 0097 and `0097-choregos-consumes-an-mcp-gateway-it-never-is-one.md`. 0088 and 0089 are reserved by open PRs and absent from docs/adr/. Before writing, check docs/adr/ on main: if 0097 is taken, use the next free number everywhere. Update the Files list to match.
- docs/adr/README.md: the last row is now 0096 (rows 0088 and 0089 are absent on purpose). Add the new row after the 0096 row. Its second cell must not say 'never hosts': Choregos runs installed plugins' own MCP servers (ADR 0096). Write: 'Choregos consumes an organisation's MCP gateway through MCP and standard auth only, behind the broker, and never routes, aggregates or re-serves third-party MCP servers (a plugin's own server, run by the platform under ADR 0096, is not an upstream)'.
- The new ADR's Concerns also link 0093 (`0093-an-agent-ships-as-a-plugin.md`) and 0096 (`0096-a-plugin-s-code-runs-as-an-mcp-server-the-platform-runs.md`). Its Decision adds a point required by the owner's reconciliation rule (a): the platform-run plugin servers of ADR 0096 are allowed. They run in-cluster, are governed as organisation `mcp` connectors with `org_connectors.plugin_server` set, are reached at the Service URL the platform wrote, and are never put behind the gateway. The gateway's `required` mode does not apply to them. Choregos still never routes, aggregates or re-serves third-party MCP servers.
- The boundary test's integrator assertion (no passthrough or aggregation endpoint, no virtual server, no multi-tenant routing of third-party MCP servers) must whitelist the plugin stream's legitimate MCP client users and routes. Do it with an explicit `ALLOWED_PLATFORM_MCP = frozenset({...})` that cites ADR 0096 and ADR 0091, listing: `choregos_adapters.plugin_servers` (PluginServerClient subclasses the MCP client), `choregos_adapters.tracker.plugin`, `choregos_adapters.fakes.plugin_tracker`, `choregos_api.services.plugin_servers`, `choregos_api.services.plugin_tracker`, `choregos_api.services.plugin_installer`, `choregos_api.routers.plugins`, `choregos_api.routers.plugin_sources`, and the route POST `/webhooks/plugins/{org}/{plugin}/{server}` (routers/webhooks.py `plugin_webhook`, L517-523: a third-party delivery handed to the plugin's own server's `tracker_parse_event`). Also whitelist `choregos_api.relay` (ADR 0091: an HTTP forward proxy for the search engines, not MCP). Add `test_the_whitelist_names_existing_modules`, which imports or resolves every entry, so that a later rename cannot leave a stale whitelist.
- Add 'choregos_api.services.plugin_servers', 'choregos_api.services.plugin_tracker' and 'choregos_adapters.plugin_servers' to FORBIDDEN_MODULES for DOOR_DIRS. A plugin server is an organisation connector, and the doors must not reach it either. Verified on main: apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/*.py and a2a/*.py import none of them (nor courtier or choregos_adapters.mcp), so the door test stays green.
- Anchors: in docs/adr/0065-catalogues-of-mcp-servers-and-skills.md the plugin stream inserted an 'Amended by ADR 0093' blockquote at L3-4. Its Status line is now L6 (not L3) and the runtime-gateway bullet is L90 (not L87). The other ADRs, docs/positioning.md and exclusions.yaml L32-33 are unchanged. The TREES of test_independent_products.py now list 2473 tracked files, and the VENDOR pattern still finds 0 hits on main (verified).

- Do not name any gateway vendor outside docs/: the new test would fail, and ADR 0097 forbids it.
- Do not use \b in VENDOR: it misses TOOLHIVE_URL.
- Do not delete or rename the French '# Moved' ADR stubs (S45 does).
- Do not link to French-named ADR files.

- Do not remove the gateway or aggregator exclusion rules: only their reason text changes.

### S46-03 · feat(adapters): the MCP client negotiates 2026-07-28, survives a lost session and takes per-request headers

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-01` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(adapters): the MCP client negotiates 2026-07-28, survives a lost session and takes per-request headers (S46-03)`

**Goal.** McpClient can talk to a gateway:
- It offers 2026-07-28 and refuses a version it does not speak.
- It never sends MCP-Protocol-Version on initialize.
- It works with stateless servers and re-initialises once after a 404 on a lost session (another gateway replica).
- It sends static routing headers plus headers from an async provider called per request, and refreshes the provider once on a 401.
The provider is how the OAuth token cache and the per-call signed assertion plug in.

**Context.**

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/client.py after S46-01. Line anchors are today's; the names are S46-01's English ones.
- `from choregos_core.mcp import VERSIONS` L26 becomes unused here: remove it, or ruff F401 fails.
- `_headers` (former _entetes, L114-131) ALWAYS sets MCP-Protocol-Version, even on initialize. The API's own MCP door refuses a request whose header is not in choregos_core.mcp.VERSIONS (apps/api/src/choregos_api/mcp/transport.py L118-119), and a strict gateway answers 400 the same way. So offering 2026-07-28 in the header on initialize would break.
- `_open` (L163-181) sends initialize with protocolVersion VERSIONS[0] and accepts whatever version comes back (L173-176).
- A session id is optional (L129-130, L146-148), but a 404 on a stale session is a plain McpError.
- Auth is static: token, auth_scheme (bearer|basic), auth_header.
- `_http` (L183) wraps the transport in `guarded()` (egress, ADR 0066). Keep it.
- __init__ already has 8 parameters after self. Adding `headers` and `headers_provider` makes 10, and ruff PLR0913 (max-args 8, checked with ruff 0.16.9) fails. Add `# noqa: PLR0913 - one keyword per transport option` on the `def __init__(` line, as packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/documents/web.py L103 does.
- ruff also enforces C901 (max-complexity 15) and PLR0912 (max-branches 20). Keep `_send` small by splitting it.

choregos_core/mcp.py L20: VERSIONS = ('2025-11-25', '2025-06-18', '2025-03-26') is what Choregos's own servers claim (the sidecar packages/tools-mcp, the API door, packages/runner serve_session.py). It must NOT change: they do not implement 2026-07-28. negocier (L30) answers VERSIONS[0] to an unknown offer, so the sidecar answers 2025-11-25 to an offer of 2026-07-28, which stays accepted.

FakeMcpServer echoes the offered protocolVersion (fakes/mcp.py `_resultat`), so existing tests negotiate 2026-07-28 and keep passing.

packages/adapters/tests/test_client_mcp.py L119 (after S46-01: `client._headers()["Authorization"]`) calls the sync method this story replaces: update it.

ADR 0017 (docs/adr/0017-mcp-2026-07-28-conformance.md) summarises 2026-07-28: stateless Streamable HTTP is normative. Tasks and elicitation are out of scope here.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py`
- Modify: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/client.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/mcp/__init__.py`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_client_mcp.py`

**Steps.**

1. client.py: remove the VERSIONS import. Add `CLIENT_VERSIONS: Final = ('2026-07-28', '2025-11-25', '2025-06-18', '2025-03-26', '2024-11-05')`, newest first. Its comment says it is the client's list and differs from choregos_core.mcp.VERSIONS (what Choregos's own servers claim). initialize sends protocolVersion CLIENT_VERSIONS[0].
2. Add `HeadersProvider = Callable[[bool], Awaitable[Mapping[str, str]]]`; the bool argument is `refresh`.
3. McpClient.__init__: add keyword arguments `headers: Mapping[str, str] | None = None` (static, sent on every request, e.g. a gateway routing header) and `headers_provider: HeadersProvider | None = None`.
   - Put `# noqa: PLR0913 - one keyword per transport option` on the def line.
   - Raise ValueError('give a token or a headers_provider, not both') when both are given.
   - Initialise `self._version: str | None = None`.
4. Replace `_headers()` with `async def _request_headers(self, *, initialize: bool = False, refresh: bool = False) -> dict[str, str]`, built in this order:
   - Content-Type and Accept;
   - MCP-Protocol-Version = self._version, ONLY when not initialize and self._version is set;
   - User-Agent;
   - the static headers;
   - the token-derived Authorization or auth_header (today's rules);
   - `await provider(refresh)`, merged last;
   - Mcp-Session-Id when a session is held.
   Never log the values.
5. `_open`:
   - Send initialize with `_request_headers(initialize=True)`.
   - Read result['protocolVersion']. If it is not a str in CLIENT_VERSIONS, raise McpError(f"initialize: the server answered protocol version {version!r}, which this client does not speak ({', '.join(CLIENT_VERSIONS)})").
   - Otherwise set self._version.
   - Expose a read-only property `negotiated_version -> str | None`.
   - notifications/initialized uses `await self._request_headers()`.
6. Split `_send` into `_post(http, method, body, *, initialize)` (returns httpx.Response) and `_parse(response, method, request_id)` (today's status, SSE and JSON handling, 'refused by the server' for 401/403). `_send`:
   - Post once.
   - On a 401 when a provider exists, post again with `_request_headers(refresh=True)`, once.
   - On a 404 when the method is not 'initialize' and a session id was sent: set self._session = None, `await self._open(http)`, and post the same body once more. A second 404 is McpError(..., 404).
   - Then `_parse`.
7. mcp/__init__.py: export CLIENT_VERSIONS and HeadersProvider.
8. test_client_mcp.py: make `test_the_connector_type_builds_a_basic_client` async and assert `(await client._request_headers())['Authorization'].startswith('Basic ')`.
9. Write the tests with inline httpx.MockTransport handlers (async handlers are allowed). Do not use or modify FakeMcpServer for the new behaviours. Run the commands. PR with the template; STATUS row: proves negotiation, lost-session recovery and the header provider against inline fakes; does not prove a real gateway, tasks or elicitation.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/plugin_servers.py L306 `class PluginServerClient(ClientMcp)` (McpClient after S46-01) overrides `_http()` to drop `guarded()` on purpose: a call to the platform's own Service is not an egress of the organisation (ADR 0096 §6). Keep `_http()` as the only place where the AsyncClient is built, with the same signature, and keep the attribute names `_timeout` and `_transport`. Do not inline `guarded()` into `_send`, `_post` or `_open`, or the subclass would be guarded again and fail closed in enforce mode (packages/adapters/tests/test_plugin_servers.py checks this).
- The plugin stream's MCP fakes answer initialize with protocolVersion '2025-06-18' and issue no session id. They are fakes/plugin_tracker.py L74-75, apps/api/tests/test_plugin_servers.py L207-208 and packages/adapters/tests/test_plugin_servers.py L95. That version is in CLIENT_VERSIONS and the 404 recovery only applies once a session id was sent, so no change is needed, but they must stay green. Add `apps/api/tests/test_plugin_servers.py apps/api/tests/test_plugin_tracker.py` to the first Commands line (packages/adapters/tests already covers the adapters-side plugin tests).
- client.py anchors are otherwise unchanged on main: VERSIONS import L27 (not L26), `_entetes` L114-131, `_envoyer` L133-161, `_ouvrir` L163-181, `_http` L183. The test to update is test_client_mcp.py::test_the_connector_type_builds_a_basic_client at L105 (its `_headers()` call is at L119).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_initialize_offers_2026_07_28_without_a_version_header :: the recorded initialize body has params.protocolVersion == '2026-07-28', and the initialize request has no 'mcp-protocol-version' header
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_the_agreed_version_travels_on_every_later_request :: the server answers '2025-06-18'; notifications/initialized and tools/list carry mcp-protocol-version: 2025-06-18; client.negotiated_version == '2025-06-18'
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_server_that_speaks_only_today_s_versions_is_reached :: the handler answers 400 to any request whose mcp-protocol-version header fails choregos_core.mcp.version_acceptable, and answers initialize with choregos_core.mcp.negocier(offered); list_tools succeeds and negotiated_version == '2025-11-25' (fails if the header is sent on initialize)
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_version_the_client_does_not_speak_is_refused :: the server answers '2023-01-01'; list_tools raises McpError whose message names '2023-01-01'
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_stateless_server_is_served_without_a_session :: the handler never returns Mcp-Session-Id and answers 400 to any request carrying one; list_tools returns the tools
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_lost_session_reinitializes_once :: the handler issues session 's1'; the first tools/list with s1 gets 404; a second initialize issues 's2'; the call succeeds; exactly two initialize requests were seen
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_second_404_is_an_error_not_a_loop :: the handler always answers 404 to tools/list; McpError with code 404, after exactly 2 initialize and 2 tools/list requests
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_static_and_provided_headers_go_with_every_request :: headers={'X-Server': 'github'} and a provider returning {'Authorization': 'Bearer p1'}; every recorded request (initialize, notifications/initialized, tools/list) carries both
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_a_401_refreshes_the_provider_once_then_fails :: the provider returns 'old' unless refresh=True, then 'new'; the handler answers 401 to 'old' and accepts 'new'; the call succeeds with exactly one refresh. A handler that always answers 401 gives McpError code 401 after one refresh
- packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py :: test_token_and_provider_are_exclusive :: McpClient(url, token='t', headers_provider=p) raises ValueError
- packages/adapters/tests/test_client_mcp.py (existing) :: all tests :: pass, with test_the_connector_type_builds_a_basic_client updated

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] choregos_core/mcp.py is unchanged; packages/tools-mcp, packages/runner and apps/api/tests/test_mcp_porte.py stay green.
- [ ] Every new behaviour has a test that fails on the S46-01 client.
- [ ] make ci is green (ruff: PLR0913 noqa present, C901 under 15).
- [ ] The PR body STATUS row says what it proves and what it does not.

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest -q packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_client_protocol.py packages/adapters/tests/test_client_mcp.py packages/adapters/tests packages/runner/tests packages/tools-mcp/tests apps/api/tests/test_mcp_porte.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not change choregos_core.mcp.VERSIONS or negocier.
- Do not modify packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/fakes/mcp.py (S45 owns it).
- Do not implement tasks, elicitation, sampling or SSE resumption.
- Do not retry on 5xx or on timeouts.
- Do not add the `mcp` SDK or any dependency.
- Do not log header values (tokens, assertions).
- Never edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `Module docstring of client.py: what is negotiated, what is recovered, what is not implemented.`

### S46-04 · feat(adapters): a fake MCP gateway, scriptable and served alone

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-01` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(adapters): a fake MCP gateway, scriptable and served alone (S46-04)`

**Goal.** Under ADR 0005 every integration has a fake. FakeMcpGateway:
- fronts several fake or HTTP MCP servers, by path or by header;
- checks each auth mode Choregos speaks: bearer, an API-key header, OAuth client credentials with its own token endpoint, and a JWT assertion verified against a JWKS that it refetches on an unknown kid;
- records who called and with which identity claims;
- can be stateless, can forget sessions (another replica), and can revoke tokens;
- resolves its fallback server on every request.
It is served as its own ASGI process for the local install.

**Context.**

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/fakes/mcp.py:
- FakeMcpServer is a dataclass (L23) with French fields: outils, jeton, recues, appels, gestes, reponses, structures.
- `transport()` (L41) returns an httpx.MockTransport with a sync handler.
- After initialize it requires Mcp-Session-Id == its `session` field (L32, default 'session-1').
- It echoes the offered protocolVersion.
The gateway code uses only the English members `transport()` and `session`. Tests confine every French field to two helpers at the top of the test module, `_server(tools, token='', gestures=None)` and `_received(server)`, so the S45 rename touches two lines per file.

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/fakes/serveur.py (ApplicationDeDemo L39-100) shows a handler served as a bare ASGI app, with `application()` and `__main__` running uvicorn (L103-112, pragma no cover). Do not import or modify it: write a small English ASGI adapter in the new module.

The demo fakes server (`python -m choregos_adapters.fakes.serveur`, port CHOREGOS_DEMO_PORT, default 8090) serves /{mdm,shipping,access_control,supplier,fournisseur,cd}/mcp behind Bearer CONNECTOR_DEMO_JETON. These are the HTTP upstreams of the local install (S46-15).

The adapters package depends on PyJWT 2.15.1 (uv.lock) and cryptography:
- `jwt.algorithms.ECAlgorithm.to_jwk(key, as_dict=True)`;
- `jwt.PyJWK(jwk).key`;
- jwt.decode raises InvalidAudienceError when the token has `aud` and no audience is given, so pass options verify_aud=bool(audience).

httpx.MockTransport awaits an async handler under an AsyncClient. packages/adapters/tests/test_faux_servis.py:25 serves an ASGI app through httpx.ASGITransport.

ruff:
- packages/**/fakes/** ignores only S;
- C901 (15) and PLR0912 (20) apply, so `respond` must dispatch to helpers;
- dataclass fields are not arguments (no PLR0913).

No vendor names (tests/licences/test_mcp_gateway_boundary.py from S46-02 scans packages/).

S46-03 (client recovery) runs in the same wave: tests here use McpClient only for paths that need no recovery. The client-through-forgotten-session test is in S46-05.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/fakes/mcp_gateway.py`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py`

**Steps.**

1. fakes/mcp_gateway.py: the first line is SPDX. Define `@dataclass class GatewayUpstream`:
   - `server: FakeMcpServer | None = None` (in memory);
   - `url: str = ''` (over HTTP);
   - `token: str = ''` (the upstream credential the gateway holds, sent as 'Authorization: Bearer <token>').
2. Define `@dataclass class GatewayCall` with fields server, method, headers (a lowercased dict), claims (dict | None) and identity_header_claims (dict | None).
3. Define `@dataclass class FakeMcpGateway` with these fields:
   - upstreams: dict[str, GatewayUpstream];
   - fallback: FakeMcpServer | Callable[[], FakeMcpServer] | None = None (resolved on EVERY request when the server is unknown);
   - routing: Literal['path', 'header'] = 'path'; path_template: str = '/{server}/mcp'; server_header: str = 'X-MCP-Server';
   - auth: Literal['none', 'bearer', 'header', 'oauth_client_credentials', 'jwt_assertion'] = 'bearer'; token: str = ''; auth_header: str = 'X-Api-Key';
   - client_id: str = ''; client_secret: str = ''; token_ttl_s: int = 300;
   - jwks: dict | None = None; jwks_url: str = ''; jwks_transport: httpx.AsyncBaseTransport | None = None (tests); audience: str = ''; issuer: str = '';
   - identity_header: str = '';
   - stateless: bool = False;
   - forget_after_initialize: int = 0 (that many upcoming sessions are forgotten right after being issued);
   - calls: list[GatewayCall]; token_requests: list[dict[str, str]]; refusals: list[tuple[int, str]];
   - private: _issued: dict[str, float] (token -> expiry), _seen_jti: set[str], _sessions: dict[str, tuple[str, str | None]] (gateway session -> server, upstream session), _stateless_upstream: dict[str, str | None] (one upstream session per server name), _counter: int.
   Add `def revoke_tokens(self) -> None` (clears _issued).
4. `def transport(self) -> httpx.MockTransport` returns httpx.MockTransport(self.respond). `async def respond(self, request) -> httpx.Response` only dispatches to helpers, so each stays under C901 15:
   - (a) `_token_endpoint`: POST /oauth/token requires HTTP Basic client_id:client_secret and grant_type=client_credentials (form parsed with urllib.parse.parse_qs). It records scope, resource and audience in token_requests and returns {'access_token': uuid4().hex, 'token_type': 'Bearer', 'expires_in': token_ttl_s}, else 401 {'error': 'invalid_client'}.
   - (b) GET /healthz -> 200.
   - (c) `_server_of`: from the path for path routing (path_template with {server} as [^/]+), from server_header for header routing. Unknown and no fallback -> 404.
   - (d) `_authenticate`:
     - none: always passes;
     - bearer: Authorization == f'Bearer {token}';
     - header: auth_header == token;
     - oauth_client_credentials: a Bearer in _issued and not expired;
     - jwt_assertion: `_verify_assertion(bearer)` decodes ES256 with the kid from the JWKS (the dict, or jwks_url fetched with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=jwks_transport) and cached, refetched ONCE when the kid is unknown). It uses audience=audience or None, issuer=issuer or None, options={'require': ['exp', 'iat', 'jti', 'sub'], 'verify_aud': bool(audience)}, requires exp - iat <= 120 and an unseen jti, and records the jti.
     - When identity_header is set, also verify the assertion found there the same way.
     - Failure: append (401, reason) to refusals and return 401 with 'WWW-Authenticate: Bearer'.
   - (e) A non-initialize request carrying an Mcp-Session-Id the gateway did not issue, or forgot, gets 404 {'error': 'unknown session'} and a refusals entry (404, 'unknown session').
   - (f) Record a GatewayCall.
   - (g) `_forward`:
     - Copy the JSON body.
     - Drop the incoming Authorization, auth_header and identity_header.
     - Set Authorization: Bearer upstream.token when non-empty.
     - On initialize, forward, remember the upstream Mcp-Session-Id, and answer with the gateway's own id f'gw-{server}-{n}' (none when stateless). When forget_after_initialize > 0, decrement it and drop that id from _sessions after answering.
     - Stateless: keep one upstream session per server in _stateless_upstream; a non-initialize request with none first initialises upstream once.
     - In memory: `await upstream.server.transport().handle_async_request(req)` then `await response.aread()`. Over HTTP: `httpx.AsyncClient().post(upstream.url, ...)`.
     - Return the upstream status, body and content-type, with Mcp-Session-Id replaced by the gateway's own.
5. `class FakeMcpGatewayApp`: a bare ASGI app around a FakeMcpGateway. It supports lifespan, builds an httpx.Request from the scope and body, awaits respond, and sends status, headers and body (the serveur.py L69-100 pattern, written in English).
6. `def application() -> FakeMcpGatewayApp` reads env:
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_UPSTREAMS (JSON {server: {url, token}});
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_AUTH (default bearer);
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_TOKEN;
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_AUTH_HEADER;
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_CLIENT_ID and CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_CLIENT_SECRET;
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_JWKS_URL, CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_AUDIENCE, CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_ISSUER;
   - CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_ROUTING and CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_SERVER_HEADER.
   Add `if __name__ == '__main__':  # pragma: no cover - the container's entry point` running uvicorn on 0.0.0.0, port CHOREGOS_FAKE_GATEWAY_PORT (default 8091).
7. Module docstring in English: what it fakes, that it is not a product, and that the gateway (never Choregos) holds the upstream tokens.
8. Write the tests. FakeMcpServer French fields only inside `_server()` and `_received()`. Run the commands. PR with the template; STATUS row: proves the fake's behaviour in every auth mode; does not prove the platform uses it (S46-05 onward).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_path_routing_reaches_the_named_server_and_404s_an_unknown_one :: McpClient('https://gw.test/supplier/mcp', token='gw', transport=gw.transport()).list_tools() returns the supplier's tools; '/other/mcp' gives McpError 404
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_header_routing_reads_the_server_header :: routing='header'; raw httpx posts to https://gw.test/mcp with X-MCP-Server: supplier reach the supplier
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_the_upstream_gets_the_gateway_s_credential_never_choregos_s :: auth='bearer', token 'gw-secret', upstream token 'upstream-key'; the upstream server records authorization 'Bearer upstream-key', and none of its received requests contains 'gw-secret'
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_a_wrong_credential_is_a_401 :: bearer and header modes, with a wrong or missing value -> 401 and a refusals entry
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_client_credentials_issue_a_token_that_opens_the_gateway :: POST /oauth/token with Basic auth and resource=https://gw.test returns access_token; token_requests[0]['resource'] == 'https://gw.test'; an MCP call with it succeeds; a bad client_secret -> 401
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_revoked_tokens_no_longer_open_the_gateway :: after gw.revoke_tokens(), the previously issued token gets 401
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_a_signed_assertion_is_verified_against_the_jwks :: generate an EC P-256 key, build the JWKS with ECAlgorithm.to_jwk(public, as_dict=True) plus kid, sign {sub, iat, exp=iat+60, jti, aud} ES256 with that kid; the call succeeds and calls[-1].claims['sub'] matches
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_an_assertion_that_is_expired_replayed_or_for_another_audience_is_refused :: three 401s and three refusals entries
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_a_rotated_key_is_fetched_again_from_the_jwks_url :: jwks_url served by an httpx.MockTransport (jwks_transport) whose keys switch from kid1 to kid2; an assertion signed with kid1, then one with kid2, are both accepted; the JWKS was fetched exactly twice
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_an_unknown_or_forgotten_gateway_session_is_a_404 :: a raw tools/list with Mcp-Session-Id 'gw-nope' -> 404; with forget_after_initialize=1, the session id returned by a raw initialize gets 404 on the next tools/list
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_a_stateless_gateway_answers_without_a_session :: stateless=True; no response carries mcp-session-id; a tools/list posted without initialize returns the tools; two such calls initialise the upstream once
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_the_fallback_is_resolved_on_every_request :: fallback=lambda: current['server']; list_tools for an unknown server reaches server A, then, after current['server'] = B, reaches B
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_the_asgi_app_serves_health_and_mcp :: httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=FakeMcpGatewayApp(gw))) gets 200 on /healthz, and McpClient over the same transport lists tools
- packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py :: test_application_reads_its_environment :: monkeypatched env with UPSTREAMS JSON and AUTH=header gives a gateway with that upstream url and auth

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Only the two new files are added: no change to fakes/mcp.py, fakes/serveur.py, fakes/__init__.py or choregos_adapters/__init__.py.
- [ ] Every auth mode has a passing and a refusing test.
- [ ] make ci is green (C901/PLR0912 respected), and the S46-02 boundary test still passes.
- [ ] The PR body STATUS row says what it proves and what it does not.

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest -q packages/adapters/tests/test_fake_mcp_gateway.py tests/licences
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not modify fakes/mcp.py, fakes/serveur.py or fakes/__init__.py.
- Do not read FakeMcpServer's French fields from the gateway code. In tests, confine them to the two helpers.
- Do not name any gateway vendor.
- Do not add dependencies (PyJWT and cryptography are already there).
- Do not make the gateway accept an unsigned identity header.
- Never edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or docs/plan/08-backlog.yaml.

### S46-05 · feat(adapters): the mcp-gateway connector type, a server the organisation's MCP gateway names

**Wave** 2 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-03`, `S46-04` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(adapters): the mcp-gateway connector type, a server the organisation's MCP gateway names (S46-05)`

**Goal.** A new connector type ('mcp', 'mcp-gateway') with:
- kind mcp; config {server, timeout_s?}; no secret field; no egress host of its own.
Its real factory builds an McpClient only from a gateway configuration that the platform injects, and fails closed otherwise. Under CHOREGOS_FAKES the fake factory routes it to a process-wide FakeMcpGateway whose fallback is always the CURRENT FAKE_MCP; a gateway connector without the platform's injection fails closed there too. The type ships with its procedure (ADR 0067) and its console fixture line.

**Context.**

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py, after S46-01:
- _objet L164.
- _SPECS_LIVREES starts at L225. The ('mcp', 'mcp') spec is at L679-703: config url (required), auth_scheme, username, auth_header, timeout_s (default 30); secret 'token'.
- FAKE_MCP ~L714, `_fake_mcp_client(cfg)` ~L717 (registered `register('mcp', 'fake')`), real `_mcp_client` ~L1233.

registry.py:
- ConnectorTypeSpec L85-108: egress callable L104; check_config L107, which raises AdapterError and which 'the adapter repeats when built'.
- validate_config L359, spec_of L146, connector_types L150, egress_hosts L366 (with egress set, it returns spec.egress(config)).
- build() L276 swaps the type for 'fake' when fakes_enabled() (L222, read from the env at call time). So under fakes BOTH mcp and mcp-gateway go to `_fake_mcp_client`, which must recognise the injected key route == 'gateway'.

TRAP: apps/api/tests/conftest.py sets CHOREGOS_FAKES=1 with os.environ.setdefault at import, for the whole pytest process. Real-factory tests must call `monkeypatch.delenv('CHOREGOS_FAKES', raising=False)`, as test_client_mcp.py::test_the_connector_type_builds_a_basic_client does. Otherwise they pass alone and fail in `make test`.

TRAP: a FakeMcpGateway cached once with fallback=<the FAKE_MCP of that moment> goes stale, because tests monkeypatch FAKE_MCP per test. Pass a callable fallback (S46-04 resolves it per request).

services/platform_network.py URL_KEYS = ('url', 'base_url', 'api_url', 'endpoint', 'server_url', 'host'). The new field is named 'server' so it is never read as a host.

Injected configuration contract (written by the API in S46-08 and S46-09, documented here):
- route: 'gateway';
- gateway_url: str;
- gateway_headers: dict[str, str];
- headers_provider: HeadersProvider;
- timeout_s: float;
- user_agent: str;
- server: str (from the connector);
- optional transport (tests only).

Procedures:
- Model: packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/connector.mcp.mcp.md. Front matter: id, title, applies_to {connector: mcp/mcp}, audience, version, reviewed, vendor_docs (the MCP transports spec URL), egress list, fields. Then six sections: 'Before you start', 'At the vendor', 'Permissions', 'In Choregos', 'Test it', 'When it fails'.
- tests/procedures/test_coverage.py: test_every_shipped_connector_type_has_a_procedure (L61) fails without the new file. test_a_connector_procedure_talks_only_about_fields_its_type_has (L145) requires fields ⊆ config properties ∪ secret_fields.
- packages/core/tests/test_procedures_format.py: every link https and listed in vendor_docs; no secret shapes.
- tools/procedures/export_docs.py writes docs/procedures/ (README.md included), and `--mocks apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json` writes the console mock. tests/procedures/test_export_docs.py checks both, and procedures-check is in make ci.

tests/web/test_connector_icon_fixture_is_current.py::test_the_fixture_equals_the_registry compares connector_types() with apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json (sorted list of {kind, type}). Kind mcp already maps to the MCP icon (apps/web/src/components/connector-icon.tsx).

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/connector.mcp.mcp-gateway.md`, `docs/procedures/connector.mcp.mcp-gateway.md`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_mcp_gateway_type.py`
- Modify: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py`, `apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json`, `apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json`, `docs/procedures/README.md`

**Steps.**

1. In choregos_adapters/__init__.py add `MCP_GATEWAY_TYPE = 'mcp-gateway'` and `GATEWAY_SERVER_PATTERN = r'^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$'`.
2. Add `def _mcp_gateway_check(config: dict[str, Any]) -> None`:
   - Raise AdapterError("`server` is the server's name at the organisation's MCP gateway: letters, digits, '.', '_' or '-', 128 at most") when server is missing or does not match.
   - Raise an AdapterError naming the key when the config holds any of ('url', 'token', 'auth_header', 'auth_scheme', 'username').
3. In _SPECS_LIVREES, after ('mcp', 'mcp'), add ('mcp', 'mcp-gateway'): ConnectorTypeSpec with:
   - display "MCP server through the organisation's MCP gateway";
   - capabilities ('mcp',);
   - config `_objet({'server': {'type': 'string', 'pattern': GATEWAY_SERVER_PATTERN, 'description': "the server's name at the organisation's MCP gateway"}, 'timeout_s': {'type': 'number', 'minimum': 1, 'maximum': 120, 'description': "seconds before a call is abandoned; absent: the gateway's timeout"}}, ('server',))`, with NO default on timeout_s;
   - secret fields ();
   - egress=lambda config: [];
   - check_config=_mcp_gateway_check.
4. Add `def _current_fake_mcp() -> Any`: return FAKE_MCP, creating FakeMcpServer() lazily (move that logic out of `_fake_mcp_client`, which now calls it).
5. Add `def _mcp_gateway_client(cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> Any`:
   - First `_mcp_gateway_check({'server': cfg.get('server', '')})`.
   - If cfg.get('route') != 'gateway' or not cfg.get('gateway_url'), raise AdapterError("an `mcp-gateway` connector is reached through its organisation's MCP gateway, which only the platform supplies: it cannot be built from its configuration alone").
   - Otherwise return McpClient(str(cfg['gateway_url']), headers=dict(cfg.get('gateway_headers') or {}), headers_provider=cfg.get('headers_provider'), timeout_s=float(cfg.get('timeout_s', 30)), transport=cfg.get('transport'), user_agent=str(cfg.get('user_agent', ''))).
   - Register it next to _mcp_client: `register('mcp', 'mcp-gateway', _SPECS_LIVREES[('mcp', 'mcp-gateway')])(_mcp_gateway_client)`.
6. Add `FAKE_MCP_GATEWAY: Any = None` and `def fake_mcp_gateway() -> Any`. It returns the process-wide FakeMcpGateway, created lazily as FakeMcpGateway(auth='none', fallback=_current_fake_mcp), passing the function itself and not its result.
7. `_fake_mcp_client(cfg)`:
   - When cfg.get('route') == 'gateway', return McpClient(str(cfg['gateway_url']), headers=dict(cfg.get('gateway_headers') or {}), headers_provider=cfg.get('headers_provider'), timeout_s=float(cfg.get('timeout_s', 30)), transport=fake_mcp_gateway().transport(), user_agent=str(cfg.get('user_agent', ''))).
   - Elif 'server' in cfg and not cfg.get('url'), raise the same AdapterError as step 5 (fail closed under fakes too).
   - The direct path is unchanged.
8. Write packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/connector.mcp.mcp-gateway.md. Front matter:
   - id connector.mcp.mcp-gateway;
   - title "Connect an MCP server through your organisation's MCP gateway";
   - applies_to {connector: mcp/mcp-gateway}; audience org_admin; version 1; reviewed 2026-10-27;
   - vendor_docs [the MCP transports spec entry of connector.mcp.mcp.md];
   - egress ["none of its own: the MCP gateway's host, opened when the gateway is set"];
   - fields {server: 'the name your MCP gateway gives the server', timeout_s: "seconds before a call is abandoned; empty uses the gateway's timeout"}.
   Six vendor-neutral sections:
   - At the vendor = at your MCP gateway: register the server under a name, and give the gateway the server's credential or OAuth; Choregos never holds it.
   - In Choregos: declare it with capability MCP and type "MCP server through the organisation's MCP gateway", then Discover.
   - When it fails: 409 gateway_not_configured, 409 gateway_assertion_key_missing, 401 from the gateway, 404 for an unknown server name.
9. Run `uv run python tools/procedures/export_docs.py` and `uv run python tools/procedures/export_docs.py --mocks apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json`. Add {"kind": "mcp", "type": "mcp-gateway"} to apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json in sort order.
10. Write the tests: put FakeMcpServer French fields in `_server()` and `_received()` helpers, and delenv CHOREGOS_FAKES in real-factory tests. Run the commands, make web-ci included. PR with the template; STATUS row: proves the type, its fail-closed factories and its fake routing; does not prove any API path (S46-08, S46-09).

### S46-06 · contract(openapi): an organisation's MCP gateway, its assertion keys and catalogue installs through it

**Wave** 2 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-02` · **Contract change** yes

**PR title:** `contract(openapi): an organisation's MCP gateway, its assertion keys and catalogue installs through it (S46-06)`

**Goal.** Contract first (ADR 0001):
- the six MCP gateway operations and their schemas;
- the public JWKS route;
- the per-organisation `install_routes` of a catalogue entry;
- `route` and `gateway_server` on a catalogue install;
- an McpGatewayConflict problem carrying the codes gateway_in_use, gateway_required, gateway_not_configured and gateway_assertion_key_missing, referenced where those 409s happen.
The API lists the new operations as CONTRACT_ONLY until S46-07 and S46-08 serve them.

**Context.**

packages/contracts/openapi.yaml:
- Template: /orgs/{org}/outbound-proxy L1769-1801 and /test L1802-1812; schemas OutboundProxy L6233 to OutboundProxyTest L6272-6282 (closed objects, writeOnly password, tag admin).
- Public operations use `security: []` (e.g. L80).
- components/responses L3724-3750: Conflict, EgressRequired (schema EgressRequired L7109: code enum [egress_required]), ValidationError.
- Problem L3861.
- createOrgConnector L365-376 (409 -> EgressRequired); discoverConnectorOperations ~L425-436 (NO 409 today); installMcpCatalogueEntry L458-474 (409 -> EgressRequired).
- McpCatalogueEntry L4232-4263 (not closed; `install` enum installable|needs_oauth|needs_hosting is computed in core with no organisation, keep it; `procedure` L4262); McpCatalogueInstall L4264-4272 (not closed).
- EgressRule.origin description L7043 lists origins; add `mcp-gateway`.

OpenAPI allows one response per status code. A no-secrets-key 409 on PUT reuses the EgressRequired response, whose `code` and `verdicts` are optional. A 409 that can be either an egress or a gateway conflict uses `oneOf [EgressRequired, McpGatewayConflict]`. Widening EgressRequired.code is rejected (it would widen a response enum).

Generated TypeScript: packages/contracts/ts/api.ts, written by `make contracts` (tools/gen_contracts.py). It maps oneOf to a union and allOf to an intersection, and only 2xx responses reach the Operations map. Flat closed objects are the safest.

apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py L13-14: CONTRACT_ONLY (operationId -> story) and CONTRACT_ONLY_PATHS, empty today.

Model test: tests/docs/test_outbound_proxy_contract.py. Its `_validator(name)` helper (L23-24) builds Draft202012Validator({'components': SPEC['components'], **SCHEMAS[name]}).

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/docs/test_mcp_gateway_contract.py`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/openapi.yaml`, `packages/contracts/ts/api.ts`, `apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py`

**Steps.**

1. Paths (tag admin, parameter Org), after /orgs/{org}/outbound-proxy/test:
   - /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway:
     - get getMcpGateway: 200 McpGatewayState, 403. org_admin.
     - put putMcpGateway: body McpGatewayPut; 200 McpGatewayState, 403, "409": {$ref: '#/components/responses/EgressRequired'} (the description says a 409 without `verdicts` means the deployment has no secrets key), 422.
     - delete deleteMcpGateway: 204, 403, "409": {$ref: '#/components/responses/McpGatewayConflict'} (code gateway_in_use, `connectors` names them).
   - /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/test: post testMcpGateway, body McpGatewayTestRequest; 200 McpGatewayTest (a failing gateway is ok:false, not an HTTP error), 403, 404.
   - /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/assertion-key: post rotateMcpGatewayAssertionKey; 200 McpGatewayAssertion, 403, 409 Conflict (no secrets key).
   - /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/jwks.json: get getMcpGatewayJwks with `security: []`; 200 JsonWebKeySet, 404. Description: public keys only, unauthenticated, cacheable 300 s; the same 404 for an unknown organisation and for one without an assertion key.
2. Schemas, all `additionalProperties: false` except JsonWebKeySet:
   - McpGatewayAuth, required [type]:
     - type enum [bearer, header, oauth_client_credentials, jwt_assertion];
     - header_name [string, null] pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$;
     - token_url [string, null] maxLength 2000;
     - client_id [string, null] maxLength 256;
     - scope [string, null] maxLength 1024;
     - resource [string, null] maxLength 2000 (RFC 8707);
     - token_audience [string, null] maxLength 512.
   - McpGateway, required [mode, endpoint, routing, server_header, auth, audience, identity_header, timeout_s, secret_set]:
     - mode enum [off, available, required];
     - endpoint string maxLength 2000, pattern ^https?://[^\s@?#]+$;
     - routing enum [path, header];
     - server_header [string, null] pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}$;
     - auth $ref McpGatewayAuth;
     - audience [string, null] maxLength 512;
     - identity_header [string, null] same pattern as server_header;
     - timeout_s number 1-120;
     - secret_set boolean.
   - McpGatewayAssertionKey, required [kid, created_at, current]: kid string; created_at date-time; current boolean.
   - McpGatewayAssertion, required [issuer, jwks_url, keys]; keys is an array of McpGatewayAssertionKey.
   - McpGatewayState, required [gateway, updated_by, updated_at, assertion]: gateway oneOf [McpGateway, null]; updated_by and updated_at [string, null]; assertion McpGatewayAssertion.
   - McpGatewayPut, required [mode, endpoint, routing, auth]: McpGateway's fields minus secret_set, with timeout_s default 30; plus secret (string, minLength 1, maxLength 4096, writeOnly); plus allow_egress (boolean, default false).
   - McpGatewayTestRequest, required [server]: server pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$.
   - McpGatewayTest, required [ok, server, protocol_version, server_info, tools, elapsed_ms, error]:
     - protocol_version [string, null];
     - server_info [object, null] (additionalProperties true);
     - tools [integer, null];
     - elapsed_ms integer >= 0;
     - error [string, null], which names the gateway, never a credential.
   - McpGatewayConflict (a flat problem, like EgressRequired), required [type, title, status, code]: type, title, status, detail; code enum [gateway_in_use, gateway_required, gateway_not_configured, gateway_assertion_key_missing]; connectors: array of string.
   - JsonWebKeySet, required [keys]; keys is an array of objects with additionalProperties true.
   Add the response component McpGatewayConflict (application/problem+json).
3. McpCatalogueEntry: add the optional property install_routes: {type: array, uniqueItems: true, items: {type: string, enum: [direct, gateway]}, description: 'how THIS organisation can install the entry: direct (Choregos holds the credential) and/or gateway (through its MCP gateway, ADR 0097)'}. Do not add it to required.
4. McpCatalogueInstall: add
   - route: {type: [string, "null"], enum: [direct, gateway, null], description: "absent: gateway when the organisation's gateway is required, or when the entry cannot be installed directly and a gateway is available; else direct"};
   - gateway_server: {type: [string, "null"], pattern: ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$, description: "the server's name at the gateway; defaults to the entry id"}.
5. 409s:
   - createOrgConnector and installMcpCatalogueEntry: replace the 409 ref with an inline response, description 'State conflict: a host to open (egress_required) or the organisation's MCP gateway (gateway_required, gateway_not_configured)', content application/problem+json schema oneOf [EgressRequired, McpGatewayConflict].
   - discoverConnectorOperations: add "409": {$ref: McpGatewayConflict response} (gateway_required, gateway_not_configured, gateway_assertion_key_missing), and say in the description that a gateway refusal does not mark the connector in error.
   - EgressRule.origin description: add `mcp-gateway`.
6. Run `make contracts` and commit packages/contracts/ts/api.ts.
7. apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py:
   - CONTRACT_ONLY = {'getMcpGateway': 'S46-08', 'putMcpGateway': 'S46-08', 'deleteMcpGateway': 'S46-08', 'testMcpGateway': 'S46-08', 'rotateMcpGatewayAssertionKey': 'S46-07', 'getMcpGatewayJwks': 'S46-07'};
   - CONTRACT_ONLY_PATHS = {'/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway', '/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/test', '/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/assertion-key', '/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/jwks.json'}.
8. Write tests/docs/test_mcp_gateway_contract.py: first line SPDX; reuse the `_validator` helper pattern; base PUT body {'mode': 'available', 'endpoint': 'https://gw.test/{server}/mcp', 'routing': 'path', 'auth': {'type': 'bearer'}}. Run the commands. Title the PR 'contract(openapi): ...' and label it contract-change (ADR 0001). STATUS row: proves the document's shape; does not prove the API serves it.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

### S46-07 · feat(api): per-organisation assertion keys for the MCP gateway, their JWKS and rotation

**Wave** 4 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-06`, `S46-13`, `S47-17` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(api): per-organisation assertion keys for the MCP gateway, their JWKS and rotation (S46-07)`

**Goal.** Each organisation gets its own ES256 signing key for MCP gateway assertions:
- The private key is sealed in the organisation's secret store.
- The public key is published unauthenticated at /api/v1/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/jwks.json, with the same 404 for an unknown organisation and for one without a key.
- An org_admin creates or rotates the key, and the previous key stays published.
One function mints the short-lived signed assertion of a call identity. A lost key fails closed with a typed refusal (GatewayRefusal, code gateway_assertion_key_missing) that later stories map without a 500 or a retry.

**Context.**

Do NOT reuse the run-token key. apps/api/src/choregos_api/security.py run_token_keys L70 and mint_run_token L96 are deployment-wide, and ADR 0077 forbids sending the run token anywhere.

Template router: apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/outbound_proxy.py.
- _admin_organisation L35-43: org_admin by `principal.org_roles.get(org) != Role.ORG_ADMIN`, then the Organization by slug, or not_found.
- Audit via `record(session, principal, action, org_id=, target_type='organization', target_id=, **fields)`.
- Outbound-proxy tests read audit rows with `_audit(action)` (apps/api/tests/test_outbound_proxy.py L106) on AuditLog.payload. `test_every_route_needs_org_admin` (L235-250) logs in as someone@elsewhere.dev for the 403.

Secret store (ADR 0058), apps/api/src/choregos_api/secret_store.py:
- get_secret_store(); store.put(session, org_id, name, value, actor), get_many (raises SecretIntrouvable when absent), meta, delete; available().
- Secret names must match ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9/_.-]{0,199}$ (L50).
- FakeSecretStore(available=False) makes put raise SecretStoreUnavailable. set_secret_store(store | None).
- services/connector_secrets.py require_store(values) returns 409 NO_KEY_MESSAGE when values are given and the store is unavailable (L25-32).
- remember_secret comes from choregos_core.redaction.

Public routes take no Me dependency.
- Use `DbPlateforme` (apps/api/src/choregos_api/deps.py L56-76), which sees every organisation, as webhooks and routers/theme.py L25-46 do.
- theme.py never leaks whether a slug exists.
- An unauthenticated test uses a fresh `AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url='http://test')` (apps/api/tests/test_agent_resolution.py L87), because the `client` fixture is logged in after `admin`.

Organization.settings (db/models.py L39) is JSON with no migration (outbound proxy precedent). Store the PUBLIC JWKs there, so the public route never reads the secret store.

PyJWT 2.15.1: `jwt.algorithms.ECAlgorithm.to_jwk(public_key, as_dict=True)`; `jwt.encode(payload, pem, algorithm='ES256', headers={'kid': kid, 'typ': 'JWT'})`.

settings.public_url: config.py L254 (CHOREGOS_PUBLIC_URL, set for the API and workers by the chart and compose).

choregos_adapters.errors.ConfigurationError(AdapterError(ChoregosError)): the broker maps AdapterError to 422, effects map it to a final EffetRefuse, and the global handler maps ChoregosError to 400. A refusal class deriving from it never becomes a 500.

main.py: router imports L33-75 (outbound_proxy L58); `routeurs_du_coeur` tuple L308-349 (outbound_proxy.router at L336). schemas/__init__.py re-exports schemas (outbound_proxy at L203).

ruff S105 flags a string constant whose name contains SECRET. The outbound proxy uses `# noqa: S105 - a name in the secret store, not a secret` (services/outbound_proxy.py L37).

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/mcp_gateway_identity.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/mcp_gateway.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/schemas/mcp_gateway.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/main.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/schemas/__init__.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py`

**Steps.**

1. schemas/mcp_gateway.py (first line SPDX): pydantic Dto classes matching the contract: McpGatewayAssertionKey(kid, created_at, current) and McpGatewayAssertion(issuer, jwks_url, keys). Export them from schemas/__init__.py next to outbound_proxy.
2. services/mcp_gateway_identity.py (first line SPDX). Constants:
   - SETTINGS_KEY = 'mcp_gateway_assertion';
   - SECRET_PREFIX = 'org/mcp-gateway/assertion-key' with `# noqa: S105 - a name in the secret store, not a secret` (one sealed PEM per kid, at f'{SECRET_PREFIX}/{kid}');
   - ASSERTION_TTL_S = 60; MAX_TTL_S = 120.
   Refusal classes:
   - `class GatewayRefusal(ConfigurationError)` with `code: ClassVar[str] = 'gateway_refused'` and the docstring 'a refusal raised before anything leaves for the MCP gateway (ADR 0097)';
   - `class AssertionKeyMissing(GatewayRefusal)` with code = 'gateway_assertion_key_missing'.
3. `@dataclass(frozen=True) class CallIdentity`:
   - org: str; subject: str; purpose: str;
   - project, run, work_item, agent, initiator, action, connector, server, tool: str | None = None;
   - approved_by: tuple[str, ...] = ().
   `def claims(self) -> dict[str, Any]` returns the non-empty fields, with sub=subject and approved_by as a list when non-empty.
4. Key helpers:
   - `_thumbprint_kid(jwk)`: sha256 of json.dumps({k: jwk[k] for k in ('crv', 'kty', 'x', 'y')}, separators=(',', ':'), sort_keys=True), hexdigest()[:32] (RFC 7638 members).
   - `issuer_of(org_slug, settings=None)` = f"{settings.public_url.rstrip('/')}/api/v1/orgs/{org_slug}/mcp-gateway".
   - `jwks_url_of(...)` = issuer + '/jwks.json'.
5. Key lifecycle:
   - `async def ensure_assertion_key(session, organisation, actor, store=None) -> str`: return the current kid, or create one by rotating.
   - `async def rotate_assertion_key(session, organisation, actor, store=None) -> tuple[str, str | None]`:
     - ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1()); PKCS8 PEM; public JWK (as_dict) + kid; remember_secret(pem);
     - store.put the PEM;
     - settings[SETTINGS_KEY] = {'current': kid, 'keys': [{'kid', 'jwk' (with use 'sig', alg 'ES256', kid), 'created_at', 'created_by'}]}, keeping only the new key and the previous current one;
     - store.delete the sealed PEM of any dropped key;
     - return (new, previous).
6. Reading and minting:
   - `jwks_of(organisation) -> dict`: {'keys': [jwk of each kept key]}, public members only, never 'd'.
   - `assertion_state(organisation, settings=None) -> McpGatewayAssertion`.
   - `async def signing_key(session, organisation, store=None) -> tuple[str, str]` (kid, pem): raise AssertionKeyMissing(f'the MCP gateway assertion key of {slug} is missing: an administrator rotates it on the Administration page') when there is no key, or when its PEM is lost (SecretIntrouvable, SecretStoreUnavailable).
   - `mint_assertion(kid, pem, *, issuer, audience, identity, now=None) -> str`: payload {'iss', 'aud', 'iat', 'exp': iat + ASSERTION_TTL_S, 'jti': uuid4().hex, **identity.claims()}, headers {'kid', 'typ': 'JWT'}, ES256.
7. routers/mcp_gateway.py (first line SPDX): `router = APIRouter(tags=['admin'])`, plus `_admin_organisation`, a copy of outbound_proxy.py L35-43 with the message 'the MCP gateway of {org} is managed by its org_admin'.
   - POST /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/assertion-key, operation_id rotateMcpGatewayAssertionKey:
     - require_store({'assertion_key': 'x'}), for the 409 without a secrets key, BEFORE any write;
     - rotate;
     - record audit 'mcp_gateway.assertion_key.rotate' with kid and previous;
     - return assertion_state.
   - GET /orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/jwks.json, operation_id getMcpGatewayJwks, response_model=None, session: DbPlateforme, NO principal:
     - look up the Organization by slug;
     - `if organisation is None or not jwks_of(organisation)['keys']: raise not_found('MCP gateway keys', org)`, one code path for both cases;
     - return JSONResponse(jwks_of(organisation), headers={'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=300'}).
8. main.py: add `mcp_gateway` to the routers import (alphabetical) and `mcp_gateway.router` after `outbound_proxy.router` in routeurs_du_coeur.
9. test_contract_conformance.py: remove rotateMcpGatewayAssertionKey and getMcpGatewayJwks from CONTRACT_ONLY, and the two paths '/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/assertion-key' and '/orgs/{org}/mcp-gateway/jwks.json' from CONTRACT_ONLY_PATHS.
10. Write the tests (fixtures client, admin, org, app from apps/api/tests/conftest.py; set_secret_store(FakeSecretStore()) in a fixture that restores None). Run the commands. PR with the template; STATUS row: proves key creation, rotation, publication, minting and the fail-closed lost key; does not prove that any call carries an assertion (S46-08, S46-09).

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Declare the JWKS route as machine-only in `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py` (`MACHINE_ONLY_ROUTES`, created by S47-17), with a one-line reason.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Anchors moved: routers/outbound_proxy.py `_admin_organisation` is L48-56 (the file now also holds the relay routes from L183; copy only the org_admin helper). deps.py `DbPlateforme` is defined at L77 (get_db_plateforme L58-75). config.py `public_url` is L293 (api_url L294). main.py: routers import L33-76 with outbound_proxy at L58; `routeurs_du_coeur` L324-366 with `outbound_proxy.router` at L352. schemas/__init__.py `from .outbound_proxy import (` at L203. Organization.settings is db/models.py L40. secret_store.py name pattern `_NAME` L50 and FakeSecretStore L298. services/connector_secrets.py require_store L25-32 and services/outbound_proxy.py L37 are unchanged. security.py now also has `mint_relay_token` (L146, ADR 0091), signed with the run-token key: do not take it as a model; this story still uses its own per-organisation key.
- ADR 0090 (landed: an organisation's slug is an alias, its id is its identity; 'no new persisted key may use the slug') applies to the issuer. The gateway compares `iss` verbatim, and a slug alias covers URLs but not a claim value, so the issuer must not change when the organisation is renamed. On the first key creation, store `issuer` (and `jwks_url`) in settings[SETTINGS_KEY]. `issuer_of`/`jwks_url_of` return the stored values when present, and the current-slug formula only before any key exists. The old JWKS URL keeps resolving through OrgAliasMiddleware (apps/api/src/choregos_api/org_alias_middleware.py) until the alias is released (releaseOrgAlias); say so in the docstring.
- Add `test_a_renamed_organisation_keeps_its_issuer_and_jwks_url`: create a key, rename the organisation with PATCH /api/v1/orgs/varga {slug: 'varga2'} (the pattern of apps/api/tests/test_org_slug_aliases.py), then check that GET /orgs/varga/mcp-gateway/jwks.json still answers 200 unauthenticated, and that a minted assertion's `iss` and assertion_state().issuer are unchanged.
- CallIdentity gains `org_id: str` (the organisation's id), emitted as an `org_id` claim beside `org` (the slug, for display), so that a gateway policy can key on an identity that survives a rename (ADR 0090 Decision 1). Its callers (S46-08 test route, S46-09 identity_of_*) pass organisation.id.
- OrgAliasMiddleware adds `Link: <current URL>; rel="canonical"` to every answer for a former slug, the JWKS 404 included. A keyless organisation reached by its alias is therefore distinguishable from an unknown slug by that header. Do not change the middleware (not this story's file). Keep the 404 body identical as the story says, and state this limit in the route's docstring and in the PR's Findings.
- GatewayRefusal's docstring cites ADR 0097 (the renumbered gateway ADR), not ADR 0088.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_an_admin_creates_a_key_and_the_jwks_publishes_it :: POST assertion-key -> 200 with one current key; a fresh AsyncClient with no cookie GETs jwks.json -> 200 with one EC P-256 key, the same kid, no 'd' member, Cache-Control 'public, max-age=300'
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_a_minted_assertion_verifies_against_the_jwks :: mint_assertion with CallIdentity(org='varga', subject='run:r1', purpose='tool_call', run='r1', agent='a@1') decodes with jwt.PyJWK(jwks['keys'][0]).key, audience 'gw', issuer issuer_of('varga'); exp - iat == 60; two mints have different jti
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_rotation_keeps_the_previous_key_and_drops_the_older :: three rotations leave two keys in jwks (the last two kids); the first kid's sealed PEM is gone from the FakeSecretStore
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_only_the_org_admin_rotates :: logged in as someone@elsewhere.dev (test_outbound_proxy.py L235 pattern), POST assertion-key -> 403
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_no_secrets_key_is_a_409 :: set_secret_store(FakeSecretStore(available=False)); POST -> 409, and the organisation's settings have no 'mcp_gateway_assertion' key
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_the_jwks_of_an_unknown_or_keyless_org_is_the_same_404 :: with a fresh unauthenticated client, /orgs/nope/mcp-gateway/jwks.json and /orgs/varga/mcp-gateway/jwks.json (no key yet) both answer 404 with the same title and the same detail once the slug is replaced
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_the_rotation_is_audited_without_key_material :: the audit row 'mcp_gateway.assertion_key.rotate' has the kid, and its payload contains no 'PRIVATE KEY' text and no 'd' member
- apps/api/tests/test_mcp_gateway_assertion.py :: test_signing_key_fails_closed_when_the_pem_is_lost :: after replacing the store with an empty FakeSecretStore, signing_key raises AssertionKeyMissing, which is a choregos_adapters.errors.ConfigurationError with code 'gateway_assertion_key_missing'
- apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py (existing) :: passes with the two operations served

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] No deployment-wide key and no config.py change.


**Do not.**
