### S47-04 · fix(api): make OIDC discovery mandatory and bind console identity to the provider subject

**Wave** 3 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-03`, `S47-07` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(api): make OIDC discovery mandatory and bind console identity to the provider subject`

**Goal.** A failed or foreign discovery document gives a 503 instead of a silent, cached Keycloak guess. An IdP without discovery is configured with three explicit endpoints, which the chart can render. A console sign-in is bound to the provider's `sub`, so an unverified or re-assigned e-mail cannot take over an existing account. ID-token claims (iss, aud, sub, nonce) are checked, and the decision not to verify the signature is recorded in an ADR.

**Context.**

apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/auth.py:
- L91 `_endpoints` cache.
- L94-125 `oidc_endpoints`: on any exception it falls back to the Keycloak layout (L106-110), caches it for the life of the process (L124), and never checks `issuer`. It returns the keys 'authorize', 'token' and 'userinfo'.
- L128-130 `oublier_la_decouverte`.
- L152-166 `_handshake` returns (state, cookie, challenge). The nonce is in both signed values but is never sent: the login params at L221-229 have no `nonce`.
- L169-183 `_auth_time` decodes the ID token without verifying it.
- L239-249 `_ensure_user` looks up `User.email == email` only.
- `callback` L323-386: identity comes from userinfo (L376-381), and its `sub` is never compared with the ID token's.

Model: `users.oidc_sub` is unique (db/models.py:46), so an e-mail change for an existing sub inserts a second row and fails with 500. The MCP door already does it right (mcp/appelant.py:134-139).

Tests mocking discovery without `issuer`:
- test_securite_authentification.py:57-65 and `_discovery()` at :177-182.
- test_coutures_identite.py:173-181.
- test_authentification_fraiche.py:48-56 (its `_id_token` helper at :28-34 carries `sub` only).
- test_mcp_oauth.py mocks its own issuer for the MCP door: unaffected.

S47-01 created test_session_signing.py, which unpacks `_handshake` into three values.

Chart: the API deployment renders CHOREGOS_OIDC_ISSUER/CLIENT_ID/CLIENT_SECRET/DEFAULT_ORG from `global.oidc` (deployment.yaml, near the CHOREGOS_BOOTSTRAP_ORG entries). New OIDC settings go in the API subchart values, because charts/choregos/values.yaml is a hot spot, and their names go into the `$apiOwned` dict from S47-01.

Verdict V5 partly: the fallback is an availability issue; the e-mail takeover is real.

**Files.**

- Create: `docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md`, `apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py`, `tests/charts/test_oidc_endpoints.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/auth.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/config.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_securite_authentification.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_coutures_identite.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_authentification_fraiche.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_session_signing.py`, `charts/choregos/charts/api/values.yaml`, `charts/choregos/charts/api/templates/deployment.yaml`, `docs/deployment.md`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`

**Steps.**

1. config.py: add `oidc_authorization_endpoint: str = ''`, `oidc_token_endpoint: str = ''` and `oidc_userinfo_endpoint: str = ''` (comment: for an IdP that publishes no discovery document; set all three or none). Add `oidc_require_email_verified: bool = True` (comment: an existing account is linked by e-mail only when the provider does not say `email_verified: false`; Entra omits the claim). Add a validator `_oidc_endpoints_all_or_none` raising ValueError naming the three variables when only one or two are set.
2. auth.py `oidc_endpoints`:
   - If the three settings are set, return them (no fetch).
   - Otherwise return the cached entry when present.
   - Otherwise GET `{issuer}/.well-known/openid-configuration` (timeout 5) and require HTTP 200, a JSON object, `str(document.get('issuer', '')).rstrip('/') == issuer`, and non-empty authorization_endpoint, token_endpoint and userinfo_endpoint.
   - On any failure, log a warning and raise `ApiError(status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, 'Identity provider unavailable', f'OIDC discovery at {issuer}/.well-known/openid-configuration failed: {reason}; set CHOREGOS_OIDC_AUTHORIZATION_ENDPOINT, CHOREGOS_OIDC_TOKEN_ENDPOINT and CHOREGOS_OIDC_USERINFO_ENDPOINT if the provider publishes none')`.
   - Cache only a success. Keep the returned keys 'authorize'/'token'/'userinfo'. Delete the `keycloak` dict and rewrite the docstring in English.
3. auth.py: `_handshake` returns `(state, cookie, challenge, nonce)`; `login` unpacks four values and adds `'nonce': nonce` to `params`. Update apps/api/tests/test_session_signing.py to unpack four values.
4. auth.py: add `_id_token_claims(id_token: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None` (the decode `_auth_time` does today) and make `_auth_time` use it (same name, same results). In `callback`:
   - After the token exchange: `claims = _id_token_claims(jetons.get('id_token'))`. When claims is not None, answer 401 unless `str(claims.get('iss', '')).rstrip('/') == issuer`, `settings.oidc_client_id` is the `aud` string or in the `aud` list, and either there is no 'nonce' claim or it equals `poignee.get('n')`.
   - After userinfo: `sub = info.get('sub')` must be a non-empty str (else 401). When claims is not None and `claims.get('sub') != sub`, answer 401 'the identity provider names two different subjects (ID token and userinfo)'.
5. auth.py: add `async def _ensure_oidc_user(session, info: dict[str, Any], settings: Settings) -> User`:
   (a) A user with `User.oidc_sub == sub` exists: set last_login_at. If its e-mail differs from info['email'] and no other user has the new e-mail, update it.
   (b) Else a user with `User.email == email` exists: if its oidc_sub is set and does not start with 'dev|', answer 401 'this e-mail belongs to another identity at the provider'. If settings.oidc_require_email_verified and `info.get('email_verified') is False`, answer 401 'the provider says this e-mail is not verified'. Otherwise set oidc_sub = sub and last_login_at.
   (c) Else create the User with oidc_sub = sub.
   Use it at L381 instead of `_ensure_user`. Keep `_ensure_user` for the development login (L341) and `terminer_la_connexion`.
6. Adapt the tests: add `'issuer': ISSUER` to the discovery JSON in test_securite_authentification.py L57-65 and `_discovery()` L177-182, in test_coutures_identite.py L173-181, and in test_authentification_fraiche.py L48-56. Make that file's `_id_token` helper add `'iss': ISSUER, 'aud': 'choregos'`. Then run `uv run pytest apps/api/tests -q` and give a discovery mock to any other test that drove /auth/login without one (it relied on the removed fallback).
7. Chart, charts/choregos/charts/api/values.yaml: add `oidcEndpoints: { authorization: '', token: '', userinfo: '' }` and `oidcRequireEmailVerified: true`, with comments.
   In deployment.yaml, right after the CHOREGOS_OIDC_DEFAULT_ORG entry:
   - Render CHOREGOS_OIDC_REQUIRE_EMAIL_VERIFIED always (`{{ .Values.oidcRequireEmailVerified | quote }}`).
   - Render the three CHOREGOS_OIDC_*_ENDPOINT entries only when `.Values.oidcEndpoints.authorization` is set, and fail the render when only some of the three are set.
   - Add the four names to the `$apiOwned` dict with the hints 'choregos-api.oidcEndpoints.<field>' and 'choregos-api.oidcRequireEmailVerified'.
8. docs/deployment.md '## Identity: what the OIDC provider must give us' (L759): discovery is required and its `issuer` must equal CHOREGOS_OIDC_ISSUER, or else set `choregos-api.oidcEndpoints`. userinfo must give `sub` and `email`. `email_verified: false` refuses linking an existing account by e-mail (`choregos-api.oidcRequireEmailVerified`). The Keycloak fallback is gone. docs/security.md row 'logging in without an identity' (L38): add the subject binding and the checked claims.
9. Write docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md, in the format of docs/adr/0087-*.md with '- **Status**: accepted, <today>':
   - Discovery is mandatory and its issuer is checked; explicit endpoints are the only alternative.
   - Identity is `sub`; an e-mail only links an unbound account, never when the provider calls it unverified.
   - The ID-token signature is not verified (code flow over TLS, OIDC Core §3.1.3.7), but iss, aud, the sub match and nonce are checked.
   - Rejected: JWKS verification for the console.
   Append its row at the end of docs/adr/README.md with status `accepted`. Write 'Release note' lines in the PR body (discovery mandatory, issuer must match).

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- ADR number: docs/adr/0096 is now taken ('a plugin's code runs as an MCP server the platform runs', ADR 0096). Create `docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md` instead (hardening 0096 -> 0100), and use 'ADR 0100' wherever the story says 'ADR 0096'. Before writing, check docs/adr/ on main and take the next free number if 0100 is taken. Append its README row after the last row present.
- routers/auth.py anchors at 3156d12 (one import line was added at L41, and alias resolution was added in `_map_groups_to_roles`): `_endpoints` L92; `oidc_endpoints` L94-126 (the keycloak dict L107-111, the cache write L125); `oublier_la_decouverte` L129-131; `_handshake` L153-167; `_auth_time` L170-184; `login` unpacks `_handshake` at L214 and builds `params` at L222-230; `_ensure_user` L240-250. `callback` starts at L335, with the dev login `_ensure_user` at L352, the token exchange at L375-377, userinfo at L387-391 and the OIDC `_ensure_user` at L392 (replace only this one with `_ensure_oidc_user`). `terminer_la_connexion` is L437-458.
- db/models.py: User is now L61-73, with the unique `oidc_sub` at L64 (not L46). mcp/appelant.py:134-139 is unchanged. Test anchors are unchanged: test_securite_authentification.py L57-65 and `_discovery()` L177-182, test_coutures_identite.py L173-181, test_authentification_fraiche.py L48-56 and `_id_token` L28-34. API deployment.yaml: CHOREGOS_OIDC_DEFAULT_ORG is at L242-243 (CHOREGOS_BOOTSTRAP_ORG at L246).
- Docs anchors: docs/deployment.md '## Identity: what the OIDC provider must give us' is now L852 (not L759). The docs/security.md row 'logging in without an identity' is now L40 (not L38).
- Do not touch `_map_groups_to_roles`: main added former-slug resolution there (ADR 0090, L294-302), and S47-05 owns it. routers/github_app.py (S42-16) now redirects a browser without a session to `/api/v1/auth/login?redirect_to=/api/v1/github/setup?...`, so the new 503 on a failed discovery also applies to that path. No code change is needed, and test_github_app.py does not follow the redirect.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_a_failed_discovery_is_an_error_and_is_not_cached :: discovery mocked 500 then 200 (with issuer): the first GET /api/v1/auth/login answers 503 and the second redirects to {ISSUER}/authorize. Today both redirect to the Keycloak path
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_a_discovery_document_for_another_issuer_is_refused :: a document whose issuer is https://other.example makes /auth/login answer 503
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_explicit_endpoints_skip_discovery :: with the three CHOREGOS_OIDC_*_ENDPOINT set and no discovery route mocked, /auth/login redirects to the configured authorization endpoint
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_an_unverified_email_does_not_take_over_an_account :: with User(email='victim@varga.dev', oidc_sub=None) seeded, a callback whose userinfo is {'sub': 'attacker-sub', 'email': 'victim@varga.dev', 'email_verified': False} answers 401 and the row's oidc_sub stays None. Today 307 with the victim's session
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_an_email_bound_to_another_subject_is_refused :: seeded oidc_sub 'victim-sub', userinfo sub 'attacker-sub' with email_verified true -> 401
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_the_id_token_and_userinfo_must_name_the_same_subject :: the token endpoint returns an id_token with sub 'x' (iss and aud right) while userinfo has sub 'u-1' -> 401
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_the_nonce_is_sent_and_a_wrong_one_is_refused :: the login redirect carries `nonce`; an id_token with a different nonce -> 401
- apps/api/tests/test_oidc_identity_binding.py :: test_a_returning_subject_keeps_its_account_after_an_email_change :: seeded oidc_sub 'u-1' with old@varga.dev; userinfo sub 'u-1' with new@varga.dev -> 307 and a single User with oidc_sub 'u-1' and e-mail new@varga.dev. Today 500 (IntegrityError)
- tests/charts/test_oidc_endpoints.py :: test_explicit_endpoints_are_rendered_together :: with all three choregos-api.oidcEndpoints set, the API container carries the three variables and CHOREGOS_OIDC_REQUIRE_EMAIL_VERIFIED='true'. With only `authorization` set, the render fails. By default no endpoint variable is rendered. Fails today
- apps/api/tests/test_securite_authentification.py, test_authentification_fraiche.py, test_coutures_identite.py (existing) :: green with `issuer` in their discovery mocks

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] /auth/login answers 503 when discovery fails or names another issuer, retries discovery on the next attempt, and works with explicit endpoints set in the chart.
- [ ] A console sign-in never attaches to an account bound to another `sub`, nor through an e-mail the IdP calls unverified.
- [ ] ID-token iss, aud and nonce, and the userinfo/ID-token `sub` match, are enforced. ADR 0100 records the signature decision.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row in the PR body: proves discovery, binding, claim checks and chart wiring against respx mocks and helm renders. Does not prove them against a real Keycloak or Entra tenant. 'Release note' lines in the PR body.

**Commands.**

```bash

uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make charts-lint
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not keep any Keycloak path fallback, and never cache a failed discovery.
- Do not verify the ID-token signature with JWKS, and do not touch mcp/oauth.py.
- Do not change the development login or `terminer_la_connexion` identity handling.
- Do not require `email_verified: true`: only an explicit false refuses.
- Do not edit charts/choregos/values.yaml (hot spot): new values go in the API subchart.
- Do not rename French identifiers; new ones are in English.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/deployment.md '## Identity: what the OIDC provider must give us'`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`

### S47-05 · fix(api): an IdP sign-in revokes the organisation roles the IdP granted and no longer maps

**Wave** 4 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-04` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(api): an IdP sign-in revokes the organisation roles the IdP granted and no longer maps`

**Goal.** A person removed from, or moved between, IdP groups loses at the next IdP sign-in the organisation roles the IdP had granted. The revocation is audited. Memberships granted by an administrator, the bootstrap or a project creation are never converted to IdP memberships and never revoked. The last org_admin of an organisation is never removed.

**Context.**

apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/auth.py:274-307 `_map_groups_to_roles`:
- L290 `roles = mappeur_de_groupes(roles_des_groupes)(groups, settings.oidc_default_org)`.
- L291-292 return early when `roles` is empty.
- L293-307 only add memberships, or overwrite an existing row's role (`elif ecraser: existing.role = ...`, L306-307). Nothing is ever deleted.

Callers:
- `callback` L382 (ecraser=True).
- The development login L343 (ecraser=False).
- `terminer_la_connexion` L447 (plugin/SAML, ecraser=True).

Membership (db/models.py:57-66) has no provenance column. Manual rows come from the bootstrap (apps/api/src/choregos_api/amorcage.py:50), the admin route (routers/admin.py:289), and project and org creation (routers/projects.py:95, :241).

Other facts:
- The last-admin guard to mirror is routers/admin.py:355-370.
- Alembic head at b3a7893: `e3a5c7b9d1f4` (migrations/versions/20261024_1000_drop_executors.py). test_migrations.py compares the migrations with the models.
- ADR 0100 was written by S47-04.

Verdict V6 confirmed. Critique: converting an existing manual row to `idp` when the IdP maps the same organisation would later revoke a bootstrap or admin grant, so manual rows stay manual.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/tests/test_idp_membership_revocation.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/db/models.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/auth.py`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/migrations/versions/20261029_1000_membership_source.py`

**Steps.**

1. db/models.py Membership (L57-66): add `source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default='manual', server_default='manual')` with an English `#:` comment: `idp` when an IdP sign-in created the row from the provider's groups, `manual` otherwise (administrator, bootstrap, project creator); a manual row is never revoked by a sign-in.
2. Create the migration, with header and docstring like 20261024_1000_drop_executors.py:
   - a fresh 12-hex `revision`;
   - `down_revision` = the single current head (e3a5c7b9d1f4 at b3a7893; if another migration landed, use the newest head);
   - upgrade: `op.add_column('memberships', sa.Column('source', sa.String(length=16), nullable=False, server_default='manual'))`;
   - downgrade: `op.drop_column('memberships', 'source')`.
3. auth.py `_map_groups_to_roles`:
   - Delete the early return at L291-292, and query `orgs` only when `roles` is non-empty.
   - When creating a Membership, pass `source='idp' if ecraser else 'manual'`.
   - Leave the `elif ecraser:` branch overwriting the role as today, but NEVER change `existing.source` (a manual row stays manual).
   - After the loop: `if ecraser: await _revoke_unmapped_memberships(session, user, set(roles))`.
   - Rewrite the docstring in English: the provider is authoritative, at each sign-in, for the roles it granted itself.
4. auth.py: add `async def _revoke_unmapped_memberships(session: Any, user: User, mapped: set[str]) -> None`. Select Membership joined to Organization where user_id == user.id, project_id IS NULL, source == 'idp' and Organization.slug is not in `mapped`. For each row:
   - If `row.role == str(Role.ORG_ADMIN)` and no OTHER org-level ORG_ADMIN membership exists in that organisation, keep it and `logger.warning('idp revocation skipped: last org_admin', org=slug, user=user.id)`.
   - Otherwise `await session.delete(row)` and `await record(session, None, 'member.revoke', org_id=row.org_id, target_type='user', target_id=user.id, role=row.role, canal='oidc', reason='no identity-provider group maps to this organisation any more')`.
5. docs/security.md: add the row 'keeping a role the identity provider withdrew'. At each IdP sign-in, organisation roles the IdP granted (`memberships.source = idp`) and no longer maps are removed and audited (`member.revoke`); the last org_admin is kept. Roles granted by people stay. Rows that existed before this release are manual, so roles withdrawn at the IdP before the upgrade are not revoked. Bearer tokens and open sessions keep their roles until that sign-in (finding linked).
6. docs/adr/0100-*.md: add '## Memberships follow the provider': the provenance column, revocation at sign-in only, manual rows never converted nor revoked, pre-migration rows manual. Rejected: deleting every unmapped membership (it would strip bootstrap admins on IdPs that emit no groups), and converting a manual row when the IdP maps the same organisation (a later group withdrawal would revoke an administrator's grant).
7. Open a `finding` issue (origin: S47-05): API tokens and sessions are not re-checked against the IdP between sign-ins (deps.py:150-175; tokens last up to 3650 days or never expire, schemas/identite.py:45).

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- The ADR this story amends is `docs/adr/0100-console-sign-in-binds-to-the-provider-subject.md`, created by S47-04 after renumbering (0096 is the plugin-servers ADR on main). Read 'ADR 0100' wherever the story says 'ADR 0096'.
- Alembic at 3156d12: the single head is `c9e1a3b5d7f8` (20261028_0900_plugin_servers.py), and the chain after e3a5c7b9d1f4 is a7c9e1b3d5f6 -> a8c0e2f4b6d9 -> f4b6d8a0c2e5 (20261028_1000_org_slug_aliases.py) -> d4f6a8c0e2b1 -> b8d0f2a4c6e7 -> c9e1a3b5d7f8. Set `down_revision` to the single head at implementation time (c9e1a3b5d7f8 unless a newer one landed). Name the file `20261029_1000_membership_source.py`, because the timestamp prefix 20261028_1000 is already used by org_slug_aliases.
- `_map_groups_to_roles` is now routers/auth.py L275-318 and resolves former slugs (ADR 0090). The early return is L292-293. The alias block L294-302 ends with `roles = resolu`, which holds CURRENT slugs. The orgs query is L303, `session.add(Membership(...))` is L316 and `elif ecraser:` is L317-318. Delete the early return and keep the alias block (it is a no-op on an empty dict). Pass `source='idp' if ecraser else 'manual'` at L316. Call `_revoke_unmapped_memberships(session, user, set(roles))` after the loop, on the alias-resolved `roles` and never on the raw mapper output. Otherwise a group that names a former slug would revoke the membership it maps.
- Add the test apps/api/tests/test_idp_membership_revocation.py :: test_a_group_naming_a_former_slug_keeps_its_role. Rename varga to fondation as in test_org_slug_aliases.py (its `_rename` helper), then run two OIDC sign-ins with ['choregos:varga:developers']. The (fondation, developer) membership stays, and no `member.revoke` audit row is written. It fails if the revocation compares unresolved slugs. test_org_slug_aliases.py::test_an_old_idp_group_still_maps_and_the_current_one_too (it calls `_map_groups_to_roles` with the default ecraser=True) must stay green.
- Other anchors: the callers are `callback` L393 (ecraser=True), the dev login L354 (False) and `terminer_la_connexion` L458 (True). The Membership model is db/models.py L74-87 (not 57-66). The manual creators are amorcage.py:51, routers/admin.py:289, routers/projects.py:102 (create_org) and :292 (project creation). The last-admin guard is routers/admin.py:355-368.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_idp_membership_revocation.py :: test_a_group_withdrawn_at_the_idp_withdraws_the_role :: two full OIDC sign-ins (respx mocks as in test_le_flux_oidc_verifie_state_et_pkce_et_borne_la_redirection, discovery with issuer) with groups ['choregos:varga:release-captains'] then []. /me memberships go from {(varga, release_captain)} to [], and audit_log holds one `member.revoke` row. Today the second /me still lists the role

### S47-06 · contract(projects): a project configuration carries a revision; a PATCH from a stale base answers 409

**Wave** 0 · **Priority** P0 · **Size** M · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** yes

**PR title:** `contract(projects): a project configuration carries a revision; a PATCH from a stale base answers 409`

**Goal.** Two editors of the same project configuration no longer silently overwrite each other. GET returns `config_revision`. A PATCH that sends `base_revision` from an older read answers 409 `stale_base` and writes nothing. PATCH and PUT /models lock the row on PostgreSQL. The console sends the revision it read and retries once. The audit entry names what changed. Clients that send no revision keep today's behaviour.

**Context.**

apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py:
- L301-323 `update_project` replaces the whole `config` (L314) with no precondition.
- Its audit `project.update` (L315-322) records no before/after.

Other code:
- routers/models.py:60-104 `put_models` does a second read-modify-write (L64 validates `ctx.project.config`).
- Neither path locks the row.
- schemas/projets.py:24-39 `ProjectDto`, :55-57 `ProjectUpdate`.
- services/projets.py:28-48 `project_dto`, which reads project.created_at/updated_at after the change.

Locking: `session.refresh(..., with_for_update=True)` expires and reloads the instance, which risks MissingGreenlet on later attribute access. Use `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` with `populate_existing`.

Contract, packages/contracts/openapi.yaml:
- /projects/{id} at L154-174 (updateProject responses 200/422).
- Project schema L3926-3942, ProjectUpdate L3967-3971.
- Problem schema with `code`/`latest_version` around L3886-3888.

Precedent: routers/agents.py:575-587 uses `conflict(detail, extra={'code': 'stale_base', ...})` (errors.py:78).

Web:
- apps/web/src/lib/project-config.ts:43-45 re-reads, then PATCHes without a precondition.
- apps/web/tests/project-config-patch.test.ts mocks `api.project` and `api.updateProject`.
- ApiError (api.ts:217-224) exposes `.status` and `.problem.code`.

Verdict V15 confirmed. Rejected: ETag/If-Match (header plumbing in the hot-spot api.ts) and a version column. This is a contract-change PR (ADR 0001): serialize it with other openapi.yaml PRs, S46 included.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/openapi.yaml`, `packages/contracts/ts/api.ts`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/schemas/projets.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/projets.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/models.py`, `apps/web/src/lib/project-config.ts`, `apps/web/tests/project-config-patch.test.ts`, `docs/usage.md`

**Steps.**

1. openapi.yaml:
   - In Project, add `config_revision` (type string, not required; description: an opaque digest of the name and configuration, to send back as `base_revision`).
   - In ProjectUpdate, add `base_revision` (type [string, 'null']; description: the config_revision the client read; when it is no longer current, the PATCH answers 409 `stale_base` and writes nothing; absent, the last write wins).
   - In the updateProject responses, add a 409 whose description says `code` is `stale_base` and the problem carries `latest_revision`; content application/problem+json, schema Problem.
   - In Problem, add `latest_revision` (type string; on stale_base from updateProject, the current config_revision) and mention updateProject's stale_base in the `code` description.
2. Run `make contracts` and commit the regenerated packages/contracts/ts files (only what the generator changes).
3. schemas/projets.py: ProjectDto gets `config_revision: str | None = None`; ProjectUpdate gets `base_revision: str | None = None`.
4. services/projets.py: add `def config_revision(name: str, config: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str` = the first 32 hex chars of the sha256 of `json.dumps({'name': name, 'config': config or {}}, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))`. `project_dto` sets `config_revision=config_revision(project.name, project.config)`.
5. routers/projects.py update_project:
   - Right after `ctx.require(Permission.PROJECT_WRITE)`, lock with `await session.execute(select(Project).where(Project.id == ctx.project.id).with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True))`. This is a row lock on PostgreSQL and a no-op on SQLite, and it refreshes the identity-map instance without expiring it.
   - Then `current = config_revision(ctx.project.name, ctx.project.config)`. If `body.base_revision is not None and body.base_revision != current`, raise `conflict('the project changed since you read it: reload, then apply your change again', extra={'code': 'stale_base', 'latest_revision': current})`.
   - Keep the groups/TOOLS_GRANT check.
   - Keep `before = dict(ctx.project.config or {})`, and pass to the audit `changes={key: {'before': before.get(key), 'after': after.get(key)} for each top-level key whose value differs}` and `name_changed=<bool>`.
6. routers/models.py put_models: right after `ctx.require(Permission.MODELS_WRITE)`, add the same `select(Project) ... with_for_update() ... populate_existing` lock before `ProjectConfig.model_validate(ctx.project.config)` (L64).
7. apps/web/src/lib/project-config.ts `patch()`: send `base_revision: latest.config_revision ?? null` with the config. On an `ApiError` with status 409 and `problem.code === 'stale_base'`, re-read the project once, re-merge and resend. A second 409 sets the error 'Someone else changed these settings: reload the page'. Update the JSDoc of `mergeConfig`.
8. docs/usage.md L479 paragraph: the console sends the revision it read; a concurrent change answers 409 `stale_base` and the console merges again once; API clients may send `base_revision`.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Anchors at 3156d12. routers/projects.py `update_project` is now L353-374: `ctx.require(Permission.PROJECT_WRITE)` is at L354 (put the FOR UPDATE lock right after it), the config is replaced at L365 and the `project.update` audit is at L366-373. routers/models.py `put_models` is L61-~105, with `ctx.require(Permission.MODELS_WRITE)` at L63 and `ProjectConfig.model_validate(ctx.project.config)` at L66. schemas/projets.py ProjectDto L24 and ProjectUpdate L55, and services/projets.py `project_dto` L28, are unchanged.
- openapi.yaml anchors (the plugin stream added ~460 lines): /projects/{id} is L172-192 (updateProject L180-188, responses 200/422 at L187-188), Problem is L4089 (`latest_version` at L4115), Project is L4154 and ProjectUpdate is L4195. The precedent `stale_base` is in routers/agents.py at L576-590 (code at L583). Run `make contracts` on top of the plugin stream's regenerated ts files.
- There is a new writer of projects.config on main: `update_org` (routers/projects.py:173-179) rewrites `config.org` of every project of an organisation whose slug changes (ADR 0090). A revision read before a rename therefore answers 409 stale_base once, and the console re-merges. This is expected. Do not lock there, and add one clause to the docs/usage.md paragraph (L479) saying that renaming the organisation also changes the revision.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py :: test_a_stale_base_revision_is_refused_and_nothing_is_lost :: GET -> revision R. PATCH {config: c1, base_revision: R} -> 200 with a new revision R2. PATCH {config: c2, base_revision: R} -> 409 with code stale_base and latest_revision R2. The GET config equals c1. Fails today
- apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py :: test_a_patch_without_base_revision_still_writes :: a PATCH without base_revision answers 200
- apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py :: test_the_revision_follows_the_configuration :: two GETs give the same revision; after a PATCH of `name` it differs
- apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py :: test_the_audit_entry_names_what_changed :: after a PATCH changing `notify`, the `project.update` audit payload's `changes` has the key 'notify' with before/after. Today there is no `changes`
- apps/web/tests/project-config-patch.test.ts :: it('sends the revision it read and retries once on stale_base') :: api.updateProject rejects first with ApiError(409, {code: 'stale_base'}) and then resolves. patch() returns true, api.project is called twice, and the second updateProject body carries the second revision
- apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py (existing) :: green after the contract change

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] GET/PATCH /projects/{id} return config_revision. A stale base_revision answers 409 stale_base with latest_revision and writes nothing. Without base_revision, behaviour is as before.
- [ ] The console never overwrites a concurrent edit without re-merging it.
- [ ] project.update audit rows list the changed top-level keys with before/after.
- [ ] make ci, make web-ci and make contracts-check green. STATUS row: proves the precondition and the retry by tests, and runs the new test file on PostgreSQL when available. Does not prove the row lock under real concurrency.

**Commands.**

```bash
make contracts
make contracts-check
uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py -q
CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES=1 uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_project_config_revision.py -q  # with a PostgreSQL
uv run pytest apps/api/tests -q
make web-ci
uv run ruff check packages apps/api apps/orchestrator tools tests && uv run ruff format --check packages apps/api apps/orchestrator tools tests
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not lock with `session.refresh(..., with_for_update=True)`: it expires the instance.
- Do not use ETag/If-Match headers, and do not add a DB column or a migration.
- Do not make config_revision required in the schema.
- Do not edit apps/web/src/lib/api.ts, types.ts or mocks/data.ts (hot spots).
- Do not change the request body of PUT /projects/{id}/models.
- Do not hand-edit packages/contracts/ts: regenerate it with make contracts.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/usage.md '### Project settings' (L476-482)`

### S47-07 · test(architecture): ratchet the imports that cross layers

**Wave** 2 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `test(architecture): ratchet the imports that cross layers`

**Goal.** Today's crossing imports are frozen and new ones are refused, each by its own guard: orchestrator->api, packages->api, services->routers, private names imported between routers, and copies of the organisation lookup. A guard fails only when its count rises, so a removal made by another track never breaks it. Router scans are recursive and exempt imports inside one router package, so the later package splits stay covered. The rules and the hand-off procedure are in an ADR and in docs/development.md.

**Context.**

Counts re-measured with ast at b3a7893:
- 149 import statements of choregos_api in apps/orchestrator/src (28 files). apps/orchestrator/pyproject.toml:10 depends on choregos-api.
- 22 in packages/*/src, all under packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/ (actions.py, api.py, plugin.py, store.py, tools.py).
- 1 services->routers import: services/readiness.py:740.
- 7 router->router ImportFrom statements importing private names: agent_realtime.py:52, agent_releases.py:34, agent_sessions.py:29, agents.py:1785, connector_catalogue.py:20, mcp_catalogue.py:44, skill_catalogue.py:31.
- 12 module-level organisation lookups: `_organisation` in agents.py:138, budget_alerts.py:23, egress.py:41, github_app.py:43, knowledge.py:92, memories.py:50, model_catalogue.py:47, org_connectors.py:79, remote_credentials.py:93, skills.py:68 and workflows.py:471, plus `_org` in projects.py:168.

Pytest runs with `--import-mode=importlib` (pyproject.toml:153). API test helpers are imported relatively (`from .catalogue_kit import project_only`).

Pattern: apps/api/tests/test_un_seul_constructeur_de_ticket.py (ast walk).

The critique, applied:
- Equality ratchets would turn another track's CI red on a removal. The guards are `count <= CEILING`, and the stories that remove imports lower CEILING, so their tests still fail without the change.
- Non-recursive scans would stop seeing routers/agents.py and knowledge.py once S47-29/30 turn them into packages.

Rejected: import-linter (new dependency, config hot spot), module allowlists (S45 renames would break them), equality.

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/architecture/__init__.py`, `tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py`, `tests/architecture/test_packages_imports_of_the_api.py`, `apps/api/tests/layering_kit.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_layering_router_private_imports.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py`, `docs/adr/0099-the-api-is-a-dependency-that-only-shrinks.md`
- Modify: `docs/development.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`

**Steps.**

1. tests/architecture/__init__.py: empty (tests/ci/__init__.py convention).
2. tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py:
   - `def count_api_imports(root: Path) -> int` counts, over root.rglob('*.py'), every ast.ImportFrom with level 0 whose module is 'choregos_api' or starts with 'choregos_api.', plus each alias of an ast.Import whose name is or starts with 'choregos_api.'.
   - `CEILING = 149`, with a comment: measured at b3a7893; lower it in the PR that removes imports; raise it only for a mechanical rename that splits an existing statement, saying so in the PR body, or by amending ADR 0099.
   - test_the_orchestrator_imports_no_more_of_the_api asserts `count <= CEILING` with the message 'apps/orchestrator imports choregos_api {count} times, the ceiling is {CEILING}: a new import is refused (ADR 0099: move shared code to packages/core)'.
   - test_the_orchestrator_never_imports_the_http_layer: no imported module starts with choregos_api.routers, choregos_api.deps, choregos_api.main, choregos_api.mcp or choregos_api.a2a, and none is fastapi.
   - test_the_counter_sees_both_import_forms: on a tmp_path file holding `from choregos_api.db import x` and `import choregos_api.config`, the counter returns 2.
3. tests/architecture/test_packages_imports_of_the_api.py: every file under packages/*/src that imports choregos_api must be under packages/ontology/src/choregos_ontology/service/. The total must be <= 22, with the same message shape. Include a self-test on tmp_path.
4. apps/api/tests/layering_kit.py (English docstrings):
   - `API_PACKAGE = Path(...)/'src'/'choregos_api'`.
   - `def module_of(path: Path, package_dir: Path, package: str) -> str`: the dotted module of a file, `__init__` collapsed.
   - `def resolve(path, package_dir, package, node: ast.ImportFrom) -> str | None`: the absolute module of an ImportFrom. level 0 returns node.module; level n drops n-1 trailing parts of the file's package (its module for an __init__.py, its module minus the last part otherwise) and appends node.module.
   - `def router_unit(module: str, package: str) -> str | None`: for '<package>.routers.X' or '<package>.routers.X.anything' returns 'X', otherwise None.
5. apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py: collect the modules under choregos_api outside routers/ and other than main.py that import (resolved) choregos_api.routers or a submodule. `CEILING = 1`, asserted with `<=` and the ratchet message. Self-test: a synthetic two-module package on tmp_path whose services/a.py does `from ..routers.x import y` gives 1.
6. apps/api/tests/test_layering_router_private_imports.py:
   - Scan routers/**/*.py RECURSIVELY.
   - Count each ImportFrom whose resolved module has a router_unit different from the importing file's own router_unit and that imports at least one name starting with '_'. Imports inside the same router package (`from ._router import router`, `from .helpers import _x` within routers/agents/) are allowed.
   - `CEILING = 7`, asserted with `<=`.
   - Self-test on tmp_path with routers/a.py (`from .b import _x`, counted), routers/b.py, routers/c/__init__.py (`from ._r import _y`, not counted) and routers/c/_r.py: expects 1.
7. apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py: recursively count the module-level FunctionDef/AsyncFunctionDef named `_organisation` or `_org` in routers/**/*.py. `CEILING = 12`, asserted with `<=` and the message 'use services.organisations.organisation_by_slug (S47-09)'. Self-test included.
8. docs/development.md, before '## Known pitfalls' (L535), add the section '## Layering: what may import what':
   - The orchestrator and plugins may import the API's persistence and services but never its HTTP layer.
   - Services never import routers.
   - Routers share helpers through services.
   - There is one organisation lookup.
   - The five guards, and the hand-off rule: a PR from ANY track that removes a counted import lowers that guard's CEILING in the same PR; a PR that adds one is refused unless it is a mechanical rename splitting a statement (raise CEILING by that number and say so in the PR body) or ADR 0099 is amended.
9. Write docs/adr/0099-the-api-is-a-dependency-that-only-shrinks.md (accepted, today):
   - Context: 149/22/1/7/12.
   - Decision: ratchets by statement count with `<=`; hard bans on the HTTP layer; recursive router scans with intra-package imports exempt; extraction to packages/core, or a future persistence package, as the way down.
   - Rejected alternatives: import-linter, module allowlists, equality ratchets across parallel tracks, extracting choregos-persistence now.
   Append the README row (status accepted).

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- ADR number: 0095 is taken on main (SkillSpector). Create `docs/adr/0099-the-api-is-a-dependency-that-only-shrinks.md` instead, and replace every 'ADR 0095' in the story with 'ADR 0099': the ratchet failure messages, the CEILING comments, the docs/development.md section and the README row. Check docs/adr/ first and take the next free number if 0099 is taken.
- Counts re-measured with ast at 3156d12. The orchestrator has 150 import statements of choregos_api in 28 files, not 149: the plugin stream added apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/base.py:23 `from choregos_api.services.plugin_tracker import pin_plugin_tracker`. Use `CEILING = 150`, commented 'measured at 3156d12'. The HTTP-layer ban still holds (no routers/deps/main/mcp/a2a/fastapi import). The packages count is still 22, all in the same 5 files under choregos_ontology/service; choregos_core.plugins, choregos_adapters.plugin_servers and choregos_adapters.tracker.plugin import no choregos_api. services->routers is still 1 (services/readiness.py:740). Router private imports are still the same 7 statements at the same lines.
- The organisation lookups are still 12 helpers, now at agents.py:138, budget_alerts.py:23, egress.py:41, github_app.py:44, knowledge.py:95, memories.py:50, model_catalogue.py:47, org_connectors.py:80, projects.py:217 (`_org`), remote_credentials.py:94, skills.py:68 and workflows.py:471. routers/plugin_sources.py:28-30 has an inline organisation select. It is not a helper, so the ratchet does not count it; S47-09 replaces it.
- Vocabulary (ADR 0093 Decision 1: 'plugin' now means the ADR 0093 package, and entry-point packages are 'extensions'). In docs/development.md '## Layering' and in ADR 0099, write 'the orchestrator and extensions (entry-point packages such as choregos_ontology) may import the API's persistence and services', not 'plugins'. docs/development.md '## Known pitfalls' is still L535.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py :: test_the_counter_sees_both_import_forms :: the counter returns 2 on a synthetic file; fails if the detector is broken
- tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py :: test_the_orchestrator_imports_no_more_of_the_api :: count <= 149; fails on any added import
- tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py :: test_the_orchestrator_never_imports_the_http_layer :: no routers/deps/main/mcp/a2a/fastapi import in apps/orchestrator/src
- tests/architecture/test_packages_imports_of_the_api.py :: test_only_the_ontology_service_imports_the_api :: only choregos_ontology/service imports choregos_api, at most 22 times; self-test included
- apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py :: test_services_do_not_import_routers :: at most 1 offender (services/readiness.py); the self-test resolves `from ..routers.x import y`
- apps/api/tests/test_layering_router_private_imports.py :: test_routers_import_no_private_names_from_each_other :: at most 7 statements; the self-test proves the recursion and the intra-package exemption
- apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py :: test_one_organisation_lookup :: at most 12 helpers, recursive; self-test
- tests/docs/test_index_des_adr.py (existing) :: green with the new ADR row

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Each ratchet passes at HEAD, fails on a new crossing import, and does not fail on a removal. Each detector has a self-test. The router scans are recursive with the intra-package exemption proven.
- [ ] ADR 0099 and docs/development.md state the rules and the cross-track hand-off rule.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row in the PR body: proves the freeze by tests; does not reduce any coupling (S47-08/09/10 do).

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest tests/architecture apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py apps/api/tests/test_layering_router_private_imports.py apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py tests/docs -q
uv run ruff check packages apps/api apps/orchestrator tools tests && uv run ruff format --check packages apps/api apps/orchestrator tools tests
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not add import-linter or any dependency.
- Do not put all ratchets in one file: the reducing stories run in parallel.
- Do not assert equality: use `<=`.

### S47-08 · refactor(api): services stop importing routers; the backend capability table moves to core

**Wave** 3 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S47-07` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(api): services stop importing routers; the backend capability table moves to core`

**Goal.** The only services->routers import, where readiness reads the backend list from the admin router, disappears. The six backends and their capabilities are declared once in choregos_core.models, and both the admin router and readiness read them from there.

**Context.**

routers/admin.py:49-56 `KNOWN_BACKENDS = [('claude-code', ['acp', 'mcp', 'agents_md', 'structured_output']), ('codex', ['acp', 'mcp', 'agents_md']), ('gemini-cli', ['acp', 'mcp']), ('goose', ['acp', 'mcp']), ('opencode', ['acp', 'mcp']), ('copilot-cli', ['acp'])]`, read at :81.

packages/core/src/choregos_core/models.py:63-70 `KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES` repeats the six names in the same order.

services/readiness.py:739-749 `_usable_backends` does `from ..routers.admin import KNOWN_BACKENDS` (L740) and iterates over the names only (L746).

Ratchet from S47-07: apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py (CEILING = 1, `<=`).

Verdict V11 confirmed.

1. packages/core/src/choregos_core/models.py: above `KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES`, add `KNOWN_BACKEND_CAPABILITIES: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...]` holding the six pairs of admin.py:49-56 in the same order, with an English comment: the ACP backends the runner image ships and what each can do. Redefine `KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES = tuple(name for name, _ in KNOWN_BACKEND_CAPABILITIES)`, keeping its annotation; rewrite its French comment in English (the comment lines are touched).
2. routers/admin.py L49-56: `KNOWN_BACKENDS = [(name, list(capabilities)) for name, capabilities in KNOWN_BACKEND_CAPABILITIES]`, with `from choregos_core.models import KNOWN_BACKEND_CAPABILITIES`. L81 is unchanged.
3. services/readiness.py `_usable_backends`: delete the L740 import, import KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES from choregos_core.models, and iterate `for name in KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES`.
4. apps/api/tests/test_layering_services_do_not_import_routers.py: set CEILING = 0 and reword the message as a hard rule (services never import routers).

- Existing tests of GET /api/v1/platform/backends and readiness :: answers unchanged

### S47-09 · refactor(api): one organisation lookup shared by every router

**Wave** 7 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-01`, `S47-06`, `S47-07`, `S47-11` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(api): one organisation lookup shared by every router`

**Goal.** The 11 copies of `select(Organization).where(Organization.slug == slug)` plus a 404 in the routers become one service function. The copy in org_connectors.py, which S46 owns, is left to S47-10. Error texts stay byte-identical.

**Context.**

Identical helpers (verified):
- `_organisation(session, slug)` in routers/agents.py:138, budget_alerts.py:23, egress.py:41, github_app.py:43, knowledge.py:92, memories.py:50, model_catalogue.py:47, remote_credentials.py:93 and skills.py:68.
- `_org(session, slug)` in projects.py:168 (it has an isinstance check).
- workflows.py:471 `_organisation(session, principal, org)` checks membership first (`forbidden('reading the policy ceiling of {org} requires being a member of it')`), because of RLS, then selects.
All of them raise `not_found('Organisation', slug)`.

Router imports of these helpers:
- agent_releases.py:34 and agent_sessions.py:29 import `_organisation` from .agents.
- skill_catalogue.py:31 imports it from .skills.
- connector_catalogue.py:20 and mcp_catalogue.py:44 import it from .org_connectors (S46 area: left to S47-10).

Ratchet from S47-07: apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py (CEILING 12, `<=`, recursive).

These files were also touched earlier by S47-01 (remote_credentials.py), S47-06 and S47-11 (projects.py), and S47-11 (workflows.py). Written before the S45 rename of apps/api. Verdict V11 confirmed.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/organisations.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_organisation_by_slug.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agents.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agent_releases.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agent_sessions.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/budget_alerts.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/egress.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/github_app.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/knowledge.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/memories.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/model_catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/remote_credentials.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/skills.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/skill_catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/workflows.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/plugin_sources.py`

**Steps.**

1. Create services/organisations.py with `async def organisation_by_slug(session: AsyncSession, slug: str) -> Organization`, which selects by slug and raises `not_found('Organisation', slug)`. English docstring: the one organisation lookup of the routers; callers that must check membership first do it before calling, because RLS hides the row otherwise.
2. In agents.py, budget_alerts.py, egress.py, github_app.py, knowledge.py, memories.py, model_catalogue.py, remote_credentials.py and skills.py: delete the local `_organisation` definition and replace every `_organisation(session, x)` call with `organisation_by_slug(session, x)`, imported from ..services.organisations.
3. projects.py: delete `_org` (L168-172) and call organisation_by_slug at its call sites.
4. workflows.py L471: rename the helper to `_member_organisation(session, principal, org)`. Keep the membership check first, then `return await organisation_by_slug(session, org)`. Update its callers.
5. agent_releases.py:34, agent_sessions.py:29 and skill_catalogue.py:31: drop `_organisation` from the private import lists and import organisation_by_slug from ..services.organisations instead.
6. apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py: CEILING 12 -> 1 (org_connectors.py, removed by S47-10).
7. Run `uv run python tools/check_english.py`: any comment line you touch must be English.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Anchors at 3156d12: `_organisation` is at agents.py:138, budget_alerts.py:23, egress.py:41, github_app.py:44, knowledge.py:95, memories.py:50, model_catalogue.py:47, remote_credentials.py:94, skills.py:68 and workflows.py:471 (membership-checked). `_org` is projects.py L217-221. Its call sites are now L145 (update_org), L208 (`release_org_alias`, new, ADR 0090), L226 and L266. Replace all of them. The bodies are still byte-identical to the story's description.
- Also replace the inline lookup added by the plugin stream in routers/plugin_sources.py:28-30 (`select(Organization.id).where(Organization.slug == org)` followed by `not_found('Organisation', org)`) with `await organisation_by_slug(session, org)`. The 404 text is identical. Drop the now unused `select`/`Organization` imports there, and add the file to the story. Leave services/plugin_installer.py `org_id_of` (L806-812, a services lookup) and db/org_aliases.py alone, as for the other service-side selects listed in Do not.
- `organisation_by_slug` must NOT resolve former slugs, so that behaviour stays byte-identical. OrgAliasMiddleware (ADR 0090) rewrites `/api/v1/orgs/<alias>/…` to the current slug before routing, and slugs from payloads or signed states are resolved by callers through `db.org_aliases.current_slug` (e.g. remote_credentials.py:363, github_app.py setup) or `organisation_by_slug_or_alias`. Say this in its docstring, so nobody 'fixes' it into an alias lookup.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py :: test_one_organisation_lookup (CEILING 1) :: fails today with 12
- apps/api/tests/test_organisation_by_slug.py :: test_an_unknown_organisation_is_the_same_404 :: organisation_by_slug(session, 'nope') raises an ApiError with status 404 whose detail equals not_found('Organisation', 'nope').detail; it returns the row for 'varga'
- Existing route tests of agents, skills, memories, knowledge, egress, budget alerts, model catalogue, github app, remote credentials, projects and the policy ceiling :: statuses and texts unchanged

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Exactly one organisation lookup helper, in services. Routers keep identical statuses and messages.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row: proves the deduplication by the ratchet; changes no behaviour.

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py apps/api/tests/test_organisation_by_slug.py -q
uv run pytest apps/api/tests -q
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not touch routers/org_connectors.py, connector_catalogue.py or mcp_catalogue.py (S46-01 area; S47-10 does it).
- Do not move the membership check in workflows.py after the lookup (RLS).
- Do not replace the inline selects in services/memoire.py, memory_access.py, amorcage.py, a2a/door.py or knowledge/sync.py.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

### S47-10 · refactor(api): routers share helpers through services, never through each other's private names

**Wave** 12 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S47-09`, `S47-03`, `S47-05`, `S46-01`, `S46-09`, `S46-10` · **Contract change** no

**Goal.** No router imports a `_`-prefixed name from another router. Error texts and status codes stay byte-identical.

**Context.**

- agent_realtime.py:52 imports `_charger, _exiger, _projet` from .agents.
- agent_releases.py:34 imports `Slug, _charger, _dto, _exiger, _lire` from .agents (`_organisation` was removed by S47-09).
- agent_sessions.py:29 imports `_charger, _exiger, _lire, _projet` from .agents.
- agents.py:1785 imports `_token_dto` from .auth.
- connector_catalogue.py:20 imports `_organisation` from .org_connectors.
- mcp_catalogue.py:44 imports `_administrer, _organisation, declare_org_connector` from .org_connectors.
- skill_catalogue.py:31 imports `_dto, _lire, _skill_sources, _snapshot` from .skills.

Definitions:
- routers/agents.py: `_agent` L147-154, `_versions` L156-161, `_version_dto` L163-186, `_pins` L188-203, `_upstream` L205-224, `_dto` L226-267, `_lire` L285-287, `_exiger` L295-300, `_charger` L303-314, `_projet` L317-323.
- routers/skills.py: `_lire` L91-94, `_version_dto` L111-125, `_dto` L127, `_skill_sources` L252-272, `_snapshot` L347-356.
- routers/org_connectors.py: `_organisation` L79 and `_administrer` L88, whose docstring L89-90 is French. S46-01 may have renamed symbols here: use the names it left.
- routers/auth.py: `_token_dto` L546-556 and `_projets_lies` L559-568 (French docstring at L560).

Anchors shift after S47-09: grep for the names.

tools/check_english.py reads docstrings, comments and string literals on ADDED lines, and every line moved into a new services module is an added line.

Ratchets: test_layering_router_private_imports.py (CEILING 7) and test_one_organisation_lookup.py (CEILING 1).

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/agent_access.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/agent_views.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/skill_views.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/org_connector_access.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/api_tokens.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agents.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agent_realtime.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agent_releases.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/agent_sessions.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/skills.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/skill_catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/org_connectors.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/connector_catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/mcp_catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/auth.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_layering_router_private_imports.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py`

**Steps.**

1. services/agent_access.py: move these helpers from routers/agents.py and rename them:
   - `_lire` -> `require_agent_reader(principal, org)`
   - `_exiger` -> `enforce_decision(decision)`
   - `_agent` -> `agent_in_org(session, organisation, slug)`
   - `_charger` -> `load_visible_agent(session, principal, org, slug)` (it calls organisation_by_slug)
   - `_projet` -> `project_in_org(session, organisation, slug)`
   Type the parameters with `Principal` (rbac) instead of the `Me` dependency alias.
2. services/agent_views.py: move `_versions` -> `agent_versions`, `_version_dto` -> `agent_version_dto`, `_pins` -> `agent_pins`, `_upstream` -> `agent_upstream` and `_dto` -> `agent_dto` (keyword `with_versions` replaces `avec_versions`), with the imports they need.
3. services/skill_views.py: move from routers/skills.py `_lire` -> `require_skill_reader`, `_version_dto` -> `skill_version_dto`, `_dto` -> `skill_dto`, `_skill_sources` -> `skill_sources`, `_snapshot` -> `skill_snapshot`, plus any private helper only they use.
4. services/org_connector_access.py: `_administrer` -> `require_connector_admin(principal, org)`. In org_connectors.py, connector_catalogue.py and mcp_catalogue.py, replace `_organisation` with services.organisations.organisation_by_slug and `_administrer` with require_connector_admin. 8. Ratchets: test_layering_router_private_imports.py CEILING 7 -> 0, and test_one_organisation_lookup.py CEILING 1 -> 0. Reword both messages as hard rules.

S47-09 will have shifted them, so grep the names.

Replacing `_administrer`/`_organisation` there must leave those branches and their messages byte-identical. ### S47-11 · feat(core): governance the platform does not enforce is refused when written, not warned

**Wave** 6 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-04`, `S47-06`, `S47-20`, `S45-02` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(core): governance the platform does not enforce is refused when written, not warned`

**Goal.** Governance that is accepted but not enforced becomes a refusal at write time. A workflow that declares `review.humans` (other than `required: never`), or names `review.agents` that never run, is invalid. A policy that writes `approvals.*`, `attempts.default_max` or `review.require_human_for_risk` is refused at PUT and when a project is created from a template. Lazy provisioning and stored documents keep loading. The shipped `advanced` template stops promising a maintainer review and is bumped to version 3.

**Context.**

Workflow contract:
- packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/workflow.py:152-159 defines `ReviewHumans{group, required: always|by_policy|never}` and `ReviewSpec{agents, humans}`. `humans` has no reader.
- `review.agents` is read only by the validator (validator.py:719-724 counts it as 'used', which hides `actor.unused`) and by dsl/edition.py:642. Nothing schedules review agents.

Templates:
- packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/templates/advanced.yaml: L7 `version: 2`, L1-2 French header comments, L94 `review: { agents: [reviewer], humans: { group: maintainers, required: by_policy } }`.
- A scan of templates/*/workflows/*.yaml and dsl/templates finds no other offender.
- Changing advanced.yaml content without bumping the version would show a template-drift fact in readiness (services/readiness.py:835-856 compares checksums).

Validator and policy:
- dsl/validator.py:65-87 `validate_workflow`, :683-740 `_check_warnings`; `ValidationReport.error(code, message, path, source)` at L48.
- packages/core/src/choregos_core/policy.py:195 `CLES_D_APPROBATION`, :198-260 `policy_warnings`.

Write paths:
- routers/workflows.py:414-426 `put_policy`.
- services/definitions.py:264-307 `ensure_defaults`, whose policy branch at L295-306 calls `signaler_ce_que_la_politique_n_applique_pas`. It is called at project creation (routers/projects.py:239) AND lazily from read paths (routers/workflows.py:177, :377), so a refusal inside it would turn unrelated GETs into 422.

Load path: definitions.py:353-365 `workflow_model` calls `parse_workflow(row.yaml)` STRICT for rows whose json_doc lacks `initial` (L364).

Tests:
- packages/core/tests/test_policy.py:134-180, test_attempts_ceiling.py:30-31 and test_policy_ceiling_network.py:106-108 use `policy_warnings` (keep its behaviour).
- apps/api/tests/test_politique_sans_gardes_fantomes.py asserts the logged warning on PUT.

docs/usage.md:2015-2021 describes the warnings. Verdict V12 confirmed, plus the review.agents finding. projects.py was edited earlier by S47-06 (dependency).

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/tests/test_unenforced_governance.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_unenforced_policy_refused.py`, `docs/adr/0101-governance-the-platform-does-not-enforce-is-refused.md`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/validator.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/dsl/templates/advanced.yaml`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/policy.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/workflows.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/definitions.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_politique_sans_gardes_fantomes.py`, `docs/usage.md`, `docs/adr/0044-people-are-declared-in-the-workflow.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`

**Steps.**

1. validator.py: add `_check_unenforced_review(wf, report, source)` and call it in `validate_workflow` before `_check_warnings`. For each (index, t) with `t.review`:
   - If `t.review.humans` and `t.review.humans.required != 'never'`: `report.error('review.humans_not_enforced', '`review.humans` is not enforced: Choregos asks no one for this review. Declare a human actor and a transition it answers (ADR 0044).', ['transitions', index, 'review', 'humans'], source)`.
   - For each agent in `t.review.agents` other than `t.by`: `report.error('review.agent_not_run', f'`{agent}` is named in `review.agents` of `{t.id}` but never runs: only the transition`s `by` actor runs. Give it its own transition.', ['transitions', index, 'review', 'agents'], source)`.
2. validator.py `_check_warnings` L719-724: stop counting `t.review.agents` as a use of an actor; only `t.by` and `train.approval` count.
3. advanced.yaml: L94 becomes `review: { agents: [reviewer] }`; L7 becomes `version: 3`. Rewrite the two header comment lines (L1-2) in English, without promising a human review (check_english reads touched lines).
4. policy.py: add `UNENFORCED_GOVERNANCE_CODES = frozenset({'policy.approval_not_enforced', 'policy.default_max_not_enforced', 'policy.review_not_enforced'})` and `def unenforced_governance(policy: Policy) -> list[Issue]`, returning the `policy_warnings` issues with those codes. ### S47-12 · fix(api): a catalogue tool that may write is neither listed nor called unless the deployment marks it read-only

**Wave** 8 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-01`, `S47-09`, `S47-13` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(api): a catalogue tool that may write is neither listed nor called unless the deployment marks it read-only`

**Goal.** Writes reach a third party only as governed actions (ADR 0034 §7). A deployment catalogue tool that may write (an http POST, or a remote MCP tool) is dropped from what a project may call, unless the deployment declares it `read_only: true`. That covers the run's tool list, the console's project tools page and the orchestrator's observability sources. A direct call answers 409 with the way out. Governed writes remain the job of organisation MCP connectors.

**Context.**

packages/core/src/choregos_core/catalogue.py: `AppelHttp.method: Literal['GET', 'POST'] = 'GET'` (L57); `AppelMcp` L65-82; `OutilCatalogue` L85-110 has no read/write flag.

apps/api/src/choregos_api/catalogue.py:39-57 `outils_du_projet(autorises, groupes)` is the single filter used by:
- routers/internal.py:825 (GET /runs/{id}/tools, getRunTools);
- internal.py:879 (call_tool);
- the orchestrator's observability sources (apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/stage.py:889-894, activity code, no replay).

Other facts:
- internal.py:870-966 `call_tool` takes the catalogue branch `elif outil is not None:` (~L922-931), which calls the tool directly.
- routers/projects.py:265-298 `get_project_tools` (getProjectTools) lists the whole catalogue with `allowed`. Its contract (openapi.yaml:3145-3175) has fixed item properties, so use `allowed: false` and add no field.
- Tests calling write-capable catalogue tools directly: apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_outils.py (e.g. :356), test_greffons_outils.py, catalogue_kit.py.

The gateway decision (made by the plan, not by you): Choregos consumes the external MCP gateway as organisation MCP connectors (the broker, ADR 0034), never as deployment-catalogue `mcp:` entries. If S46 stories merged before this one added catalogue entries for the gateway, mark the read-only ones `read_only: true` where they are declared and leave the writers refused.

internal.py imports courtier helpers that S46-01 renamed (services/broker.py): use the English names. projects.py was edited by S47-06/S47-11/S47-09.

Verdict V17 partly.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_write_is_refused.py`, `docs/adr/0034-connectors-by-capability.md`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/catalogue.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/internal.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_outils.py`, `docs/deployment.md`, `docs/security.md`

**Steps.**

1. packages/core catalogue.py, OutilCatalogue: add `read_only: bool | None = None`. Its English `#:` comment: the deployment's statement that the tool only reads; unset, a GET http tool reads, while a POST or a remote MCP tool may write. Add a property `may_write` returning `self.read_only is False or (self.read_only is None and (self.mcp is not None or (self.http is not None and self.http.method != 'GET')))`.
2. apps/api catalogue.py `outils_du_projet`: add a keyword `include_writers: bool = False`. Unless it is set, drop tools whose `may_write` is True. Every existing caller (run tool list, observability sources) thereby stops seeing writers. Update the docstring in English.
3. internal.py call_tool: look the tool up with `outils_du_projet(..., include_writers=True)` (L879). At the start of the `elif outil is not None:` branch: `if outil.may_write: raise ApiError(status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, 'Catalogue write refused', f'catalogue tool `{name}` may write (an http POST or a remote MCP tool) and catalogue calls are not governed actions: the deployment marks it `read_only: true` if it only reads, or the organisation declares the server as an MCP connector, whose writes are governed (ADR 0034)', errors=[{'code': 'catalogue_write_refused', 'msg': ...}])`, before any call or cost row.
4. routers/projects.py get_project_tools: `allowed` becomes `(tout or outil.name in autorises) and outil.ouvert_a(groupes) and not outil.may_write`. The item shape is unchanged.
5. Tests that rely on direct POST or MCP catalogue calls: set `read_only: true` on their catalogue entries (test_catalogue_outils.py :356 and any other entry with `method: POST` or `mcp:` that grep finds in apps/api/tests).
6. docs/deployment.md '### How a tool reaches the agent' (L465-490): the read_only flag, the refusal, and how to migrate a write to an organisation MCP connector. docs/security.md: a row 'a run writing to a third party without a governed action'. Write a 'Release note' line in the PR body: a POST or MCP catalogue tool without `read_only: true` stops being listed and callable.

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Also amend `docs/adr/0034-connectors-by-capability.md` §1 (add it to your files): a project holds at most one connector per kind (`models.py` `UniqueConstraint("project_id", "kind")`); several named connectors of one capability are organisation connectors (`OrgConnector`, ADR 0074). The code is right and the page was stale.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Anchors at 3156d12. `get_project_tools` is now routers/projects.py:317 (not L265-298). In openapi.yaml, getProjectTools is around L3347 (not 3145-3175). The docs/deployment.md '### How a tool reaches the agent' section starts at L470 (not 465-490). These anchors are unchanged: packages/core catalogue.py (L57, L65-82, L85-110), apps/api catalogue.py:39-57, internal.py:825/:879 and the `elif outil is not None:` branch at ~L922, and activities/stage.py:889-894.
- Clarify the Do-not line 'Do not change organisation-connector (broker) branches or plugin tools'. 'Plugin tools' there means tools of entry-point extensions (`choregos_api.greffons.declarer_un_fournisseur_d_outils`, tested by test_greffons_outils.py); leave them untouched. The tools of ADR 0096 plugin servers are organisation `mcp` connectors (`org_connectors.plugin_server` set) served through the broker (`du_courtier` branch of call_tool). They are not catalogue entries, are not affected by `may_write`, and are the governed way to write. Say so in the docs/deployment.md migration paragraph.
- The integrator amendment on docs/adr/0034-connectors-by-capability.md still applies, and the file is now in this story's file list. Main added an 'Amended by ADR 0096' block at L10-13: keep it, and add the one-connector-per-kind sentence in '## Decision' (L27). The constraint is `UniqueConstraint('project_id', 'kind', name='project_kind')` at db/models.py:116.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_write_is_refused.py :: test_a_post_catalogue_tool_is_refused_and_never_called :: a catalogue with `create_ticket` (http POST to a fake provider transport), opened to the project via config.tools. POST /api/v1/internal/runs/{id}/tools/create_ticket with a run token -> 409 code catalogue_write_refused; the fake provider received 0 requests; no CostLedger row. Today it is called
- apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_write_is_refused.py :: test_a_tool_marked_read_only_is_called :: the same entry with read_only: true is called and answered
- apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_write_is_refused.py :: test_a_writing_tool_is_not_listed_anywhere :: GET /api/v1/internal/runs/{id}/tools omits create_ticket but lists a GET tool; GET /api/v1/projects/{id}/tools shows create_ticket with allowed false; catalogue.outils_du_projet(['*']) omits it and outils_du_projet(['*'], include_writers=True) includes it
- apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_outils.py (existing, with read_only added) :: green

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] No catalogue tool that may write is listed or called for a run, nor shown as allowed in the console, unless the deployment declared it read-only. The refusal names the way out.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row: proves the refusal and the filtering by tests. Does not prove that existing deployments' catalogues were reviewed (operator action, release note).

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_write_is_refused.py apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_outils.py apps/api/tests/test_greffons_outils.py apps/api/tests/test_catalogue_plugin_result_bounds.py -q
uv run pytest apps/api/tests -q
uv run pytest apps/orchestrator/tests -q -k 'observability or verif'
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not route catalogue writes through ActionWorkflow, and do not invent an effect.
- Do not change organisation-connector (broker) branches or plugin tools.
- Do not refuse at catalogue load (charger_catalogue): an API must still start with an old catalogue.
- Do not add a field to the getProjectTools response (no contract change).
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/deployment.md '## Playbooks and the tool catalogue'`, `docs/security.md`
