### S47-13 · fix(api): tracker commands are answered, never dropped; a webhook for an ended interpreter is kept

**Wave** 7 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-01`, `S47-05`, `S47-11` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(api): tracker commands are answered, never dropped; a webhook for an ended interpreter is kept`

**Goal.** A `/choregos ...` comment no longer disappears into the interpreter's inbox. Choregos answers it on the tracker with a console link, audits it, and sends nothing to the workflow. A webhook event for a work item whose interpreter has ended, or for a project whose tracker connector is broken, no longer fails the delivery or rolls back its dedupe row. Unsigned GitHub and Tekton webhooks are accepted only on instances that are not deployed. The trust decision is recorded in an ADR.

**Context.**

packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/tracker/github_events.py:20-22 `COMMAND_RE` (`^\s*/choregos\s+(approve|reject|answer|scope|pause|resume|stop)\b`, re.M) and :100-131. An issue_comment becomes `InboundEvent(type=HUMAN_DECISION, payload={'verb', 'argument', 'channel', 'by': login})`. jira_events.py:137-141 and gitlab_events.py:104-108 reuse the regex.

Current path of a command:
1. routers/webhooks.py:104-163 `_dispatch`.
2. :159 `await deliver_inbound(project.slug, key, event)`.
3. temporal.py:419-422 sends the `inbound` signal WITHOUT the NOT_FOUND translation that `_signal_interpreter` (:403-412) has.
4. The interpreter's `_absorb` (interpreter.py:994-1007) drops `human.decision`.

Failure modes:
- A gone interpreter makes `_dispatch` raise; get_db_plateforme then rolls back the WebhookDelivery dedupe row (`_already_seen`, :46-71) and the rest of the delivery, `_embarquer` for PR_MERGED (:160-161) included.
- services/tickets.py:270-293 `tracker_du_projet` itself raises `upstream(...)` (an ApiError, 502) when the connector cannot be built.

Signature checks: L208-213 accepts unsigned GitHub webhooks unless env is staging/prod; L254-259 does the same for Tekton. `Settings.deployed` comes from S47-01.

Test seams:
- `services.tickets.tracker_du_projet`, monkeypatched in apps/api/tests/test_work_item_external_tracker.py:44-58 with a FakeTracker whose `comment()` appends to `items[key].comments`. Call it through the module (`tickets.tracker_du_projet`) so the patch applies.
- FakeTemporal (temporal.py:116-145) has `signals` and `gone`.

The web item page is `/p/{project slug}/items/{item id}` (apps/web/src/app/p/[slug]/items/[id]).

Verdict V14 confirmed, plus new findings.

**Files.**

- Create: `docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md`, `apps/api/tests/test_tracker_commands.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_webhook_to_an_ended_interpreter.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_unsigned_webhooks.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/webhooks.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/temporal.py`, `docs/usage.md`, `docs/security.md`, `docs/deployment.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`

**Steps.**

1. temporal.py `deliver_inbound` (L419-422): send through `_signal_interpreter(project_slug, tracker_key, 'inbound', event.model_dump(mode='json'))`, so NOT_FOUND raises InterpreterNotRunningError.
2. webhooks.py: import the tickets module (`from ..services import tickets`) and add `DECISION_VERBS = frozenset({'approve', 'reject', 'answer', 'scope'})` and `CONTROL_VERBS = frozenset({'pause', 'resume', 'stop'})`. Add `async def _answer_tracker_command(session, project, item, event) -> None`:
   - Read verb and by from event.payload. base = get_settings().public_url.rstrip('/').
   - For decision verbs: link = base + '/inbox'; markdown = '<!-- choregos:command -->' plus a line starting with a backtick: '`/choregos {verb}` is not taken from a comment: a decision is made in the console, after a fresh sign-in: {link}'.
   - For control verbs: link = f'{base}/p/{project.slug}/items/{item.id}'; same shape, saying 'pause, resume and stop are done from the work item in the console: {link}'.
   - Inside ONE try/except catching (ApiError, AdapterError, UpstreamError), with a logged warning and no re-raise: `row, tracker = await tickets.tracker_du_projet(session, project)`, then `await tracker.comment(item.tracker_key, markdown)` when tracker is not None.
   - Then `await record(session, None, 'tracker.command.redirected', org_id=project.org_id, target_type='work_item', target_id=item.id, verb=verb, by=by, source=event.source)`.
3. webhooks.py `_dispatch`: right before `deliver_inbound` (L159), add `if event.type == InboundEventType.HUMAN_DECISION: await _answer_tracker_command(...); delivered += 1; continue`. Wrap `deliver_inbound` in `try/except InterpreterNotRunningError`: log at info 'event for a work item whose interpreter has ended' (project, work_item, event type) and carry on; `_embarquer` for PR_MERGED still runs.
4. webhooks.py L208-213 and L254-259: replace `settings.env in {'staging', 'prod'}` with `settings.deployed`; the refusal messages are unchanged.
5. Docs:
   - docs/usage.md '## 5. Let a ticket run' (L2193): what a `/choregos` comment gets (a reply with the console link, audited, no effect).
   - docs/security.md: a row 'deciding from a tracker comment'.
   - docs/deployment.md '### Webhook secrets' (L1057): unsigned GitHub/Tekton webhooks are refused on any deployed instance (staging/prod or https).
   Write a 'Release note' line in the PR body.
6. Write docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md (accepted, today):
   - A tracker login is not a Choregos principal.
   - Decisions never come from comments: step-up, group verdicts and gesture controls live in the console.
   - pause/resume/stop may be honoured only for tracker identities an organisation admin linked by their stable provider id, on authenticated deliveries (S47-14).
   - Until then every command is answered with a console link, and no command reaches the interpreter.
   Append the README row.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- ADR number: 0098 is now the English ADR (renumbered from 0089). Create `docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md` instead (hardening 0098 -> 0102), and replace every 'ADR 0098' in the story with 'ADR 0102'. Check docs/adr/ first and take the next free number if 0102 is taken.
- routers/webhooks.py anchors at 3156d12. `_dispatch` is L104-~166 and now has the signature `_dispatch(session, events, project_of: Project | None = None)` (S45-07). `deliver_inbound` is called at L162 (put the HUMAN_DECISION branch and the InterpreterNotRunningError try/except there), and `_embarquer` for PR_MERGED is at L163-164. The GitHub unsigned check is L212-216 (`settings.env in {'staging', 'prod'}` at L215), and the Tekton one is L259-264 (the env test at L261). `_already_seen` L46-71 and temporal.py (`_signal_interpreter` L403-412, `deliver_inbound` L419-422, FakeTemporal L117-145) are unchanged. services/tickets.py `tracker_du_projet` is now L270-296 and also pins plugin trackers (`pin_plugin_tracker`). It still raises `upstream(...)`, which the step-2 try/except (ApiError, AdapterError, UpstreamError) covers.
- New call site of the function you change: `plugin_webhook` (`POST /webhooks/plugins/{org}/{plugin}/{server}`, L517-~588, ADR 0096) dedupes with `_already_seen(session, 'plugin', ...)` and then calls `_dispatch(session, [event], project_of=project)`. An ended interpreter rolled that delivery back too. The `_dispatch` fix covers it. Add apps/api/tests/test_webhook_to_an_ended_interpreter.py :: test_a_plugin_tracker_event_for_an_ended_interpreter_is_kept, set up like apps/api/tests/test_plugin_tracker.py (the fake plugin tracker), with the interpreter id in FakeTemporal.gone. The delivery answers 202 and the same delivery again answers duplicate=True.
- Plugin trackers never emit HUMAN_DECISION (plugin-tracker-profile.schema.json allows only tracker.item.created/updated/commented/closed), so `_answer_tracker_command` only sees GitHub, Jira and GitLab commands. Do not add a plugin branch.
- Docs anchors: docs/usage.md '## 5. Let a ticket run' is now L2254 (not L2193), and docs/deployment.md '### Webhook secrets' is now L1155 (not L1057).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

### S47-14 · contract(api): link members to their GitHub user id and honour pause, resume and stop from signed GitHub comments

**Wave** 8 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** L · **Depends on** `S47-13`, `S47-05`, `S47-06`, `S47-03`, `S47-18`, `S47-09` · **Contract change** yes

**PR title:** `contract(api): link members to their GitHub user id and honour pause, resume and stop from signed GitHub comments`

**Goal.** An organisation administrator can link a member to their GitHub account by its stable numeric user id. A `/choregos pause|resume|stop` comment then works only when it arrives in an HMAC-verified GitHub delivery from that id. It is applied with the member's own rights (ITEM_CONTROL), audited on channel `tracker` and confirmed on the tracker. Jira display names, GitLab usernames and deliveries authenticated by the shared generic secret are never identities. Decisions (approve, reject, answer, scope) still answer with the console link (ADR 0102).

**Context.**

After S47-13, routers/webhooks.py `_answer_tracker_command` answers every verb with a link.

Forgeable identities (blocker of the critique):
- jira_events.py:127 and :146 use `comment.author.displayName` as `by`, which is neither unique nor stable.
- gitlab_events.py uses `user.username`. GitHub and GitLab logins can be renamed and re-registered.
- Jira and GitLab deliveries are authenticated only by CHOREGOS_GENERIC_WEBHOOK_SECRET, which is also accepted in the Tekton sink URL (`?jeton=`, webhooks.py:250-259).
- GitHub deliveries are HMAC-signed with CHOREGOS_GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET (webhooks.py:208-214) when it is set.
- github_events.py `_issue_comment` (:100-131) has `comment.user.id` available but does not emit it.

REST control path: routers/workitems.py:514-574 `post_action`:
- checks `Permission.ITEM_CONTROL` (:521);
- builds `Control(..., by=principal.email)`;
- calls `deliver_control` and handles InterpreterNotRunningError (:546-558);
- sets `item.paused` (:559-562) and records `workitem.{action}`.

deps.py `_principal_from_user(session, user)` (L85-122) ends with `limiter_aux_organisations` (L120) and `nommer_l_utilisateur` (L121). A webhook processing several events must do neither, or later events are scoped and attributed to the first member.

Other facts:
- No tracker identity column on User (db/models.py:44-54). Members routes are in routers/admin.py (DELETE /orgs/{org}/members/{user_id} at :317).
- Every org table is under forced RLS (test_rls_postgres.py:291-300 exempts five tables). The pattern to copy is migrations/versions/20261022_1500_a2a_push_configs.py:87-102.
- tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py (S47-17/S47-18) refuses an API path without a client unless it is allowlisted.

This is a contract-change PR (ADR 0001); it serializes after S47-06 and with S46's contract PRs.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/migrations/versions/20261030_1000_tracker_identities.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/tracker_identities.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/schemas/tracker_identities.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/work_item_controls.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_tracker_identities.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_github_command_author_id.py`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/openapi.yaml`, `packages/contracts/ts/api.ts`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/db/models.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/main.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/deps.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/webhooks.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/workitems.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/schemas/__init__.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/tracker/github_events.py`, `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py`, `docs/usage.md`, `docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md`

**Steps.**

1. github_events.py `_issue_comment`: add `'by_id': str((comment.get('user') or {}).get('id') or '')` to the HUMAN_DECISION payload (keep `by`). Do not touch the Jira or GitLab parsers.
2. Contract, openapi.yaml: add GET /orgs/{org}/members/{user_id}/tracker-identities (operationId listTrackerIdentities, 200 array of TrackerIdentity), and PUT and DELETE /orgs/{org}/members/{user_id}/tracker-identities/{tracker} (operationIds setTrackerIdentity and deleteTrackerIdentity). PUT body {external_id: string (the provider's numeric user id), login: string (display only)} returns 200 TrackerIdentity; DELETE returns 204; both have 403/404 and a 409 when the external_id is linked to another member. TrackerIdentity: {tracker: enum [github], external_id, login, user_id, created_by: [string, null], created_at: date-time}; its description says other trackers come when their deliveries are signed per source. Run `make contracts`.
3. Model TrackerIdentity (`tracker_identities`) in db/models.py, with PkMixin and TimestampMixin: org_id FK organizations (CASCADE), user_id FK users (CASCADE), tracker String(16), external_id String(64), login String(255), created_by String(320) nullable. Add UniqueConstraint(org_id, tracker, external_id) and UniqueConstraint(org_id, user_id, tracker). The migration's down_revision is the current head (the S47-05 migration or newer), with forced RLS and the policy `tracker_identities_org_isolation` copied from 20261022_1500_a2a_push_configs.py.
4. Add schemas/tracker_identities.py (TrackerIdentityDto, TrackerIdentityPut; external_id must match `^[0-9]{1,20}$`), exported from schemas/__init__.py. Add routers/tracker_identities.py with the three routes: org admin only (Permission.MEMBER_MANAGE at org level, and the user must hold an org-level membership), audited `tracker_identity.set` / `tracker_identity.delete`. Register the router in main.py `routeurs_du_coeur` after `admin.router`.
5. deps.py: give `_principal_from_user` a keyword `narrow: bool = True`. When it is False, skip BOTH `limiter_aux_organisations` and `nommer_l_utilisateur`. Add a public `async def principal_for_user(session, user, *, narrow: bool = True) -> Principal` calling it.
6. services/work_item_controls.py: extract the pause/resume/stop part of workitems.post_action into `async def apply_control(session, principal, project, item, org_slug, action, reason, channel) -> None`. It does the permission check (same forbidden text), builds Control, calls deliver_control, re-raises InterpreterNotRunningError, sets item.paused and records `workitem.{action}` with `channel`. post_action calls it for those three actions; its messages and statuses are unchanged.
7. webhooks.py `_answer_tracker_command`, for CONTROL_VERBS: honour the command only when ALL of these hold: event.source == 'github', `get_settings().github_webhook_secret` is set (the delivery was HMAC-verified), payload by_id is non-empty, and a TrackerIdentity with (project.org_id, 'github', by_id) exists.
   - Then: user = session.get(User, identity.user_id); principal = `principal_for_user(session, user, narrow=False)`; call `apply_control(..., reason=f'/choregos {verb} on the tracker', channel='tracker')`; reply 'Done: {verb} by {email}', or the forbidden text, or 'the work item is not running'.
   - Otherwise keep the S47-13 reply and add 'an organisation administrator can link your GitHub account to your member'.
   Decision verbs are unchanged.
8. tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py: add '/orgs/{org}/members/{user_id}/tracker-identities' and '/orgs/{org}/members/{user_id}/tracker-identities/{tracker}' to `NO_CLIENT_YET` with the reason 'console screen: S47-15'.
9. docs/usage.md '## 5. Let a ticket run': the three honoured commands (GitHub only, signed deliveries, linked numeric id), how to find a GitHub user id (`https://api.github.com/users/<login>`), and what stays in the console. ADR 0102: mark the 'controls' point implemented (S47-14), and say why Jira and GitLab are not honoured (display names and usernames are not stable ids, and their deliveries carry only the shared secret).

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- The ADR this story amends is `docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md`, created by S47-13 after renumbering. Replace every 'ADR 0098' in the story with 'ADR 0102'.
- Alembic: `down_revision` is S47-05's membership_source revision, which sits on c9e1a3b5d7f8 (plugin_servers) at 3156d12. Check that `alembic heads` shows one head. The new table must be under forced RLS: test_rls_postgres.py `EXEMPTEES` is now L294-303, has six entries (org_slug_aliases was added) and must not gain tracker_identities.
- Anchors at 3156d12: deps.py `_principal_from_user` is L86-123, with `limiter_aux_organisations` at L121 and `nommer_l_utilisateur` at L122. db/models.py User is L61-73. In routers/webhooks.py, the GitHub HMAC check is L212-218 and the Tekton `?jeton=` handling is at L253/L259. `_dispatch` now takes `project_of=None` (the two-command test calls it without it). main.py `admin.router` is at L354 in `routeurs_du_coeur` (register the new router right after it). workitems.py `post_action` L517-~575 and github_events.py `_issue_comment` L100 are unchanged.
- The new routers/tracker_identities.py must not define an `_organisation`/`_org` helper, because the S47-07 lookup ratchet is at CEILING 1 after S47-09. Look the organisation up with `services.organisations.organisation_by_slug` (S47-09). Path slugs arrive already rewritten to the current slug by OrgAliasMiddleware (ADR 0090).

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/adapters/tests/test_github_command_author_id.py :: test_a_command_carries_the_commenter_id :: parse_github_event('issue_comment', ...) with comment.user {login: 'mallory', id: 1001} gives a HUMAN_DECISION whose payload by_id == '1001'; fails today
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_identities.py :: test_an_admin_links_a_github_identity_and_a_developer_cannot :: PUT as org admin -> 200; as a developer -> 403; the same external_id for another member -> 409; DELETE -> 204; audit rows exist. Fails today (404)
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_a_linked_developer_pauses_from_a_signed_comment :: CHOREGOS_GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET set and the delivery signed (copy the signing helper the existing GitHub webhook tests use), dev@varga.dev (developer) linked to id 1001. '/choregos pause' from user id 1001 sends one `control` signal with action pause and by dev@varga.dev, sets item.paused, writes the audit workitem.pause with channel tracker, and replies 'Done'
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_a_renamed_github_login_does_not_inherit_the_link :: link id 1001 with login mallory; a comment from login 'mallory' with id 2002 sends no signal and gets the link reply
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_an_unsigned_delivery_honours_nothing :: without CHOREGOS_GITHUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET (env test), a comment from a linked id sends no signal
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_a_jira_display_name_is_not_an_identity :: a Jira comment '/choregos pause' whose author.displayName equals a linked member's login sends no signal
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_an_unlinked_id_or_a_viewer_changes_nothing :: an unlinked id gives no signal and the link reply; a linked viewer gives no signal and the forbidden text in the reply
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_a_decision_is_never_taken_from_a_comment :: a linked org admin's '/choregos approve' sends no human_decision signal
- apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py :: test_two_commands_in_one_delivery_keep_their_own_members :: webhooks._dispatch on two HUMAN_DECISION events (pause by id 1001 -> alice, resume by id 1002 -> bob) writes audit rows attributed to alice then bob
- apps/api/tests/test_rls_postgres.py :: test_chaque_table_est_sous_rls_ou_exemptee (existing, PostgreSQL) :: green with the new table
- apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py and tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py (existing) :: green

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Linked members control their work items from signed GitHub comments with their own rights, audited on channel tracker. Nobody decides from a comment. Jira/GitLab commands and unsigned deliveries are never honoured.
- [ ] The new table is under forced RLS; the migration goes down and up.
- [ ] make ci and make contracts-check green. STATUS row: proves the stable-id binding, the RBAC and the audit by tests. Does not verify that the member owns the GitHub account (admin-asserted link), and not against a real GitHub.

**Commands.**

```bash
make contracts && make contracts-check
uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_tracker_identities.py apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py apps/api/tests/test_tracker_commands.py apps/api/tests/test_contract_conformance.py apps/api/tests/test_migrations.py packages/adapters/tests/test_github_command_author_id.py tests/web -q
CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES=1 uv run pytest apps/api/tests/test_rls_postgres.py apps/api/tests/test_tracker_controls.py -q  # with a PostgreSQL
uv run pytest apps/api/tests -q
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Never honour approve/reject/answer/scope from a comment.
- Never match on a login, a username or a display name: only the numeric provider id.
- Do not honour any command from Jira, GitLab, or a GitHub delivery without HMAC verification.
- Do not leave the webhook session narrowed or attributed to one member (narrow=False skips both calls).
- Do not change the REST control endpoint's messages or statuses.
- Do not add the console screen here (S47-15).
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/usage.md '## 5. Let a ticket run'`, `docs/adr/0102-a-tracker-comment-carries-no-decision.md`

### S47-15 · feat(web): link a member's GitHub account from the members page

**Wave** 9 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S47-14`, `S47-18` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(web): link a member's GitHub account from the members page`

**Goal.** An organisation administrator can see, set and remove a member's GitHub link (numeric id and login) in the console. Without the screen, the S47-14 control path is reachable only through the API. The route-client guard no longer needs its S47-14 allowlist entries.

**Context.**

Endpoints from S47-14: listTrackerIdentities, setTrackerIdentity and deleteTrackerIdentity, with tracker = 'github' and a body {external_id, login}. The generated types are in @choregos/contracts.

Web:
- The members page is apps/web/src/app/admin/members/page.tsx.
- apps/web/src/lib/api.ts imports types from ./types, and apps/web/src/lib/types.ts re-exports @choregos/contracts.
- The mock-mode routes are in apps/web/src/mocks/data.ts.
- api.ts, types.ts and mocks/data.ts are hot spots, serialized after S47-18.

tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py holds the two tracker-identity paths in NO_CLIENT_YET (added by S47-14).

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/web/src/components/admin/tracker-identities.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/tracker-identities.test.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`, `apps/web/src/mocks/data.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/admin/members/page.tsx`, `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py`

**Steps.**

1. types.ts: export TrackerIdentity from @choregos/contracts.
2. api.ts, next to the members functions: `trackerIdentities(org, userId)`, `setTrackerIdentity(org, userId, tracker, body: { external_id: string; login: string })` (PUT) and `deleteTrackerIdentity(org, userId, tracker)` (DELETE).
3. mocks/data.ts: in-memory routes for the three operations, following the existing members mocks.
4. components/admin/tracker-identities.tsx: `TrackerIdentities({ org, userId })` with a GitHub login field, a numeric GitHub user id field (digits only) with a hint linking to `https://api.github.com/users/<login>`, save and remove buttons, and the 409 detail shown inline. Add a short note: only pause, resume and stop are taken from signed GitHub comments.
5. app/admin/members/page.tsx: render it for each member, for org admins only (follow how the page gates admin actions).
6. tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py: remove the two tracker-identity paths from NO_CLIENT_YET.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/web/tests/tracker-identities.test.tsx :: it('shows the conflict when the id belongs to another member') :: a 409 ApiError shows its detail
- tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py :: test_every_api_path_has_a_client (entries removed) :: fails without the api.ts functions

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] Org admins manage GitHub links from the members page; others do not see the controls.
- [ ] make web-ci and make ci green. STATUS row: proves the screen against mocks; not against the live API.


### S47-16 · fix(api): one HumanRequest constructor, so agent questions and scope changes get a due date

**Wave** 9 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S46-01`, `S47-07`, `S47-12` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(api): one HumanRequest constructor, so agent questions and scope changes get a due date`

**Goal.** All human requests are built by one function that sets `due_at`, so agent questions and scope-change requests show an SLA date in the inbox like those the orchestrator creates. A self-tested guard keeps a fourth construction site from appearing.

**Context.**

HumanRequest rows are built in three places:
- apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/tracker.py:238-246, which sets `due_at=utcnow() + timedelta(hours=sla_hours)` with `sla_hours = int(payload.get('sla_hours') or 24)` (L237).
- apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/internal.py:330-337 (scope_change), with no `due_at`.
- internal.py:357-364 (question), with no `due_at`.
Anchors shift after S46-01/S47-12: grep `HumanRequest(`.

The model (db/models.py:295-307) has requested_at and due_at. The inbox reads due dates in services/tickets.py:119 and services/parcours.py:222.

tracker.py:11 already has `from choregos_api.services import Naissance, nouveau_ticket, persist_event`: extending that statement keeps the S47-07 ratchet unchanged.

The guard follows apps/api/tests/test_un_seul_constructeur_de_ticket.py, and must match both `HumanRequest(...)` and `models.HumanRequest(...)`.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/human_requests.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_one_human_request_constructor.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_agent_requests_have_a_due_date.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/services/__init__.py`, `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/internal.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/tracker.py`

**Steps.**

1. services/human_requests.py: `DEFAULT_SLA_HOURS = 24` and `def new_human_request(*, work_item_id: str, project_id: str, transition_id: str | None, kind: str, payload: dict[str, Any], sla_hours: int | None = None) -> HumanRequest`, which sets `requested_at = utcnow()` and `due_at = requested_at + timedelta(hours=sla_hours or DEFAULT_SLA_HOURS)`. English docstring: the one constructor; callers add the row.
2. services/__init__.py: re-export `new_human_request` (import and `__all__`).
3. internal.py: build both rows with `new_human_request(...)` (kind 'scope_change' / 'question', same payloads), then `session.add` as today.
4. activities/tracker.py: add `new_human_request` to the existing import statement at L11 (do not add a new import statement), and build the row at L238-246 with it, passing `sla_hours=sla_hours`.
5. test_one_human_request_constructor.py: `def constructions(root: Path) -> list[tuple[Path, int]]` collects every ast.Call whose func is `ast.Name(id='HumanRequest')` or `ast.Attribute(attr='HumanRequest')`. The real test runs it over apps/*/src and packages/*/src and allows only services/human_requests.py. The self-test runs it on a tmp_path file holding `HumanRequest(x=1)` and `models.HumanRequest(x=1)` and expects 2 hits.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- The orchestrator ratchet baseline is 150 at 3156d12, not 149: the plugin stream added activities/base.py:23 importing choregos_api.services.plugin_tracker. The listed test is therefore 'tests/architecture/test_orchestrator_imports_of_the_api.py (S47-07) :: still at or below its CEILING (150)'. Extending the existing L11 import keeps the count unchanged.
- db/models.py HumanRequest is now L312+ (not L295-307). Every other anchor is unchanged at 3156d12: tracker.py:11 import, `sla_hours` at L237, the construction at L238-246, internal.py:330 and :357, tickets.py:119, parcours.py:222. Main added no other `HumanRequest(` construction site.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

### S47-17 · feat(web): project screens for the cross-backend review metric and the memory re-import

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S47-06` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(web): project screens for the cross-backend review metric and the memory re-import`

**Goal.** Two API features that only the API exposes get a console screen: the cross-backend review report becomes a card next to DORA, and the project memory re-import becomes an action on the memory page. A self-tested guard fails whenever an API path has neither a web client nor a CLI client, apart from a reasoned allowlist.

**Context.**

API endpoints:
- GET /projects/{id}/metrics/cross-backend (getProjectCrossBackend, CrossBackendReport; openapi.yaml:1499-1508; schema L7207-7223).
- POST /projects/{id}/memory/reimport (reimportMemory; openapi.yaml:1337-1344).

Web:
- apps/web/src/lib/api.ts has `dora` (L932) and `query(params?: Record<string, string>)` (L960). `query({ since })` with an optional `since` fails typecheck and would send since=undefined.
- The pages are apps/web/src/app/p/[slug]/page.tsx (DORA card) and apps/web/src/app/p/[slug]/memory/page.tsx.

A scan at b3a7893 of the openapi paths against apps/web/src (excluding src/mocks) and packages/cli/src finds 13 paths without a client:
- /projects/{id}/memory/reimport, /projects/{id}/metrics/cross-backend (this story);
- /orgs/{org}/memory/ab-report, /platform/gateway/keys (S47-18);
- /github/setup, /remote-credentials/oauth/callback (redirect targets);
- /healthz, /readyz (probes);
- /orgs/{org}/agents/{slug}/responses, /orgs/{org}/agent-sessions/{session_id}, /orgs/{org}/hosted-sources/{sha256} (machine endpoints);
- /projects/{id}/workflows/validate (the project-scoped twin of /workflows/validate, which api.ts:814 and the CLI main.py:622 call);
- /templates/{name} (getTemplate/updateTemplate: no client).
/theme is called outside api.ts (lib/served-theme-server.ts:55), which is why the search covers all of apps/web/src. /platform/secrets/rewrap has a CLI client (packages/cli/src/choregos_cli/main.py:152-157).

Verdict V21 partly. This story touches the hot spots api.ts, types.ts and mocks/data.ts (serialized: S47-18, then S47-15, follow) and docs/usage.md.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/web/src/components/cross-backend-card.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/memory-reimport.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/cross-backend-card.test.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/memory-reimport.test.tsx`, `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`, `apps/web/src/mocks/data.ts`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[slug]/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/p/[slug]/memory/page.tsx`, `docs/usage.md`

**Steps.**

1. types.ts: export CrossBackendReport and CrossBackendArm from @choregos/contracts.
2. api.ts: add `crossBackend: (id: string, since?: string) => request<CrossBackendReport>(`/projects/${qualify(id)}/metrics/cross-backend${query(since ? { since } : undefined)}`)` and `reimportMemory: (id: string, sources?: string[]) => request<...>(`/projects/${qualify(id)}/memory/reimport`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(sources ? { sources } : {}) })`, with the response type of openapi.yaml:1341-1344.
3. mocks/data.ts: mock both routes the way /metrics/dora is mocked.
4. components/cross-backend-card.tsx: the verdict, the detail, the two arms (reviews, caught, catch rate as %, backends) and a 'required by the policy' badge when cross_backend_required. Place it beside the DORA card in app/p/[slug]/page.tsx.
5. components/memory-reimport.tsx: a 'Re-import the project memory' button with a confirmation and the result counts, rendered on app/p/[slug]/memory/page.tsx only when the page's existing memory-write permission check passes (reuse it).
6. tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py:
   - Parse the openapi.yaml paths (yaml.safe_load) and skip the prefixes /webhooks/, /internal/ and /auth/.
   - `def path_pattern(path) -> re.Pattern`: literal segments escaped; a {param} segment matches `${...}` (TS), `{...}` (Python f-string) or a literal [A-Za-z0-9_.:-]+; the match ends at a non [A-Za-z0-9_-] char.
   - Search the text of every *.ts/*.tsx under apps/web/src except apps/web/src/mocks, plus every *.py under packages/cli/src.
   - `NOT_CALLED_BY_A_CLIENT`, each entry with a one-line reason: /github/setup, /remote-credentials/oauth/callback, /healthz, /readyz, /orgs/{org}/agents/{slug}/responses, /orgs/{org}/agent-sessions/{session_id}, /orgs/{org}/hosted-sources/{sha256}, /projects/{id}/workflows/validate, /templates/{name} (platform template authoring by API; open a `finding` issue, origin S47-17, and cite it).
   - `NO_CLIENT_YET = {'/orgs/{org}/memory/ab-report', '/platform/gateway/keys'}` (S47-18 removes them).
   - `CLI_ONLY = {'/platform/secrets/rewrap'}` (KEK rotation stays an operator command).
   - Every other path must match.
7. docs/usage.md: a short paragraph on the cross-backend card under '## 6. Read what came out' (L2260), and the re-import action under '### Memories' (L1296).

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- `tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py` keeps an explicit `MACHINE_ONLY_ROUTES` allowlist (webhooks, `/internal/*`, the MCP and A2A doors, health and metrics) with a reason per entry; later stories (S46-07, S47-14, S47-15) add their machine-only routes there.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- A re-scan at 3156d12 (openapi paths minus /webhooks/, /internal/ and /auth/, searched in apps/web/src without mocks and in packages/cli/src) finds 21 client-less paths, not 13: the story's 13 plus 8 new ones. - Anchors at 3156d12. In openapi.yaml: /projects/{id}/memory/reimport L1359, /projects/{id}/metrics/cross-backend L1521, CrossBackendArm L7649, CrossBackendReport L7656. In api.ts: `dora` L939, `query()` L967. In the CLI (packages/cli/src/choregos_cli/main.py): /platform/secrets/rewrap L152-160, /workflows/validate L624. served-theme-server.ts:55 is unchanged. ### S47-19 · refactor(core): one definition of the cross-backend review, used by the metric and by the reviewer swap

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `refactor(core): one definition of the cross-backend review, used by the metric and by the reviewer swap`

**Goal.** Which runs count as a cross review, who the implementer was at review time, the reviewer candidates and the verdict are defined once, as pure functions in choregos_core.cross_review, and the API metric and the orchestrator's reviewer swap both use them. The metric now measures what the policy decides: it counts no verify runs and no fixed-runtime or remote reviewers, and it compares each review with the implementer at the time of that review. The verdict strings and their rounding are unchanged.

**Context.**

apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/stage.py:
- L1286-1343 `_reviewer_backend`: it takes the latest IMPLEMENT backend (L1305-1315), then walks `allowed = list(bundle.config.agent.allowed_backends) or list(KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES)` (L1316).
- The swap applies only to `plan.role == REVIEW` with a non-fixed backend (L217-223; `_fixed_backend` L1194-1197).

apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/costs.py:
- L382-482 `project_cross_backend` counts REVIEW and VERIFY runs (L408) of any backend and compares each one with the LAST implementer overall (L413-428).
- A review counts as caught when a Finding has origin_run_id == run.id, or when the result status is not in {None, 'done'}.
- `to_arm` (L458-466) rounds catch_rate to 4 decimals; `_cross_backend_verdict` (L485-505) compares those ROUNDED rates against ±0.05 and formats them with `{:.0%}` / `{:+.1%}`.
- L380 `MIN_REVIEWS_PAR_BRAS = 5`.

KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES is at packages/core/src/choregos_core/models.py:63-70. Existing tests: apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend.py and apps/orchestrator/tests/test_cross_backend.py. This is an activity change, not a workflow change. Verdict V13 partly.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/core/src/choregos_core/cross_review.py`, `packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py`, `apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend_metric_scope.py`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/costs.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/stage.py`, `docs/concepts.md`

**Steps.**

1. Create packages/core/src/choregos_core/cross_review.py (English):
   - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class StageRun(run_id: str, work_item_id: str, role: str, backend: str | None, created_at: datetime, caught: bool = False)`.
   - `def counts_as_cross_review(role: str, backend: str | None) -> bool` = role == StageRole.REVIEW value and backend in KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES.
   - `def implementer_backend(runs, work_item_id, *, at: datetime | None = None) -> str | None` = the backend of the latest IMPLEMENT run of that item with a backend and created_at <= at (when given).
   - `def reviewer_candidates(implementer: str, allowed: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]` = (allowed or KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES) without the implementer, order kept.
   - `@dataclass class Arm(reviews: int, caught: int, backends: set[str])` with a property `catch_rate` = `round(caught / reviews, 4) if reviews else None`.
   - `def cross_backend_arms(runs: Sequence[StageRun]) -> tuple[Arm, Arm]` (same, other), over the runs that count as a cross review and have a known implementer at their time.
   - `MIN_REVIEWS_PER_ARM = 5`.
   - `def cross_backend_verdict(same: Arm, other: Arm) -> tuple[str, str]`, moved verbatim from costs._cross_backend_verdict. It uses the ROUNDED `catch_rate` of each arm for the delta, the comparisons and the formatting, exactly as today.
2. costs.py project_cross_backend: query the project's REVIEW runs since `start` and all IMPLEMENT runs with a backend, build the StageRun lists (caught as today), then call cross_backend_arms and cross_backend_verdict. Map the arms to CrossBackendArm (reviews, caught, catch_rate = arm.catch_rate, sorted backends). Keep `MIN_REVIEWS_PAR_BRAS = MIN_REVIEWS_PER_ARM` as an alias if any test imports it (grep). Delete `_cross_backend_verdict`.
3. stage.py `_reviewer_backend`: keep the implementer query, and replace the candidate list at L1316-1318 with `reviewer_candidates(implementer, list(bundle.config.agent.allowed_backends))`. Log messages are unchanged.
4. docs/concepts.md '## Stage and run': a paragraph on the cross-backend review. The swap applies only to review stages of coding agents. The metric (`GET /projects/{id}/metrics/cross-backend`) counts reviews by coding agents; caught means a finding or a non-done result; each review is compared with the implementer at review time; at least 5 reviews are needed per arm. The numbers change for projects with verify or remote reviews.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- routers/costs.py anchors shifted by +5 to +8 lines (the S42-10 cost-kind change above them; the cross-backend code itself is unchanged). `MIN_REVIEWS_PAR_BRAS` is L388, `project_cross_backend` starts at L396, the REVIEW/VERIFY filter is L413, `to_arm` is L466 and `_cross_backend_verdict` is L488-508. The activities/stage.py anchors (L217-223, `_fixed_backend` L1194, `_reviewer_backend` L1286-1343, the candidate line L1316), models.py KNOWN_BACKEND_NAMES (L63-70) and docs/concepts.md '## Stage and run' (L165) are unchanged.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py :: test_only_reviews_by_coding_agents_count :: counts_as_cross_review('review', 'codex') is True; ('verify', 'codex'), ('review', 'a2a') and ('review', 'code') are False
- packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py :: test_the_implementer_is_the_one_before_the_review :: with implement runs claude-code at t1 and codex at t3, and a review at t2, implementer_backend(..., at=t2) == 'claude-code'
- packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py :: test_candidates_exclude_the_implementer_in_order :: reviewer_candidates('claude-code', []) == ['codex', 'gemini-cli', 'goose', 'opencode', 'copilot-cli']
- packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py :: test_the_verdict_needs_five_per_arm :: 4 reviews on one arm gives 'not enough data'
- packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py :: test_the_verdict_uses_the_rounded_rates :: Arm(reviews=3, caught=1) vs Arm(reviews=7, caught=2) and an edge case whose rounded delta is exactly 0.05 give the same verdict and detail text as the pre-refactor costs._cross_backend_verdict on the equivalent CrossBackendArms (expected strings written literally in the test)
- apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend_metric_scope.py :: test_verify_and_remote_reviews_are_not_counted :: a work item with IMPLEMENT claude-code, REVIEW backend 'a2a' and VERIFY backend 'codex': GET /api/v1/projects/{id}/metrics/cross-backend gives other_backend.reviews == 0 and same_backend.reviews == 0. Today other_backend.reviews == 2
- apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend.py and apps/orchestrator/tests/test_cross_backend.py (existing) :: green

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] The metric and the swap share choregos_core.cross_review. The metric excludes verify and non-coding reviewers and compares with the implementer at review time; verdict strings and rounding are unchanged.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row: proves the shared definitions and the corrected scope by tests. The report's numbers change for projects with verify or remote reviews (said in the docs).

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest packages/core/tests/test_cross_review.py apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend.py apps/api/tests/test_cross_backend_metric_scope.py apps/orchestrator/tests/test_cross_backend.py -q
uv run pytest -q -m 'not slow'
uv run mypy packages/*/src apps/api/src apps/orchestrator/src tools
uv run python tools/check_english.py
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not change the CrossBackendReport response shape, the verdict strings or the rounding to 4 decimals before comparing.
- Do not touch choregos_core/__init__.py (import from choregos_core.cross_review).
- Do not add a choregos_api import to the orchestrator.
- Do not change Temporal workflows.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `docs/concepts.md '## Stage and run'`

### S47-20 · fix(orchestrator): enforce the project's allowed agent backends at stage start

**Wave** 5 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S47-19`, `S47-18` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `fix(orchestrator): enforce the project's allowed agent backends at stage start`

**Goal.** A stage whose backend is not in the project's `agent.allowed_backends` is refused before any budget, key or pod is spent, exactly like a backend the platform disabled. The docs stop claiming a deployment allowlist that nothing enforces.

**Context.**

packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/project.py:55 `agent.allowed_backends` is read at runtime only to pick a cross-backend reviewer (stage.py `_reviewer_backend`; S47-19 moved its candidates into reviewer_candidates).

The stage backend `plan.backend or bundle.config.agent.default_backend` (stage.py:209) is checked only by `_require_backend_enabled` (stage.py:625-651). It raises ApplicationError type 'BackendDisabled', non_retryable, which the interpreter escalates as a configuration refusal (history wi-backend-disabled-S42-07c.json covers that path). `_resolve_on_enabled_backend(session, agent, resolver, plan, bundle, item, backend)` (L611-615) is the single call site. `_fixed_backend` (L1194-1197) exempts code/prompt/hosted.

`backend_enabled(name, None)` is True only for claude-code (packages/core/src/choregos_core/models.py:76-82). A plan on 'codex' with no `agent_backends` row is therefore ALREADY refused by `_require_backend_enabled`. The test must first enable codex, as apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_toggle_enforced.py:28-41 `_switch` does, and assert the new message.

The deployment's CHOREGOS_ALLOWED_AGENT_BACKENDS only seeds new project configs (routers/projects.py:202) and readiness, yet docs/usage.md:1232-1234 says it decides. Behaviour change: a project seeded with ['opencode'] whose workflow names a claude-code actor is now refused (release note).

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_allowlist_enforced.py`
- Modify: `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/stage.py`, `docs/usage.md`

**Steps.**

1. stage.py: add `def _require_backend_allowed(agent: Any, bundle: Any, backend: str) -> None` next to `_require_backend_enabled`. Return if `_fixed_backend(agent)`. Let `allowed = list(bundle.config.agent.allowed_backends)`. If allowed is non-empty and backend is not in it, log 'stage refused: backend not allowed' and raise `ApplicationError(f'the agent backend `{backend}` is not one this project allows ({', '.join(allowed)}): add it to the project`s agent settings, or choose another backend.', type='BackendDisabled', non_retryable=True)`.
2. stage.py `_resolve_on_enabled_backend` (L611-615): call `_require_backend_allowed(agent, bundle, backend)` BEFORE `await _require_backend_enabled(...)`.
3. docs/usage.md '### Platform' (L1232-1234): the project's allowed backends are enforced at stage start (the same refusal as a disabled backend); the deployment's list seeds new projects and readiness, and does not restrict existing ones. Write a 'Release note' line in the PR body.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- docs/usage.md moved on main (+86 lines): '### Platform' is now at L1253 (was L1219), and the sentence to rewrite ('Which backends a project may use is still set by the deployment (`CHOREGOS_ALLOWED_AGENT_BACKENDS`, chart `global.agents.defaultBackend` and `allowedBackends`) and by the project's policy.') is now at L1266-1268 (was L1232-1234). Find it by its text, not by its line number.
- The deployment seeds allowed_backends at apps/api/src/choregos_api/routers/projects.py:251-252 (`permis = list(reglages.allowed_agent_backends) or [reglages.default_agent_backend]`). The story cited :202. activities/stage.py has not changed since b3a7893, so every stage.py anchor in the story is still valid.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_allowlist_enforced.py :: test_a_backend_outside_the_project_list_is_refused_before_any_key :: after `_switch('codex', enabled=True)` (copy the helper from test_backend_toggle_enforced.py) and with allowed_backends ['claude-code'], prepare_stage for a plan on 'codex' raises an ApplicationError of type BackendDisabled whose message contains 'is not one this project allows', and no gateway key, budget reservation or run row is created. Today the stage starts
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_allowlist_enforced.py :: test_the_allowlist_is_checked_before_the_platform_switch :: codex disabled AND outside ['claude-code'] reports 'is not one this project allows'
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_allowlist_enforced.py :: test_an_empty_list_allows_every_enabled_backend :: allowed_backends [] with codex enabled keeps today's behaviour
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_backend_allowlist_enforced.py :: test_a_runtime_that_fixes_its_backend_is_not_concerned :: a `code` runtime agent passes
- tests/replay (existing) :: all histories replay

### S47-21 · feat(adapters): an e-mail (SMTP) notifier with its fake, honouring the project's notify.emails everywhere a notification is sent

**Wave** 12 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** `S46-01`, `S47-16`, `S45-09`, `S45-10`, `S45-23` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `feat(adapters): an e-mail (SMTP) notifier with its fake, honouring the project's notify.emails everywhere a notification is sent`

**Goal.** The console already lets a project set `notify.emails`, but nothing sends e-mail. This story ships a `notify: email` connector type with a scriptable SMTP fake and a setup procedure. Every orchestrator notification site, the release train's included, addresses an e-mail notifier to the project's addresses. A guard keeps a new site from reading the Slack channel directly.

**Context.**

Notify adapters today:
- packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/base.py:290-292 `Notifier.send(channel, message)`; `Message(title, body, url, severity, actions, context)` in packages/core/src/choregos_core/domain.py:435-441.
- Only Slack ships: spec in __init__.py:704-709, registered at :1244-1251. The fake is fakes/notify.py. No smtplib anywhere.
- `NotifyConfig.emails` exists (packages/contracts/src/choregos_contracts/project.py:77-79) and the console sets it (apps/web/src/components/project-settings/notifications-section.tsx:60-80); readiness counts it (services/readiness.py:512).

Every orchestrator site reads `bundle.config.notify.slack_channel or '#choregos'`:
- activities/provisioning.py:275
- findings.py:166 and :280
- bilan.py:44
- memory.py:335
- tracker.py:254, and tracker.py:354 (`payload.get('channel') or ...`)
- train.py:33 (`_courtoisie`) and train.py:596
They all go through activities/base.py:269-283 `send_courtesy` or train.py `_envoyer`, which never raise.

Procedures:
- Every shipped type needs a procedure (tests/procedures/test_coverage.py reads `choregos_adapters._SPECS_LIVREES`).
- Procedures live in packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/ (see connector.notify.slack.md). tools/procedures/export_docs.py exports them to docs/procedures, including the index docs/procedures/README.md, and with --mocks to apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json.

Connector icons: adding a registry type turns tests/web/test_connector_icon_fixture_is_current.py red until apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json is regenerated. `email` needs no brand: the icon falls back to the notify kind's lucide icon (apps/web/src/components/connector-icon.tsx:71-95).

Other facts:
- SMTP does not go through the httpx egress guard: the spec declares its egress, and the operator's network policy must open it.
- Python 3.12 has no `smtpd`.
- The adapters registry `__init__.py` is a hot spot shared with S46-01 (dependency). tracker.py is also edited by S47-16. If S45 renamed `_SPECS_LIVREES`/`_register_builtins` first, use the names in the file (aliases kept).

Verdict V24 confirmed.

**Files.**

- Create: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/notify/email.py`, `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/fakes/smtp.py`, `packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/connector.notify.email.md`, `docs/procedures/connector.notify.email.md`, `packages/adapters/tests/test_email_notifier.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_notification_target.py`
- Modify: `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/base.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/provisioning.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/findings.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/bilan.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/memory.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/tracker.py`, `apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/activities/train.py`, `apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json`, `apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json`, `docs/procedures/README.md`, `docs/integrations.md`

**Steps.**

1. notify/email.py: `class EmailNotifier` with `channel_kind = 'email'` and `__init__(*, host: str, port: int = 587, username: str = '', password: str = '', from_address: str, to: list[str] | None = None, starttls: bool = True, use_ssl: bool = False, timeout_s: float = 15.0)`. `async def send(self, channel: str, message: Message) -> None`:
   - Recipients are the comma-separated entries of `channel` that contain '@', else self.to. With none, raise UpstreamError('email', 'no recipient: set notify.emails on the project or `to` on the connector').
   - Build an `email.message.EmailMessage` with Subject '[Choregos] ' + title, plus From and To. The text body holds the body, the context lines and the url.
   - Send with `await asyncio.to_thread(self._deliver, msg, recipients)`: `smtplib.SMTP_SSL` when use_ssl, otherwise `smtplib.SMTP` followed by `starttls()` when starttls; `login` when a username is set.
2. fakes/smtp.py: `class FakeSmtpServer` (asyncio.start_server on 127.0.0.1:0) speaking EHLO/HELO, MAIL FROM, RCPT TO, DATA (until a lone '.'), RSET, NOOP and QUIT. It records `messages: list[tuple[str, list[str], bytes]]` and has async `start()` (returns the port) and `stop()`.
3. __init__.py: add `('notify', 'email'): ConnectorTypeSpec('E-mail (SMTP)', ('notify',), _objet({...host, port default 587, username, from_address, to (array of strings), starttls default true, use_ssl default false}), ('password',), egress=lambda cfg: [(str(cfg['host']), int(cfg.get('port', 587)))])` to `_SPECS_LIVREES`. In `_register_builtins`, next to Slack, add `register('notify', 'email', _SPECS_LIVREES[('notify', 'email')])(lambda cfg: EmailNotifier(...))`.
4. activities/base.py: add `def notification_target(bundle: Any) -> str`, returning `','.join(bundle.config.notify.emails)` when `getattr(bundle.adapters.notify, 'channel_kind', '') == 'email'` and emails are set, else `bundle.config.notify.slack_channel or '#choregos'`. Use it at provisioning.py:275, findings.py:166 and :280, bilan.py:44, memory.py:335, tracker.py:254, train.py:33 (`_courtoisie`) and train.py:596. At tracker.py:354 use `payload.get('channel') or notification_target(bundle)`. Rewrite in English any French comment line you touch.
5. apps/orchestrator/tests/test_notification_target.py also holds a guard: no file under apps/orchestrator/src other than activities/base.py contains the text 'slack_channel or'.
6. Write packages/core/src/choregos_core/procedures/connector.notify.email.md, following connector.notify.slack.md: SMTP host, port, TLS, a sending address, the password as a write-only secret, and opening the network policy. Then run `uv run python tools/procedures/export_docs.py` (it also rewrites docs/procedures/README.md) and `uv run python tools/procedures/export_docs.py --mocks apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json`.
7. Regenerate apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json from `choregos_adapters.connector_types()` (sorted kind/type entries, same JSON layout as the file). No icon change is needed (notify falls back to its kind's icon).
8. docs/integrations.md: a short section on e-mail notifications (recipients come from the project's e-mail addresses, otherwise the connector's `to`).

**Integrator amendments (they override the text above).**

- Re-anchor before starting: S47-23 moved `activities/bilan.py` to `activities/outcome.py`; S47-09 renamed `notify/` and `fakes/` symbols; S47-10 renamed the registry helpers in `packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py`. Use the rename tables in `docs/upgrade-notes/0.21/`.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- activities/base.py moved on main: `send_courtesy` is now at L276-290 (was L269-283). The plugin stream added `from choregos_api.services.plugin_tracker import pin_plugin_tracker` (L23) and wrapped each connector config of `load_project` in `pin_plugin_tracker(...)` (L111-117). Leave both as they are. Add `notification_target` after `send_courtesy`.
- packages/adapters/src/choregos_adapters/__init__.py moved: the ('notify','slack') spec is now at L731-736 (was 704-709) and is the last entry of `_SPECS_LIVREES` (L240-737); the Slack registration is now at L1288 (was 1244-1251). The plugin stream added the ('tracker','plugin') spec (L273-284, with `check_config=_check_plugin_tracker`) and its registration (L1101). Do not move or edit them; add ('notify','email') after ('notify','slack').
- apps/web/tests/fixtures/connector-types.json, apps/web/src/mocks/procedures.json and docs/procedures/README.md already carry the plugin stream's tracker/plugin entries. Regenerate them whole, from `connector_types()` and `tools/procedures/export_docs.py`, and never hand-merge. Every orchestrator `slack_channel or` site the story lists is unchanged on main, and there is no new one.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- packages/adapters/tests/test_email_notifier.py :: test_a_channel_naming_addresses_overrides_the_default :: send('a@x.dev,b@y.dev', ...) delivers to both and not to po@acme.dev
- packages/adapters/tests/test_email_notifier.py :: test_without_a_recipient_the_send_raises :: to=None with channel '#choregos' raises UpstreamError
- packages/adapters/tests/test_email_notifier.py :: test_the_email_type_is_registered_with_a_secret_password :: the registry spec ('notify', 'email') exists with secret_fields ('password',), and its egress is the configured host

### S47-22 · ci: run the orchestrator suite on PostgreSQL

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `ci: run the orchestrator suite on PostgreSQL`

**Goal.** The orchestrator's PostgreSQL mode runs in CI. The orchestrator writes directly through the API's models, and its advisory-lock budget path (services/budgets.py:63-67) has never run on PostgreSQL in CI.

**Context.**

apps/orchestrator/tests/conftest.py:30-31 reads CHOREGOS_TEST_ORCH_ON_POSTGRES and CHOREGOS_TEST_DATABASE_URL. Its PostgreSQL mode drops and recreates the public schema (:34-41) and runs `create_all()` (:65). Nothing sets the flag (ci.yml, nightly.yml and the Makefile all omit it).

.github/workflows/ci.yml job `python` (L196-236):
- a pgvector/pgvector:0.8.1-pg16 service and env CHOREGOS_TEST_DATABASE_URL;
- steps lint, typing, tests and coverage (SQLite), then 'API suite on PostgreSQL' (L233-236, CHOREGOS_TEST_SUITE_SUR_POSTGRES=1).
The API PG fixture recreates the vector extension itself (apps/api/tests/conftest.py:178), so the orchestrator step goes after it.

ci.yml is a hot spot: serialize with any S45/S46 ci.yml change.

Verdict V18 confirmed.

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/ci/test_orchestrator_on_postgres.py`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Steps.**

1. ci.yml job `python`: after the 'API suite on PostgreSQL' step, add a step named 'orchestrator suite on PostgreSQL' with `run: uv run pytest apps/orchestrator/tests -q` and `env: { CHOREGOS_TEST_ORCH_ON_POSTGRES: "1" }`. Add an English comment: the component that writes through the API's models, and the advisory lock of budgets.

For product defects, open one `finding` issue each (origin S47-22), and narrow the step with `-k 'not <test>'`, naming each excluded test with its issue number in a YAML comment. Never mark them xfail or skip in code.
3. Locally, when a PostgreSQL is available, run the step's command with CHOREGOS_TEST_DATABASE_URL set to reproduce.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- In apps/api/tests/conftest.py, the API PostgreSQL fixture's `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector` is now at L180 (was :178): main added a `CHOREGOS_PLUGIN_SERVERS_REGISTRIES` default at L20-21. The ci.yml `python` job is unchanged ('API suite on PostgreSQL' is still L233-236). Main's only ci.yml change is a Trivy `env` in the images job (L394-396), so there is no conflict.

### S47-23 · test(replay): record histories for ProjectProvisioning, FindingsTriage, MemoryIngestion and AgentTrial, and require one per workflow

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** M · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `test(replay): record histories for ProjectProvisioning, FindingsTriage, MemoryIngestion and AgentTrial, and require one per workflow`

**Goal.** Each of the 13 workflow classes has at least one archived history that replays, and a guard refuses a workflow that has none. That guard is what the rule 'no PR on workflows/ without a replayed history' needs.

**Context.**

apps/orchestrator/src/choregos_orchestrator/workflows/__init__.py:27-41 lists 13 classes in ALL_WORKFLOWS.

tests/replay/histories/ holds 68 files. None covers:
- ProjectProvisioning (workflows/provisioning.py:33)
- FindingsTriage (findings.py:28)
- MemoryIngestion (memory.py:36)
- AgentTrial (trial.py:28)

The pattern to copy is apps/orchestrator/tests/test_hosted_build_workflow.py:
- `play()` (L19-57) uses WorkflowEnvironment.start_time_skipping and `choregos_orchestrator.testing.workers_repartis(client, activites=[stubs], workflows=[W])`, which puts each stub on the queue workflows/planification.py routes its activity name to.
- `test_the_archived_history_replays` (L98-107).
- A `__main__` block (L110-117), run with `python -m` from the repository root.
apps/orchestrator/tests has no __init__.py; with `--import-mode=importlib`, `python -m apps.orchestrator.tests.<module>` works from the root as a namespace package.

Stubs must use the registered activity names: read `@activity.defn` in the activities modules (some English functions keep French alias names).

tests/replay/test_replay.py:19-33 replays every file. Verdict V19 confirmed.

**Files.**

- Create: `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_provisioning_history.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_findings_triage_history.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_memory_ingestion_history.py`, `apps/orchestrator/tests/test_agent_trial_history.py`, `tests/replay/histories/provisioning-S47-23.json`, `tests/replay/histories/findings-triage-S47-23.json`, `tests/replay/histories/memory-ingestion-S47-23.json`, `tests/replay/histories/agent-trial-S47-23.json`
- Modify: `tests/replay/test_replay.py`

**Steps.**

1. For each of the four workflows, create apps/orchestrator/tests/test_<name>_history.py following test_hosted_build_workflow.py. Read the workflow file and stub every activity it schedules with `@activity.defn(name=<registered name>)`, returning payloads that drive one representative path:
   - provisioning: two steps succeed and the finish step returns {}.
   - findings: one 'created' and one 'duplicate' outcome without agent_ready (no child workflow), then the `stop` signal.
   - memory ingestion: one ingest, then stop through its signal.
   - agent trial: prepare, start, await succeeded, collect spend.
   Pass ALL stubs to `workers_repartis` (it places each on its queue).
   Each file has a `play()` returning (result, history), an assertion test on the result, `test_the_archived_history_replays` (it replays the fresh history and the archived file with Replayer(workflows=ALL_WORKFLOWS)), and a `__main__` block that writes the history file.
2. Record the four files from the repository root with `uv run python -m apps.orchestrator.tests.test_provisioning_history`, `... test_findings_triage_history`, `... test_memory_ingestion_history` and `... test_agent_trial_history`. Commit them under tests/replay/histories/ with the names listed.
3. tests/replay/test_replay.py: add `test_every_workflow_has_a_history`. It computes the recorded types from `json.loads(p.read_text())['events'][0]['workflowExecutionStartedEventAttributes']['workflowType']['name']` for each history file, and the expected names from `getattr(w, '__temporal_workflow_definition').name` for each w in ALL_WORKFLOWS. It asserts that no expected name is missing, and a self-test feeds the same logic a synthetic list of types and asserts the missing names are reported.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/replay/test_replay.py :: test_every_workflow_has_a_history :: fails today with {AgentTrial, FindingsTriage, MemoryIngestion, ProjectProvisioning}
- apps/orchestrator/tests/test_provisioning_history.py :: test_the_archived_history_replays :: the archived provisioning history replays against ALL_WORKFLOWS

### S47-24 · ci(nightly): live tests fail when a required service's secrets vanish, and a crashing conformance report surfaces

**Wave** 1 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** — · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `ci(nightly): live tests fail when a required service's secrets vanish, and a crashing conformance report surfaces`

**Goal.** A nightly run no longer stays green when the secrets of a service it is meant to test have vanished: a required service's missing variable fails the test, while an optional companion variable still skips it. A crashing conformance report now reaches the alert job. Failing backends already fail the job through the `all ACP backends` pytest step.

**Context.**

.github/workflows/nightly.yml:
- L81-83: 'conformance report' has `continue-on-error: true`. L79-80 'all ACP backends' runs `pytest tests/conformance -m conformance`, which already fails on a failing backend. tests/conformance/report.py:68-84 always returns 0.
- L112-131: job `live` passes LITELLM, GITLAB and JIRA variables only.
- L150-152: job `alerte` has `needs: [..., live]` and `if: failure()`.

tests/live/conftest.py:18-24 `require()` skips when a variable is missing.

test_realtime_live.py:37 and :57 call `require('CHOREGOS_LIVE_LITELLM_URL', ..., 'CHOREGOS_LIVE_REALTIME_MODEL')`. So 'fail when a required service is named' must look only at the MISSING variables, or an optional REALTIME_MODEL would turn a litellm-required run red.

Other live variables the job never passes:
- CHOREGOS_LIVE_AZURE_* (test_aca_live.py:55)
- CHOREGOS_LIVE_ECPHORIA_* (test_ecphoria_live.py:34)
- CHOREGOS_LIVE_ROVO_ACCESS_TOKEN (test_rovo_a2a_live.py:26-27, a module-level skipif `needs_token` at L27, used at L36)
- CHOREGOS_LIVE_REALTIME_MODEL

tests/live is a package (it has __init__.py) and is in norecursedirs; a unit test loads tests/live/conftest.py by path (importlib.util.spec_from_file_location). Verdict V20 confirmed, plus the omitted env.

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/ci/test_nightly_job.py`, `tests/ci/test_live_require.py`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/nightly.yml`, `tests/live/conftest.py`, `tests/live/test_rovo_a2a_live.py`

**Steps.**

1. tests/live/conftest.py:
   - Add `def required_services() -> set[str]`, reading CHOREGOS_LIVE_REQUIRED as a comma-separated, lowercased list (the value `all` is allowed).
   - Add `def service_of(name: str) -> str`: the third underscore-separated token of a CHOREGOS_LIVE_* name (CHOREGOS_LIVE_GITLAB_TOKEN -> 'gitlab').
   - In `require()`, with `missing` as today and `wanted = required_services()`: if missing and (`'all' in wanted` or `{service_of(n) for n in missing} & wanted`), call `pytest.fail(f'live test required for {...} but variables not set: ...')`; otherwise keep the skip.
   - Update the module docstring in English.
2. tests/live/test_rovo_a2a_live.py: replace the module-level `needs_token` skipif with a `require('CHOREGOS_LIVE_ROVO_ACCESS_TOKEN')` call inside the tests that use the token, importing `require` from .conftest as the other live tests do.
3. nightly.yml job `live`:
   - Add `if: github.repository == 'VargaFoundation/choregos'` (forks have no secrets).
   - Add env `CHOREGOS_LIVE_REQUIRED: litellm,gitlab,jira`.
   - Add env entries from secrets for CHOREGOS_LIVE_AZURE_{TOKEN,SUBSCRIPTION,RG,ENV_ID}, CHOREGOS_LIVE_ECPHORIA_{URL,TOKEN,TENANT}, CHOREGOS_LIVE_ROVO_ACCESS_TOKEN and CHOREGOS_LIVE_REALTIME_MODEL, without adding them to REQUIRED.
   - Fix the job comment.
4. nightly.yml job `conformance`: remove `continue-on-error: true` from 'conformance report', so a crashing report fails the job and reaches `alerte`. Leave report.py unchanged: failing backends already fail the pytest step.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/ci/test_nightly_job.py :: test_no_nightly_step_hides_a_failure :: no step of any job in nightly.yml has a truthy continue-on-error; fails today on 'conformance report'
- tests/ci/test_nightly_job.py :: test_the_live_job_requires_the_services_it_has_secrets_for :: jobs.live.env CHOREGOS_LIVE_REQUIRED is non-empty, and for each listed service at least one CHOREGOS_LIVE_<SERVICE>_* variable is in the job env; fails today
- tests/ci/test_live_require.py :: test_a_required_service_fails_instead_of_skipping :: with CHOREGOS_LIVE_REQUIRED=gitlab and the GitLab variables unset, require('CHOREGOS_LIVE_GITLAB_TOKEN') raises pytest.fail.Exception; with REQUIRED unset it raises the skip exception. Fails today (always skips)
- tests/ci/test_live_require.py :: test_an_optional_companion_still_skips :: with REQUIRED=litellm, the LITELLM variables set and CHOREGOS_LIVE_REALTIME_MODEL unset, require('CHOREGOS_LIVE_LITELLM_URL', 'CHOREGOS_LIVE_REALTIME_MODEL') raises the skip exception, not the fail one

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] A nightly live run with a missing secret for a required service is red and triggers `alerte`. An optional companion variable still skips. A crashing conformance report is red.
- [ ] make ci green. STATUS row: proves the job configuration by tests; does not prove that the repository holds the listed secrets (owner action).

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest tests/ci/test_nightly_job.py tests/ci/test_live_require.py -q
uv run pytest tests/live -q -m live -rs  # locally: skips without secrets
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not compute the required-service rule over all the requested names: only the missing ones.
- Do not add services to CHOREGOS_LIVE_REQUIRED whose secrets the repository may not hold.
- Do not make tests/live part of the default pytest run.
- Do not edit ci.yml here.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

### S47-25 · ci: one measured Python coverage threshold over every package, enforced by make ci, and a web coverage floor

**Wave** 3 · **Priority** P1 · **Size** S · **Depends on** `S47-07` · **Contract change** no

**PR title:** `ci: one measured Python coverage threshold over every package, enforced by make ci, and a web coverage floor`

**Goal.** The coverage scope covers every Python package. The threshold, its comment and AGENTS.md state the same measured number. `make ci` enforces what the CI enforces. The web suite gets a measured coverage floor. AGENTS.md states the hand-off rule for ratchets and allowlists, so parallel tracks update them in the PR that moves them.

**Context.**

pyproject.toml:
- `[tool.coverage.run].source` (L171) omits packages/cli, ontology, playbooks, quant, tools-mcp and contracts. packages/contracts/src is hand-written pydantic; only packages/contracts/ts is generated.
- The French comment (L175-178) says 72 % was measured on 2026-09-24, while L179 sets `fail_under = 80`.

The CI python job runs `pytest --cov` (ci.yml:224-225). `make ci` (Makefile:28) runs `test` (L48-49, no --cov); the help text of `test-cov` (Makefile:52) is stale.

AGENTS.md '## Commands' says make ci = lint + typecheck + test + contracts-check + charts-lint (procedures-check is missing) and 'Coverage: make test-cov, threshold 80 %'. tests/docs/test_instructions_des_agents.py requires the sections '## Context', '## Languages', '## Commands', '## Conventions' and '## Definition of done', and that every cited `make <target>` exists.

Web: apps/web/vitest.config.ts has no `coverage` block; apps/web/package.json:13 has `"test": "vitest run"`; vitest ^5.0.5. The web CI job runs `pnpm test` (ci.yml:270).

Makefile and AGENTS.md are hot spots: serialize with S45/S46 edits of them. Verdict V22 partly.

**Files.**

- Create: `tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py`
- Modify: `pyproject.toml`, `Makefile`, `AGENTS.md`, `apps/web/vitest.config.ts`, `apps/web/package.json`, `apps/web/pnpm-lock.yaml`

**Steps.**

1. pyproject.toml: add 'packages/cli/src', 'packages/contracts/src', 'packages/ontology/src', 'packages/playbooks/src', 'packages/quant/src' and 'packages/tools-mcp/src' to `source`. Run `uv run pytest --cov --cov-report=term -q`, read the TOTAL, and set `fail_under` to the measured floor (rounded down to an integer) if it is below 80, else keep 80. Rewrite the comment above it in English: the scope, the date, the measured value, and that the threshold only rises.
2. Makefile: `ci:` uses `test-cov` instead of `test`. Fix the `test-cov` help text in English: coverage over the packages listed in pyproject's coverage source, threshold in fail_under.
3. AGENTS.md '## Commands': `make ci` = lint + typecheck + test-cov + contracts-check + procedures-check + charts-lint. The coverage line states the SAME number as `fail_under`.
4. AGENTS.md '## Conventions': add one bullet. A PR from any track that adds or removes a crossing import, a router-private import, an organisation lookup or an API path updates, in the same PR, the CEILING or the allowlist of the guard that moved (tests/architecture/, apps/api/tests/test_layering_*.py, apps/api/tests/test_one_organisation_lookup.py, tests/web/test_api_routes_have_a_client.py), and says so in its body (ADR 0099). A ceiling is lowered on removal and raised only as ADR 0099 allows.
5. apps/web: run `pnpm -C apps/web add -D @vitest/coverage-v8@5.0.5` (exact version, the same as vitest), so package.json and pnpm-lock.yaml change. After one measuring run, add to vitest.config.ts `test.coverage = { provider: 'v8', include: ['src/**/*.{ts,tsx}'], exclude: ['src/mocks/**'], thresholds: { lines: <measured floor>, branches: <measured floor> } }`. Set the package.json script `test` to 'vitest run --coverage'.

**Recalibrated on main 3156d12 (overrides the text above).**

- Step 4 and its AGENTS.md bullet cite 'ADR 0095' for the ratchet rule. On main, ADR 0095 is now SkillSpector (docs/adr/0095-skillspector-behind-the-skill-gate-offline.md). The ratchet ADR that S47-07 writes ('the API is a dependency that only shrinks') is renumbered 0099. Cite the number S47-07 actually used (check docs/adr/ on main, expected 0099) in both sentences of the bullet, never 0095.

**Tests (each fails without the change).**

- tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py :: test_every_python_package_is_measured :: every packages/*/src containing .py files is in tool.coverage.run.source (tomllib); fails today for cli, contracts, ontology, playbooks, quant, tools-mcp
- tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py :: test_the_threshold_comment_and_agents_md_state_the_threshold :: the comment block above fail_under and the AGENTS.md '## Commands' section both contain the fail_under value followed by ' %' or '%'; fails today (72 vs 80 in the comment)
- tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py :: test_make_ci_enforces_coverage :: the Makefile `ci:` prerequisites include test-cov
- tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py :: test_the_web_suite_has_a_coverage_floor :: vitest.config.ts contains 'thresholds' and package.json scripts.test contains '--coverage'; fails today
- tests/docs/test_instructions_des_agents.py (existing) :: green after the AGENTS.md change

**Acceptance.**

- [ ] One measured, documented Python threshold over every package, enforced by make ci and CI and stated identically in AGENTS.md. A web floor enforced by pnpm test. The ratchet hand-off rule is in AGENTS.md.
- [ ] make ci and make web-ci green. STATUS row: proves the configuration by tests and states the measured numbers; does not raise coverage.

**Commands.**

```bash
uv run pytest --cov --cov-report=term -q
uv run pytest tests/ci/test_coverage_config.py tests/docs/test_instructions_des_agents.py -q
pnpm -C apps/web install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm -C apps/web test
make web-ci
make ci
```

**Do not.**

- Do not set a threshold above the measured value.
- Do not use a caret range for @vitest/coverage-v8, and add no other dependency.
- Do not include packages/contracts/ts or apps/web/src/mocks in coverage.
- Do not edit .github/workflows/ci.yml.
- Do not edit docs/plan/STATUS.md or 08-backlog.yaml.

**Docs.** `AGENTS.md '## Commands' and '## Conventions'`
